




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau


pañ‍cakanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. saikhabalavaggau

1. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, saikhabalāni saikkhabalāni (ka॰)| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, hirībalaṃ hiribalaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ viriyabalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca saikhabalāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘saddhābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, hirībalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, auttappabalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, vīriyabalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, pañ‍ñābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalainā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vitthatasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, saikhabalāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, hirībalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, hirībalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau hirimā hauti, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, hirībalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, auttappabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau auttappī hauti, auttappati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, auttappabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā  pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca saikhabalāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘saddhābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, hirībalaina… auttappabalaina … vīriyabalaina… pañ‍ñābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalainā’ti| aivañhi khau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dukkhasuttaṃ

3. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  duggati pāṭikaṅkhā| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, hirīmā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. yathābhatasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, hirīmā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sikkhāsuttaṃ

5. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati, tassa diṭṭhaiva diṭṭhai caiva (sī॰) dhammai pañ‍ca sahadhammikā vādānupātā vādānuvādā (a॰ ni॰ 8.12; a॰ ni॰ 3.58) gārayhā ṭhānā āgacchanti| katamai pañ‍ca? saddhāpi nāma tai nāhausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirīpi nāma tai nāhausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappampi  nāma tai nāhausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyampi nāma tai nāhausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñāpi nāma tai nāhausi kusalaisu dhammaisu| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai imai pañ‍ca sahadhammikā vādānupātā gārayhā ṭhānā āgacchanti|

‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina assumukhau assumukhaupi (syā॰) rudamānau paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai pañ‍ca sahadhammikā pāsaṃsā ṭhānā pāsaṃsaṃ ṭhānaṃ (syā॰) āgacchanti| katamai  pañ‍ca? saddhāpi nāma tai ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirīpi nāma tai ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappampi nāma tai ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyampi nāma tai ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñāpi nāma tai ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina assumukhau rudamānau paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, tassa diṭṭhaiva  dhammai imai pañ‍ca sahadhammikā pāsaṃsā ṭhānā āgacchantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samāpattisuttaṃ

6. ‘‘na  tāva, bhikkhavai, akusalassa samāpatti hauti yāva saddhā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, saddhā antarahitā hauti, asaddhiyaṃ pariyuṭṭhāya tiṭṭhati; atha akusalassa samāpatti hauti|

‘‘na tāva, bhikkhavai, akusalassa samāpatti hauti yāva hirī pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, hirī antarahitā hauti, ahirikaṃ pariyuṭṭhāya tiṭṭhati; atha akusalassa samāpatti hauti|

‘‘na tāva, bhikkhavai, akusalassa samāpatti hauti yāva auttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, auttappaṃ antarahitaṃ hauti, anauttappaṃ pariyuṭṭhāya tiṭṭhati; atha akusalassa samāpatti hauti|

‘‘na  tāva, bhikkhavai, akusalassa samāpatti hauti yāva vīriyaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ antarahitaṃ hauti, kausaj‍jaṃ  pariyuṭṭhāya tiṭṭhati; atha akusalassa samāpatti hauti|

‘‘na tāva, bhikkhavai, akusalassa samāpatti hauti yāva pañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñā antarahitā hauti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaṃ (ka॰) pariyuṭṭhāya tiṭṭhati; atha akusalassa samāpatti hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kāmasuttaṃ

7. ‘‘yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, sattā kāmaisu laḷitā palāḷitā (sī॰)| asitabyābhaṅgiṃ asitabyābhaṅgi caipi (?), bhikkhavai, kulaputtau auhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, ‘saddhāpabbajitau kulaputtau’ti alaṃ vacanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? labbhā labbhā hi (syā॰), bhikkhavai, yaubbanaina kāmā tai ca khau yādisā vā tādisā vā| yai ca, bhikkhavai, hīnā kāmā yai ca majjhimā kāmā yai ca paṇītā kāmā, sabbai kāmā ‘kāmā’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau dhātiyā pamādamanvāya kaṭṭhaṃ vā kaṭhalaṃ kathalaṃ (ka॰) vā mukhai āharaiyya| tamainaṃ dhāti sīghaṃ sīghaṃ sīghasīghaṃ (sī॰) manasi karaiyya; sīghaṃ sīghaṃ manasi karitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ āharaiyya| nau cai sak‍kuṇaiyya sīghaṃ sīghaṃ āharituṃ, vāmaina hatthaina sīsaṃ pariggahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina vaṅkaṅguliṃ karitvā salauhitampi āharaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘atthaisā, bhikkhavai, kumārassa vihaisā; naisā natthī’ti vadāmi| karaṇīyañ‍ca khau aitaṃ aivaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, dhātiyā atthakāmāya hitaisiniyā anukampikāya, anukampaṃ upādāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sau kumārau vuddhau  hauti alaṃpañ‍ñau, anapaikkhā dāni anapaikkhā pana (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, dhāti tasmiṃ kumārai hauti – ‘attaguttau dāni kumārau nālaṃ pamādāyā’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca bhikkhunau saddhāya akataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, hiriyā akataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappaina akataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyaina akataṃ hauti  kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñāya akataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, anurakkhitabbau tāva mai sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau saddhāya kataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, hiriyā kataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappaina kataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyaina kataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, anapaikkhau dānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai panāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), tasmiṃ bhikkhusmiṃ haumi – ‘attaguttau dāni bhikkhu nālaṃ pamādāyā’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. cavanasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? asaddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| ahirikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| anauttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| kusītau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| duppañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| hirīmā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| auttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| āraddhavīriyau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamaagāravasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| ahirikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| anauttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| kusītau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| duppañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu agāravau appatissau cavati, nappatiṭṭhāti saddhammai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| hirimā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| auttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau  na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| āraddhavīriyau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau na cavati, patiṭṭhāti saddhammai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyaagāravasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| ahirikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| anauttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| kusītau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| duppañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu agāravau appatissau abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai  vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| hirīmā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… auttappī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… āraddhavīriyau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jitu’’nti| dasamaṃ|

saikhabalavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃkhittaṃ vitthataṃ dukkhā, bhataṃ sikkhāya pañ‍camaṃ|

samāpatti ca kāmaisu, cavanā dvai agāravāti||


2. balavaggau

1. ananussutasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘pubbāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ananussutaisu dhammaisu abhiñ‍ñāvausānapāramippattau paṭijānāmi| pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, hirībalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaitī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kūṭasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, saikhabalāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, hirībalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca saikhabalāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ saikhabalānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ kūṭaṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ saikhabalānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘saddhābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalaina, hirībalaina… auttappabalaina… vīriyabalaina… pañ‍ñābalaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma saikhabalainā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vitthatasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satibalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satibalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ  paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti  – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samādhibalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. daṭṭhabbasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu| aittha saddhābalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sammappadhānaisu| aittha vīriyabalaṃ  daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, satibalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu| aittha satibalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu jhānaisu| aittha samādhibalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| aittha pañ‍ñābalaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. punakūṭasuttaṃ

16. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ kūṭaṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghātaniyaṃ, yadidaṃ pañ‍ñābala’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamahitasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā samādhisampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ samādhisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ vimuttisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya samādapaiti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyahitasuttaṃ

18. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā na sīlasampan‍nau hauti, paraṃ sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na samādhisampan‍nau hauti, paraṃ samādhisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, paraṃ pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, paraṃ vimuttisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, paraṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya samādapaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyahitasuttaṃ

19. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu naiva attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā na sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na samādhisampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ samādhisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ vimuttisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā na vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya samādapaiti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu naiva attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. catutthahitasuttaṃ

20. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca samādhisampadāya samādapaiti, attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca vimuttisampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya samādapaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya cā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

balavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ananussutakūṭañ‍ca, saṃkhittaṃ vitthataina ca|

daṭṭhabbañ‍ca puna kūṭaṃ, cattāraupi hitaina cāti||


3. pañ‍caṅgikavaggau

1. paṭhamaagāravasuttaṃ

21. ‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau asabhāgavuttikau ‘sabrahmacārīsu ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ  paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūraitvā saikhaṃ saikkhaṃ (ka॰) dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ  ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saikhaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūraitvā sīlāni paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlāni aparipūraitvā sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammādiṭṭhiṃ aparipūraitvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau sabhāgavuttikau ‘sabrahmacārīsu ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā sīlāni paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlāni paripūraitvā sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraitvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaagāravasuttaṃ

22. ‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu agāravau appatissau asabhāgavuttikau ‘sabrahmacārīsu ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūraitvā saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saikhaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūraitvā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlakkhandhaṃ aparipūraitvā samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘samādhikkhandhaṃ aparipūraitvā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sagāravau sappatissau sabhāgavuttikau ‘sabrahmacārīsu ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraitvā samādhikkhandhaṃ  paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ  vij‍jati| ‘samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraitvā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. upak‍kilaisasuttaṃ

23. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| katamai pañ‍ca? ayau, lauhaṃ, tipu, sīsaṃ, sajjhaṃ sajjhu (sī॰) – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, jātarūpaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi upak‍kilaisaihi vimuttaṃ vippamuttaṃ (sī॰) hauti, taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ mudu ca kammaniyañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca na ca pabhaṅgu sammā upaiti kammāya| yassā yassā ca yassa kassaci (syā॰ pī॰) piḷandhanavikatiyā ākaṅkhati – yadi muddikāya yadi kuṇḍalāya yadi gīvaiyyakāya gīvaiyyakaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰, a॰ ni॰ 3.102) yadi suvaṇṇamālāya – tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhauti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai cittassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca na ca sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandau, byāpādau, thinamiddhaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, vicikicchā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca cittassa upak‍kilaisā yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca na ca sammā  samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi upak‍kilaisaihi vimuttaṃ hauti, taṃ hauti cittaṃ mudu ca kammaniyañ‍ca  pabhassarañ‍ca na ca pabhaṅgu sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| yassa yassa ca abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau assaṃ; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānau gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai candimasuriyai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasaiyyaṃ parāmasaiyyaṃ (ka॰) parimaj‍jaiyyaṃ yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇaiyyaṃ – dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’ti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānaiyyaṃ – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti  pajānaiyyaṃ, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītadausaṃ vā  cittaṃ vītadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ vītamauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ vikkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ mahaggataṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ amahaggataṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ sauttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ anuttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ asamāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ saiyyathīdaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattārīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi  saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau  cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyyaṃ – imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nāti, iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dussīlasuttaṃ

24. a॰ ni॰ 5.168; 6.50; 7.65 ‘‘dussīlassa, bhikkhavai, sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi na pāripūriṃ  gacchati, phaiggupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti  nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi pāripūriṃ gacchati, phaiggupi pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati; aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. anuggahitasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi anuggahitā sammādiṭṭhi caitauvimuttiphalā ca hauti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi sīlānuggahitā ca hauti, sutānuggahitā ca hauti, sākacchānuggahitā ca hauti, samathānuggahitā ca hauti, vipassanānuggahitā ca hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi anuggahitā sammādiṭṭhi caitauvimuttiphalā ca hauti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā cā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vimuttāyatanasuttaṃ

26. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, vimuttāyatanāni yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā  vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau  vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ pāmuj‍jaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.322 jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti vaidiyati (sī॰)| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ  yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ  yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau  dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau…pai॰… yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti; api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ  dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati; api ca khvassa añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa  viharatau  avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ  vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanāni yattha bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, aparikkhīṇā vā āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattaṃ vā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samādhisuttaṃ

27. ‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha appamāṇaṃ nipakā patissatā| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvayataṃ appamāṇaṃ nipakānaṃ patissatānaṃ pañ‍ca ñāṇāni pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva uppaj‍janti| katamāni pañ‍ca? ‘ayaṃ samādhi pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākau’ti pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, ‘ayaṃ samādhi ariyau nirāmisau’ti pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, ‘ayaṃ samādhi akāpurisasaivitau’ti mahāpurisasaivitauti (ka॰) pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, ‘ayaṃ samādhi santau  paṇītau paṭippassaddhaladdhau aikaudibhāvādhigatau, na saṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau’ti na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritappatitauti (sī॰), na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritapattauti (syā॰), na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvaṭauti (ka॰), na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāriyādhigatauti (?) dī॰ ni॰ 3.355; a॰ ni॰ 3.102; 9.27 pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, ‘satau khau panāhaṃ imaṃ samāpaj‍jāmi satau vuṭṭhahāmī’ti sau khau panāhaṃ imaṃ samādhiṃ satauva samāpaj‍jāmi, satau uṭṭhahāmīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati|

‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha appamāṇaṃ nipakā patissatā| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvayataṃ appamāṇaṃ nipakānaṃ patissatānaṃ imāni pañ‍ca ñāṇāni pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva uppaj‍jantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. pañ‍caṅgikasuttaṃ

28. ‘‘ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa bhāvanaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, ariyassa pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa bhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau nhāpakau nahāpakau (sī॰ pī॰) vā nhāpakantaivāsī vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni nahānīyacuṇṇāni (sī॰ pī॰) ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ san‍naiyya| sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi sāssa nahānīyapiṇḍī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) snaihānugatā snaihaparaitā santarabāhirā phuṭā snaihaina, na ca paggharinī| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa ayaṃ paṭhamā bhāvanā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau gambhīrau ubbhidaudakau ubbhitaudakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tassa naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na uttarāya disāya  udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, daivau ca kālaina kālaṃ sammā dhāraṃ nānuppavaicchaiyya daivau ca na kālaina… anupavaicchaiyya (dī॰ ni॰ 1.227 ādayau; ma॰ ni॰ 2.251 ādayau| atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa ayaṃ dutiyā bhāvanā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ  vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antau nimuggapausīni| tāni yāva  caggā yāva ca mūlā sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni paripūrāni paripphuṭāni; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa ayaṃ tatiyā bhāvanā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā  …pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisin‍nau assa; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa audātaina vatthaina apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa ayaṃ catutthā bhāvanā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñauva añ‍ñaṃ añ‍ñau vā añ‍ñaṃ vā (sī॰), añ‍ñau vā añ‍ñaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), añ‍ñau añ‍ñaṃ (?) pac‍cavaikkhaiyya, ṭhitau vā nisin‍naṃ pac‍cavaikkhaiyya, nisin‍nau vā nipan‍naṃ pac‍cavaikkhaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ  suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caṅgikassa sammāsamādhissa ayaṃ pañ‍camā pañ‍camī (sī॰) bhāvanā| aivaṃ bhāvitai khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ bhāvitai khau bhikkhavai (sī॰) ariyai pañ‍caṅgikai sammāsamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakamaṇikau ādhārai ṭhapitau pūrau udakassa samatittikau kākapaiyyau| tamainaṃ balavā purisau yatau yatau āvaj‍jaiyya āvaṭṭaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), āgacchaiyya udaka’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ bhāvitai ariyai pañ‍caṅgikai sammāsamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, samai bhūmibhāgai paukkharaṇī caturaṃsā ālibaddhā pūrā udakassa samatittikā kākapaiyyā| tamainaṃ balavā purisau yatau yatau āliṃ muñ‍caiyya, āgacchaiyya udaka’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ bhāvitai ariyai pañ‍caṅgikai sammāsamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa…pai॰… sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, subhūmiyaṃ catumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ pī॰), catummahāpathai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ājañ‍ñarathau yuttau assa ṭhitau audhastapataudau| tamainaṃ dakkhau yauggācariyau assadammasārathi abhiruhitvā vāmaina hatthaina rasmiyau gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina pataudaṃ gahaitvā yainicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ sāraiyyapi pac‍cāsāraiyyapi| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ bhāvitai ariyai pañ‍caṅgikai sammāsamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ  – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇaiyyaṃ – dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’ti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai akaṅkhati – ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānaiyyaṃ – sarāgaṃ  vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva  sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. caṅkamasuttaṃ

29. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caṅkamai ānisaṃsā| katamai pañ‍ca? addhānakkhamau  hauti, padhānakkhamau hauti, appābādhau hauti, asitaṃ pītaṃ khāyitaṃ sāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, caṅkamādhigatau samādhi ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caṅkamai ānisaṃsā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nāgitasuttaṃ

30. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina icchānaṅgalaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā  icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| assausuṃ khau icchānaṅgalakā icchānaṅgalikā (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.42; a॰ ni॰ 8.86 brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppattau; icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi, satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti| atha khau icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya yaina icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhaṃsu uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā nāgitau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kai pana khau, nāgita, uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā, kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘aitai, bhantai, icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā bhagavantañ‍ñaiva uddissa bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī  akasiralābhī| sau taṃ sauhaṃ (ka॰), sau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘adhivāsaitu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, adhivāsaitu sugatau; adhivāsanakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| yaina yainaiva dāni bhagavā gamissati taṃnin‍nāva gamissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai yathānin‍naṃ udakāni pavattanti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhantai, yaina yainaiva dāni bhagavā gamissati, taṃnin‍nāva gamissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva  jānapadā ca| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi, bhantai, bhagavatau sīlapañ‍ñāṇa’’nti|

‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| sau taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyya| asitapītakhāyitasāyitassa  khau, nāgita, uc‍cārapassāvau – aisau tassa nissandau| piyānaṃ khau, nāgita, vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā – aisau tassa nissandau| asubhanimittānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa khau, nāgita, subhanimittai pāṭikulyatā paṭik‍kūlatā (sī॰), paṭik‍kūlyatā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṇṭhāti – aisau tassa nissandau| chasu khau, nāgita, phassāyatanaisu anic‍cānupassinau viharatau phassai pāṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti – aisau tassa nissandau| pañ‍casu khau, nāgita, upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassinau viharatau upādānai pāṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti – aisau tassa nissandau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

pañ‍caṅgikavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai agāravupak‍kilaisā, dussīlānuggahitaina ca|

vimuttisamādhipañ‍caṅgikā, caṅkamaṃ nāgitaina cāti||


4. sumanavaggau

1. sumanasuttaṃ

31. aikaṃ  samayaṃ…pai॰… anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sumanā rājakumārī pañ‍cahi rathasataihi pañ‍cahi rājakumārisataihi parivutā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau sumanā rājakumārī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhassu, bhantai, bhagavatau dvai sāvakā samasaddhā samasīlā samapañ‍ñā – aikau dāyakau, aikau adāyakau| tai kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyuṃ| daivabhūtānaṃ pana naisaṃ taisaṃ (sī॰), bhantai, siyā visaisau, siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti?

‘‘siyā, sumanai’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ daivabhūtau samānau pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina| yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ daivabhūtau samānau imaihi pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti’’|

‘‘sacai pana tai, bhantai, tatau cutā itthattaṃ āgacchanti, manussabhūtānaṃ pana naisaṃ, bhantai, siyā visaisau, siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘siyā, sumanai’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ manussabhūtau samānau pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – mānusakaina āyunā, mānusakaina vaṇṇaina, mānusakaina sukhaina, mānusakaina yasaina, mānusakaina ādhipataiyyaina| yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ manussabhūtau samānau imaihi pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti’’|

‘‘sacai  pana tai, bhantai, ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, pabbajitānaṃ pana naisaṃ, bhantai, siyā visaisau, siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘siyā, sumanai’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ pabbajitau samānau pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati appaṃ ayācitau, yācitauva bahulaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jati  appaṃ ayācitau, yācitauva bahulaṃ saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jati appaṃ ayācitau, yācitauva bahulaṃ gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jati appaṃ ayācitau| yaihi khau pana sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharati tyassa manāpainaiva bahulaṃ kāyakammaina samudācaranti appaṃ amanāpaina, manāpainaiva bahulaṃ vacīkammaina samudācaranti appaṃ amanāpaina, manāpainaiva bahulaṃ manaukammaina samudācaranti appaṃ amanāpaina, manāpaṃyaiva bahulaṃ upahāraṃ upaharanti appaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau sau, sumanai, dāyakau sau amuṃ adāyakaṃ pabbajitau samānau imaihi pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhātī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, bhantai, ubhau arahattaṃ pāpuṇanti, arahattappattānaṃ pana naisaṃ, bhantai, siyā visaisau, siyā nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘aittha khau panaisāhaṃ, sumanai, na kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ vimuttiyā vimutti’’nti vimuttanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhantai, alamaiva dānāni dātuṃ alaṃ puñ‍ñāni kātuṃ; yatra hi nāma daivabhūtassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñāni, manussabhūtassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñāni, pabbajitassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, sumanai! alañhi, sumanai, dānāni dātuṃ alaṃ puñ‍ñāni kātuṃ! daivabhūtassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñāni, manussabhūtassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñāni, pabbajitassāpi upakārāni puñ‍ñānī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna idaṃ vatvā (sī॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yathāpi candau vimalau, gacchaṃ ākāsadhātuyā|

sabbai tārāgaṇai laukai, ābhāya atiraucati||

‘‘tathaiva sīlasampan‍nau, saddhau purisapuggalau|

sabbai maccharinau laukai, cāgaina atiraucati||

‘‘yathāpi  maighau thanayaṃ, vij‍jumālī satak‍kaku|

thalaṃ nin‍nañ‍ca pūraiti, abhivassaṃ vasundharaṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ dassanasampan‍nau, sammāsambuddhasāvakau|

macchariṃ adhigaṇhāti, pañ‍caṭhānaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘āyunā  yasasā caiva āyunā ca yasaina ca (ka॰), vaṇṇaina ca sukhaina ca|

sa vai bhaugaparibyūḷhau bhaugaparibbūḷhau (sī॰), paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. cundīsuttaṃ

32. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau cundī rājakumārī pañ‍cahi rathasataihi pañ‍cahi ca kumārisataihi parivutā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau cundī rājakumārī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘amhākaṃ, bhantai, bhātā cundau nāma  rājakumārau, sau aivamāha – ‘yadaiva sau hauti itthī vā purisau vā buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau, musāvādā paṭiviratau, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃyaiva upapaj‍jati, nau duggati’nti| sāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ pucchāmi – ‘kathaṃrūpai khau, bhantai, satthari pasan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃyaiva upapaj‍jati, nau duggatiṃ? kathaṃrūpai dhammai pasan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃyaiva upapaj‍jati, nau duggatiṃ? kathaṃrūpai saṅghai pasan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃyaiva upapaj‍jati, nau duggatiṃ? kathaṃrūpaisu sīlaisu paripūrakārī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃyaiva upapaj‍jati, nau duggati’’’nti?

‘‘yāvatā, cundi, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā apādā vā dvīpādā vācatuppādā vā bahuppādā vā (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.34; itivu॰ 90 rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñināsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati  arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| yai khau, cundi, buddhai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, cundi, dhammā saṅkhatā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati| yai, cundi, ariyai aṭṭhaṅgikai maggai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā, aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, cundi, dhammā saṅkhatā vā asaṅkhatā vā, virāgau taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – madanimmadanau pipāsavinayau ālayasamugghātau vaṭṭupacchaidau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| yai khau, cundi, virāgai  dhammai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, cundi, saṅghā vā gaṇā vā, tathāgatasāvakasaṅghau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| yai khau, cundi, saṅghai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, cundi, sīlāni, ariyakantāni sīlāni taisaṃ ariyakantānitaisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni| yai khau, cundi, ariyakantaisu sīlaisu paripūrakārinau, aggai tai paripūrakārinau| aggai khau pana paripūrakārīnaṃ aggau vipākau hautī’’ti|

‘‘aggatau vai pasan‍nānaṃ, aggaṃ dhammaṃ vijānataṃ|

aggai buddhai pasan‍nānaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyai anuttarai||

‘‘aggai dhammai pasan‍nānaṃ, virāgūpasamai sukhai|

aggai saṅghai pasan‍nānaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai||

‘‘aggasmiṃ  dānaṃ dadataṃ, aggaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

aggaṃ āyu ca vaṇṇau ca, yasau kitti sukhaṃ balaṃ||

‘‘aggassa dātā maidhāvī, aggadhammasamāhitau|

daivabhūtau manussau vā, aggappattau pamaudatī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. uggahasuttaṃ

33. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaddiyai viharati jātiyā vanai| atha khau uggahau  maiṇḍakanattā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggahau maiṇḍakanattā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘adhivāsaitu  mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya attacatutthau bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau uggahau maiṇḍakanattā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina uggahassa maiṇḍakanattunau nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau uggahau maiṇḍakanattā bhagavantaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau uggahau maiṇḍakanattā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggahau maiṇḍakanattā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imā mai, bhantai, kumāriyau patikulāni gamissanti| auvadatu tāsaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā; anusāsatu tāsaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā, yaṃ tāsaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tā kumāriyau aitadavauca – ‘‘tasmātiha, kumāriyau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yassa vau yassa khau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) mātāpitarau bhattunau dassanti atthakāmā hitaisinau anukampakā anukampaṃ upādāya, tassa bhavissāma pubbuṭṭhāyiniyau pacchānipātiniyau kiṃkārapaṭissāviniyau  manāpacāriniyau piyavādiniyau’ti| aivañhi vau, kumāriyau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, kumāriyau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yai tai bhattu garunau gurunau (ka॰) bhavissanti mātāti vā pitāti vā samaṇabrāhmaṇāti vā, tai sak‍karissāma garuṃ karissāma garukarissāma (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mānaissāma pūjaissāma abbhāgatai ca āsanaudakaina paṭipūjaissāmā’ti pūjaissāmāti (sī॰)| aivañhi vau, kumāriyau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, kumāriyau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yai tai bhattu abbhantarā kammantā uṇṇāti vā kappāsāti vā, tattha dakkhā bhavissāma analasā, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatā, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātu’nti| aivañhi vau, kumāriyau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, kumāriyau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yau sau bhattu abbhantarau abbhantarai (ka॰) antaujanau dāsāti vā paissāti vā  kammakarāti vā, taisaṃ katañ‍ca katatau jānissāma akatañ‍ca akatatau jānissāma, gilānakānañ‍ca balābalaṃ jānissāma, khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyañ‍cassa pac‍caṃsaina pac‍cayaṃ taina (ka॰ sī॰), pac‍cayaṃsaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pac‍cayaṃ saināsanaṃ pac‍cattaṃsaina (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.46 saṃvibhajissāmā’ti vibhajissāmāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| aivañhi vau, kumāriyau, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, kumāriyau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ bhattā āharissati dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā, taṃ ārakkhaina taṃ ārakkhāya (sī॰) guttiyā sampādaissāma, tattha ca bhavissāma adhuttī athainī asauṇḍī avināsikāyau’ti| aivañhi vau, kumāriyau, sikkhitabbaṃ| imaihi khau, kumāriyau, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘yau naṃ bharati sabbadā, nic‍caṃ ātāpi ussukau|

sabbakāmaharaṃ pausaṃ, bhattāraṃ nātimañ‍ñati||

‘‘na  cāpi sautthi bhattāraṃ, issācāraina icchācāraina (sī॰), issāvādaina (pī॰) rausayai|

bhattu ca garunau sabbai, paṭipūjaiti paṇḍitā||

‘‘uṭṭhāhikā  uṭṭhāyikā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) analasā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ manāpā (sī॰) carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘yā aivaṃ vattatī nārī, bhattuchandavasānugā|

manāpā nāma tai daivā, yattha sā upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. sīhasaināpatisuttaṃ

34. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, bhagavā sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti?

‘‘sak‍kā, sīhā’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘dāyakau, sīha, dānapati bahunau janassa piyau hauti manāpau| yampi, sīha, dāyakau dānapati bahunau janassa piyau hauti manāpau, idampi sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sīha, dāyakaṃ dānapatiṃ santau sappurisā bhajanti| yampi, sīha, dāyakaṃ dānapatiṃ santau sappurisā bhajanti, idampi sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sīha, dāyakassa dānapatinau kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| yampi, sīha, dāyakassa dānapatinau kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, idampi sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sīha, dāyakau dānapati yaṃ yadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau  visāradauva (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 7.57 passitabbaṃ upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| yampi, sīha, dāyakau dānapati yaṃ yadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi  khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau, idampi sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sīha, dāyakau dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| yampi, sīha, dāyakau dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, idaṃ idampi sīha (ka॰) samparāyikaṃ dānaphala’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā cattāri sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni akkhātāni, nāhaṃ aittha bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi; ahaṃ paitāni jānāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati bahunau janassa piyau manāpau| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati; maṃ santau sappurisā bhajanti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati; mayhaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘sīhau saināpati dāyakau kārakau  saṅghupaṭṭhākau’ti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati yaṃ yadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamāmi – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau upasaṅkamāmi amaṅkubhūtau| yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā cattāri sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni akkhātāni, nāhaṃ aittha bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi; ahaṃ paitāni jānāmi| yañ‍ca khau maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha – ‘dāyakau, sīha, dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti, aitāhaṃ na jānāmi; aittha ca panāhaṃ  bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, sīha, aivamaitaṃ, sīha! dāyakau dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘dadaṃ piyau hauti bhajanti naṃ bahū,

kittiñ‍ca pappauti yasau ca vaḍḍhati yasassa vaḍḍhati (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yasaṃ pavaḍḍhati (ka॰)|

amaṅkubhūtau parisaṃ vigāhati,

visāradau hauti narau amaccharī||

‘‘tasmā hi dānāni dadanti paṇḍitā,

vinaiyya macchairamalaṃ sukhaisinau|

tai  dīgharattaṃ tidivai patiṭṭhitā,

daivānaṃ sahabyagatā ramanti tai sahabyataṃ gatā ramanti (sī॰), sahabyatā ramanti tai (ka॰)||

‘‘katāvakāsā katakusalā itau cutā tatau cutā (sī॰),

sayaṃpabhā anuvicaranti nandanaṃ nandanai (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

tai tattha nandanti ramanti maudarai,

samappitā kāmaguṇaihi pañ‍cahi|

‘‘katvāna vākyaṃ asitassa tādinau,

ramanti saggai ramanti sumanā (ka॰), kamanti sabbai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sugatassa sāvakā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. dānānisaṃsasuttaṃ

35. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dānai ānisaṃsā| katamai pañ‍ca? bahunau janassa piyau hauti manāpau; santau sappurisā bhajanti; kalyāṇau kittisaddau  abbhuggacchati; gihidhammā anapagatau gihidhammā anapaitau (sī॰ pī॰), gihidhammamanupagatau (ka॰) hauti; kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dānai ānisaṃsā’’ti|

‘‘dadamānau piyau hauti, sataṃ dhammaṃ anuk‍kamaṃ|

santau naṃ sadā bhajanti santau bhajanti sappurisā (sī॰), sañ‍ñatā brahmacārayau||

‘‘tai tassa dhammaṃ daisainti, sabbadukkhāpanūdanaṃ|

yaṃ sau dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, parinibbāti anāsavau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kāladānasuttaṃ

36. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, kāladānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? āgantukassa dānaṃ daiti; gamikassa dānaṃ daiti; gilānassa dānaṃ daiti; dubbhikkhai dānaṃ daiti; yāni tāni navasassāni  navaphalāni tāni paṭhamaṃ sīlavantaisu patiṭṭhāpaiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāladānānī’’ti|

‘‘kālai dadanti sappañ‍ñā, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

kālaina din‍naṃ ariyaisu, ujubhūtaisu tādisu||

‘‘vippasan‍namanā tassa, vipulā hauti dakkhiṇā|

yai tattha anumaudanti, vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaunti vā|

na taina na taisaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) dakkhiṇā ūnā, taipi puñ‍ñassa bhāginau||

‘‘tasmā dadai appaṭivānacittau, yattha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

9. bhaujanasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘bhaujanaṃ, bhikkhavai, dadamānau dāyakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ pañ‍ca ṭhānāni daiti| katamāni pañ‍ca? āyuṃ daiti, vaṇṇaṃ daiti, sukhaṃ daiti, balaṃ daiti, paṭibhānaṃ paṭibhāṇaṃ (sī॰) daiti| āyuṃ khau pana datvā āyussa  bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā; vaṇṇaṃ datvā vaṇṇassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā; sukhaṃ datvā sukhassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa  vā; balaṃ datvā balassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā; paṭibhānaṃ datvā paṭibhānassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| bhaujanaṃ, bhikkhavai, dadamānau dāyakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ imāni pañ‍ca ṭhānāni daitī’’ti|

‘‘āyudau baladau dhīrau, vaṇṇadau paṭibhānadau|

sukhassa dātā maidhāvī, sukhaṃ sau adhigacchati||

‘‘āyuṃ datvā balaṃ vaṇṇaṃ, sukhañ‍ca paṭibhānakaṃ paṭibhāṇakaṃ (sī॰), paṭibhānadau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

dīghāyu yasavā hauti, yattha yatthūpapaj‍jatī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. saddhasuttaṃ

38. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, saddhai kulaputtai ānisaṃsā| katamai pañ‍ca? yai tai, bhikkhavai, laukai santau sappurisā tai saddhañ‍ñaiva paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampanti, nau tathā assaddhaṃ; saddhañ‍ñaiva paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamanti, nau tathā assaddhaṃ; saddhañ‍ñaiva paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā paṭiggaṇhanti, nau tathā assaddhaṃ; saddhañ‍ñaiva paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintā daisainti, nau tathā assaddhaṃ; saddhau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca saddhai kulaputtai ānisaṃsā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, subhūmiyaṃ catumahāpathai mahānigraudhau samantā pakkhīnaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ hauti; aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, saddhau kulaputtau bahunau janassa paṭisaraṇaṃ hauti bhikkhūnaṃ  bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikāna’’nti|

‘‘sākhāpattaphalūpaitau sākhāpattabahupaitau (katthaci), sākhāpattapalāsūpaitau (?), khandhimāva khandhimā ca (sī॰) mahādumau|

mūlavā phalasampan‍nau, patiṭṭhā hauti pakkhinaṃ||

‘‘manauramai āyatanai, saivanti naṃ vihaṅgamā|

chāyaṃ chāyatthikā chāyatthinau (sī॰) yanti, phalatthā phalabhaujinau||

‘‘tathaiva sīlasampan‍naṃ, saddhaṃ purisapuggalaṃ|

nivātavuttiṃ atthaddhaṃ, saurataṃ sakhilaṃ muduṃ||

‘‘vītarāgā  vītadausā, vītamauhā anāsavā|

puñ‍ñakkhaittāni laukasmiṃ, saivanti tādisaṃ naraṃ||

‘‘tai tassa dhammaṃ daisainti, sabbadukkhāpanūdanaṃ|

yaṃ sau dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, parinibbāti anāsavau’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. puttasuttaṃ

39. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni sampassantā mātāpitarau puttaṃ icchanti kulai jāyamānaṃ| katamāni pañ‍ca? bhatau vā nau bharissati; kic‍caṃ vā nau karissati; kulavaṃsau ciraṃ ṭhassati; dāyaj‍jaṃ paṭipaj‍jissati; atha vā pana paitānaṃ kālaṅkatānaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ anuppadassatīti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ṭhānāni sampassantā mātāpitarau puttaṃ icchanti kulai jāyamāna’’nti|

kathā॰ 491 ‘‘pañ‍ca ṭhānāni sampassaṃ, puttaṃ icchanti paṇḍitā|

bhatau vā nau bharissati, kic‍caṃ vā nau karissati||

‘‘kulavaṃsau ciraṃ tiṭṭhai, dāyaj‍jaṃ paṭipaj‍jati|

atha  vā pana paitānaṃ, dakkhiṇaṃ anuppadassati||

‘‘ṭhānānaitāni sampassaṃ, puttaṃ icchanti paṇḍitā|

tasmā santau sappurisā, katañ‍ñū katavaidinau||

‘‘bharanti mātāpitarau, pubbai katamanussaraṃ|

karaunti  naisaṃ kic‍cāni, yathā taṃ pubbakārinaṃ||

‘‘auvādakārī bhatapausī, kulavaṃsaṃ ahāpayaṃ|

saddhau sīlaina sampan‍nau, puttau hauti pasaṃsiyau’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. mahāsālaputtasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘himavantaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājaṃ nissāya mahāsālā pañ‍cahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhanti| katamāhi pañ‍cahi? sākhāpattapalāsaina vaḍḍhanti; tacaina vaḍḍhanti; papaṭikāya vaḍḍhanti; phaiggunā vaḍḍhanti; sāraina vaḍḍhanti| himavantaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājaṃ nissāya mahāsālā imāhi pañ‍cahi  vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhanti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saddhaṃ kulaputtaṃ nissāya antaujanau pañ‍cahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhati| katamāhi  pañ‍cahi? saddhāya vaḍḍhati; sīlaina vaḍḍhati; sutaina vaḍḍhati; cāgaina vaḍḍhati; pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati| saddhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtaṃ nissāya antaujanau imāhi pañ‍cahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhatī’’ti|

‘‘yathā hi pabbatau sailau, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

taṃ rukkhā upanissāya, vaḍḍhantai tai vanappatī||

‘‘tathaiva sīlasampan‍naṃ, saddhaṃ kulaputtaṃ imaṃ kulapatiṃ idha (sī॰), kulaputtaṃ idha (syā॰)|

upanissāya vaḍḍhanti, puttadārā ca bandhavā|

amac‍cā  ñātisaṅghā ca, yai cassa anujīvinau||

‘‘tyassa sīlavatau sīlaṃ, cāgaṃ sucaritāni ca|

passamānānukubbanti, yai bhavanti vicakkhaṇā||

‘‘imaṃ dhammaṃ caritvāna, maggaṃ saggaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) sugatigāminaṃ|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

sumanavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sumanā cundī uggahau, sīhau dānānisaṃsakau|

kālabhaujanasaddhā ca, puttasālaihi tai dasāti||


5. muṇḍarājavaggau

1. ādiyasuttaṃ

41. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, gahapati, bhaugānaṃ ādiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ  pariharati; mātāpitarau sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati; puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisai sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| ayaṃ paṭhamau bhaugānaṃ ādiyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi  bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi mittāmac‍cai sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| ayaṃ dutiyau bhaugānaṃ ādiyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi yā tā haunti āpadā – aggitau vā udakatau vā rājatau vā cauratau vā appiyatau vā dāyādatau appiyatau vā dāyādatau vā (bahūsu) a॰ ni॰ 4.61; 5.148 – tathārūpāsu āpadāsu bhaugaihi pariyaudhāya vattati, sautthiṃ attānaṃ karauti| ayaṃ tatiyau bhaugānaṃ ādiyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi pañ‍cabaliṃ kattā hauti| ñātibaliṃ, atithibaliṃ, pubbapaitabaliṃ, rājabaliṃ, daivatābaliṃ – ayaṃ catutthau bhaugānaṃ ādiyau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi  dhammaladdhaihi yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā madappamādā paṭiviratā khantisaurac‍cai niviṭṭhā aikamattānaṃ damainti aikamattānaṃ samainti aikamattānaṃ parinibbāpainti, tathārūpaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| ayaṃ pañ‍camau bhaugānaṃ ādiyau| imai khau, gahapati, pañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ ādiyā|

‘‘tassa cai, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imai pañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ ādiyai ādiyatau bhaugā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yai vata bhaugānaṃ ādiyā tai cāhaṃ  ādiyāmi bhaugā ca mai parikkhayaṃ gacchantī’ti| itissa hauti avippaṭisārau| tassa cai, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imai pañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ ādiyai ādiyatau bhaugā abhivaḍḍhanti, tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yai vata bhaugānaṃ ādiyā tai cāhaṃ ādiyāmi bhaugā ca mai abhivaḍḍhantī’ti| itissa hauti itissa hauti avippaṭisārau, (sī॰ syā॰) ubhayainaiva avippaṭisārau’’ti|

‘‘bhuttā bhaugā bhatā bhac‍cā gatā tacchā (ka॰), vitiṇṇā āpadāsu mai|

uddhaggā dakkhiṇā din‍nā, athau pañ‍cabalīkatā|

upaṭṭhitā sīlavantau, sañ‍ñatā brahmacārayau||

‘‘yadatthaṃ bhaugaṃ icchaiyya, paṇḍitau gharamāvasaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, kataṃ ananutāpiyaṃ||

‘‘aitaṃ aivaṃ (ka॰) anussaraṃ mac‍cau, ariyadhammai ṭhitau narau|

idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti paic‍ca saggai ca maudatīti (sī॰ syā॰)|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sappurisasuttaṃ

42. ‘‘sappurisau, bhikkhavai, kulai jāyamānau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; mātāpitūnaṃ mātāpitun‍naṃ (sī॰ pī॰) atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; dāsakammakarapaurisassa  atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; mittāmac‍cānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāmaighau sabbasassāni sampādaintau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau kulai  jāyamānau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; mātāpitūnaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; dāsakammakarapaurisassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; mittāmac‍cānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti; samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hautī’’ti|

‘‘hitau bahun‍naṃ paṭipaj‍ja bhaugai, taṃ daivatā rakkhati dhammaguttaṃ|

bahussutaṃ sīlavatūpapan‍naṃ, dhammai ṭhitaṃ na vijahati vijahāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kitti||

‘‘dhammaṭṭhaṃ  sīlasampan‍naṃ, sac‍cavādiṃ hirīmanaṃ|

naikkhaṃ jambaunadassaiva, kau taṃ ninditumarahati|

daivāpi naṃ pasaṃsanti, brahmunāpi pasaṃsitau’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. iṭṭhasuttaṃ

43. atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimai, gahapati, dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āyu, gahapati, iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ; vaṇṇau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ; sukhaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ dul‍labhaṃ laukasmiṃ; yasau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ; saggā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ| imai  khau, gahapati, pañ‍ca dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘imaisaṃ  khau, gahapati, pañ‍can‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ na āyācanahaitu  vā patthanāhaitu vā na patthanāhaitu vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭilābhaṃ vadāmi| imaisaṃ khau, gahapati, pañ‍can‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ āyācanahaitu vā patthanāhaitu vā paṭilābhau abhavissa, kau idha kaina hāyaitha?

‘‘na khau, gahapati, arahati ariyasāvakau āyukāmau āyuṃ āyācituṃ vā abhinandituṃ vā āyussa vāpi haitu| āyukāmaina, gahapati, ariyasāvakaina āyusaṃvattanikā paṭipadā paṭipaj‍jitabbā| āyusaṃvattanikā hissa paṭipadā paṭipan‍nā āyupaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| sau lābhī hauti āyussa dibbassa vā mānusassa vā|

‘‘na khau, gahapati, arahati ariyasāvakau vaṇṇakāmau vaṇṇaṃ āyācituṃ vā abhinandituṃ vā vaṇṇassa vāpi haitu| vaṇṇakāmaina, gahapati, ariyasāvakaina vaṇṇasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā paṭipaj‍jitabbā| vaṇṇasaṃvattanikā hissa paṭipadā paṭipan‍nā vaṇṇapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| sau lābhī hauti vaṇṇassa dibbassa vā mānusassa vā|

‘‘na  khau, gahapati, arahati ariyasāvakau sukhakāmau sukhaṃ āyācituṃ vā abhinandituṃ vā sukhassa vāpi haitu| sukhakāmaina, gahapati, ariyasāvakaina sukhasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā paṭipaj‍jitabbā| sukhasaṃvattanikā hissa paṭipadā paṭipan‍nā sukhapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| sau lābhī hauti sukhassa dibbassa vā mānusassa vā|

‘‘na khau, gahapati, arahati ariyasāvakau yasakāmau yasaṃ  āyācituṃ vā abhinandituṃ vā yasassa vāpi haitu| yasakāmaina, gahapati, ariyasāvakaina yasasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā paṭipaj‍jitabbā| yasasaṃvattanikā hissa paṭipadā paṭipan‍nā yasapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| sau lābhī hauti yasassa dibbassa vā mānusassa vā|

‘‘na khau, gahapati, arahati ariyasāvakau saggakāmau saggaṃ āyācituṃ vā abhinandituṃ vā saggānaṃ vāpi haitu| saggakāmaina, gahapati, ariyasāvakaina saggasaṃvattanikā paṭipadā  paṭipaj‍jitabbā| saggasaṃvattanikā hissa paṭipadā paṭipan‍nā saggapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| sau lābhī hauti saggāna’’nti|

‘‘āyuṃ vaṇṇaṃ yasaṃ kittiṃ, saggaṃ uc‍cākulīnataṃ|

ratiyau patthayānaina patthayamānaina (ka॰), uḷārā aparāparā||

‘‘appamādaṃ pasaṃsanti, puñ‍ñakiriyāsu paṇḍitā|

‘‘appamattau  ubhau atthai, adhigaṇhāti paṇḍitau||

‘‘diṭṭhai dhammai ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai (sī॰) yau atthau, yau catthau samparāyikau|

atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. manāpadāyīsuttaṃ

44. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina uggassa gahapatinau vaisālikassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpaṃ mai, bhantai, sālapupphakaṃ sālipupphakaṃ (ka॰) khādanīyaṃ; taṃ mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpaṃ mai, bhantai, sampan‍nakaulakaṃ sūkaramaṃsaṃ sampan‍nasūkaramaṃsaṃ (sī॰), sampan‍navarasūkaramaṃsaṃ (syā॰); taṃ mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpaṃ mai, bhantai, nibbattatailakaṃ nibaddhatailakaṃ (pī॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰), nibbaṭṭatailakaṃ (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) nāliyasākaṃ; taṃ mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpau mai, bhantai, sālīnaṃ audanau vicitakāḷakau vigatakālakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) anaikasūpau anaikabyañ‍janau; taṃ mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpāni mai, bhantai, kāsikāni vatthāni; tāni mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’nti| manāpau mai, bhantai, pal‍laṅkau gaunakatthatau paṭalikatthatau kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇau kādalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇau (sī॰) sauttaracchadau ubhataulauhitakūpadhānau| api ca, bhantai, mayampaitaṃ jānāma – ‘naitaṃ bhagavatau kappatī’ti| idaṃ mai, bhantai, candanaphalakaṃ agghati adhikasatasahassaṃ; taṃ mai bhagavā  paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya| atha khau bhagavā uggaṃ gahapatiṃ vaisālikaṃ iminā anumaudanīyaina anumaudi –

‘‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpaṃ,

yau uj‍jubhūtaisu ujubhūtaisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dadāti chandasā|

acchādanaṃ sayanaman‍napānaṃ sayanamathan‍napānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

nānāppakārāni ca pac‍cayāni||

‘‘cattañ‍ca muttañ‍ca anuggahītaṃ anaggahītaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

khaittūpamai arahantai viditvā|

sau  duc‍cajaṃ sappurisau cajitvā,

manāpadāyī labhatai manāpa’’nti||

atha khau bhagavā uggaṃ gahapatiṃ vaisālikaṃ iminā anumaudanīyaina anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|
