atha khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau aparaina samayaina kālamakāsi| kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca uggau gahapati vaisālikau añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍ji| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau uggau daivaputtau  abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ  jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau uggaṃ daivaputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, ugga, yathādhippāyau’’ti? ‘‘taggha mai, bhagavā, yathādhippāyau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā uggaṃ daivaputtaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘manāpadāyī labhatai manāpaṃ,

aggassa dātā labhatai punaggaṃ|

varassa dātā varalābhi hauti,

saiṭṭhaṃ dadau saiṭṭhamupaiti ṭhānaṃ||

‘‘yau  aggadāyī varadāyī, saiṭṭhadāyī ca yau narau|

dīghāyu yasavā hauti, yattha yatthūpapaj‍jatī’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. puñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

45. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau…pai॰… yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vihāraṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau…pai॰… yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mañ‍capīṭhaṃ  paribhuñ‍jamānau…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘imaihi ca pana, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha khau asaṅkhaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakānīti vā  aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānīti vā aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānīti vā aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānīti vā; atha khau asaṅkhaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati’| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi pañ‍cahi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau  iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha khau asaṅkhaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

‘‘mahaudadhiṃ  aparimitaṃ mahāsaraṃ,

bahubhairavaṃ ratnagaṇānamālayaṃ|

naj‍jau yathā naragaṇasaṅghasaivitā maccha gaṇasaṃghasaivitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1037 passitabbaṃ,

puthū savantī upayanti sāgaraṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ  naraṃ an‍nadapānavatthadaṃ,

saiyyānisaj‍jattharaṇassa dāyakaṃ|

puñ‍ñassa dhārā upayanti paṇḍitaṃ,

naj‍jau yathā vārivahāva sāgara’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sampadāsuttaṃ

46. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā pañ‍ca? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, sutasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sampadā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dhanasuttaṃ

47. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, dhanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhādhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhādhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sīladhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīladhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sutadhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sutadhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cāgadhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cāgadhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhanānī’’ti|

a॰ ni॰ 4.52 ‘‘yassa  saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| sattamaṃ|

8. alabbhanīyaṭhānasuttaṃ

48. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, alabbhanīyāni ṭhānāni samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| katamāni pañ‍ca? ‘jarādhammaṃ mā jīrī’ti alabbhanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ samaṇaina  vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| ‘byādhidhammaṃ mā byādhīyī’ti vyādhidhammaṃ ‘‘mā vyādhīyī’’ti (sī॰ pī॰) …pai॰… ‘maraṇadhammaṃ mā mīyī’ti… ‘khayadhammaṃ mā khīyī’ti… ‘nassanadhammaṃ mā nassī’ti alabbhanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘assutavatau, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa mayhamaivaikassa (sī॰) jarādhammaṃ jīrati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jarādhammaṃ jīrati| ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau pana jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ kammantaupi nappavattaiyya (ka॰), amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau viddhau savisaina saukasal‍laina attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavatau puthuj‍janassa byādhidhammaṃ  byādhīyati…pai॰… maraṇadhammaṃ mīyati… khayadhammaṃ khīyati… nassanadhammaṃ nassati| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa nassanadhammaṃ nassati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ nassanadhammaṃ nassati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau viddhau savisaina saukasal‍laina attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti’’’|

‘‘sutavatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jarādhammaṃ jīrati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau abbuhi abbahi (sī॰) savisaṃ saukasal‍laṃ, yaina viddhau assutavā puthuj‍janau attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti| asaukau visal‍lau ariyasāvakau attānaṃyaiva parinibbāpaiti’’’|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa byādhidhammaṃ byādhīyati…pai॰… maraṇadhammaṃ mīyati… khayadhammaṃ khīyati… nassanadhammaṃ nassati| sau nassanadhammai  naṭṭhai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa nassanadhammaṃ nassati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ nassanadhammaṃ  nassati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau abbuhi savisaṃ saukasal‍laṃ, yaina viddhau assutavā puthuj‍janau attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti| asaukau visal‍lau ariyasāvakau attānaṃyaiva parinibbāpaitī’’’ti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca alabbhanīyāni ṭhānāni samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti|

jā॰ 1.5.96 jātakaipi ‘‘na saucanāya paridaivanāya,

atthaudha labbhā atthau idha labbhati (syā॰), atthau idha labbhā (pī॰) api appakaupi|

saucantamainaṃ dukhitaṃ viditvā,

pac‍catthikā attamanā bhavanti||

‘‘yatau ca khau paṇḍitau āpadāsu,

na vaidhatī atthavinicchayañ‍ñū|

pac‍catthikāssa  dukhitā bhavanti,

disvā mukhaṃ avikāraṃ purāṇaṃ||

‘‘jappaina  mantaina subhāsitaina,

anuppadānaina pavaiṇiyā vā|

yathā yathā yattha yathā yathā yattha yattha (ka॰) labhaitha atthaṃ,

tathā tathā tattha parak‍kamaiyya||

‘‘sacai  pajānaiyya alabbhanaiyyau,

mayāva mayā vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) añ‍ñaina vā aisa atthau|

asaucamānau adhivāsayaiyya,

kammaṃ daḷhaṃ kinti karaumi dānī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kausalasuttaṃ

49. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

(taina khau pana samayaina mal‍likā daivī kālaṅkatā hauti|) ( ) natthi (sī॰) atha khau añ‍ñatarau purisau yaina rājā pasainadi kausalau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa upakaṇṇakai āraucaiti – ‘‘mal‍likā daivī, daiva daiva daivī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), kālaṅkatā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai rājā pasainadi kausalau dukkhī dummanau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi|

atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ dukkhiṃ dummanaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ  appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, mahārāja, alabbhanīyāni ṭhānāni samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| katamāni pañ‍ca? ‘jarādhammaṃ mā jīrī’ti alabbhanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ…pai॰… na saucanāya paridaivanāya…pai॰… kammaṃ daḷhaṃ kinti karaumi dānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nāradasuttaṃ

50. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā nāradau pāṭaliputtai viharati kuk‍kuṭārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau bhaddā daivī kālaṅkatā  hauti piyā manāpā| sau bhaddāya daiviyā kālaṅkatāya piyāya manāpāya naiva nhāyati nahāyati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) na vilimpati na bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jati na kammantaṃ payaujaiti – rattindivaṃ rattidivaṃ (ka॰) bhaddāya daiviyā sarīrai ajjhaumucchitau| atha khau muṇḍau rājā piyakaṃ kausārakkhaṃ saukārakkhaṃ (syā॰)  āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, samma piyaka, bhaddāya daiviyā sarīraṃ āyasāya tailadauṇiyā pakkhipitvā añ‍ñissā āyasāya dauṇiyā paṭikuj‍jatha, yathā mayaṃ bhaddāya daiviyā sarīraṃ cirataraṃ passaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau piyakau kausārakkhau muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau paṭissutvā bhaddāya daiviyā sarīraṃ āyasāya tailadauṇiyā pakkhipitvā añ‍ñissā āyasāya dauṇiyā paṭikuj‍ji|

atha khau piyakassa kausārakkhassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imassa khau muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau bhaddā daivī kālaṅkatā piyā manāpā| sau bhaddāya daiviyā kālaṅkatāya piyāya manāpāya naiva nhāyati na vilimpati na bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jati na kammantaṃ payaujaiti  – rattindivaṃ bhaddāya daiviyā sarīrai ajjhaumucchitau| kaṃ kiṃ (ka॰) nu khau muṇḍau rājā samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā payirupāsaiyya, yassa dhammaṃ sutvā saukasal‍laṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti!

atha khau piyakassa kausārakkhassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā nāradau pāṭaliputtai viharati kuk‍kuṭārāmai| taṃ khau panāyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘paṇḍitau viyattau vyattau (sī॰ pī॰), byattau (syā॰ kaṃ॰, dī॰ ni॰ 2.407) maidhāvī bahussutau cittakathī kalyāṇapaṭibhānau vuddhau caiva buddhau caiva (ka॰) arahā ca’ arahā cā’ti (?)| yaṃnūna muṇḍau rājā āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ payirupāsaiyya, appaiva nāma muṇḍau rājā āyasmatau nāradassa dhammaṃ sutvā saukasal‍laṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau piyakau kausārakkhau yaina muṇḍau rājā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā muṇḍaṃ rājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, daiva, āyasmā nāradau pāṭaliputtai viharati kuk‍kuṭārāmai| taṃ khau panāyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī bahussutau cittakathī kalyāṇapaṭibhānau vuddhau caiva  arahā ca’ arahā cā’ti (?)| yadi pana daivau āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ payirupāsaiyya, appaiva nāma daivau āyasmatau nāradassa dhammaṃ sutvā saukasal‍laṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma piyaka, āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ paṭivaidaihi| kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti! ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau piyakau kausārakkhau muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā nāradau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau piyakau kausārakkhau āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘imassa, bhantai, muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau bhaddā daivī kālaṅkatā piyā manāpā| sau bhaddāya daiviyā kālaṅkatāya piyāya manāpāya naiva nhāyati na vilimpati na bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jati na kammantaṃ payaujaiti – rattindivaṃ bhaddāya daiviyā sarīrai ajjhaumucchitau| sādhu, bhantai, āyasmā nāradau muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā muṇḍau rājā āyasmatau nāradassa dhammaṃ sutvā saukasal‍laṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni, piyaka, muṇḍau rājā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

atha khau piyakau kausārakkhau uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina muṇḍau rājā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā muṇḍaṃ rājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katāvakāsau khau, daiva, āyasmatā nāradaina| yassadāni daivau kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma piyaka, bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau piyakau kausārakkhau muṇḍassa rañ‍ñau paṭissutvā bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā muṇḍaṃ rājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yuttāni khau tai, daiva, bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni| yassadāni daivau kālaṃ  mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

atha khau muṇḍau rājā bhadraṃ yānaṃ bhadraṃ bhadraṃ yānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhaddaṃ yānaṃ (pī॰) abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi yaina kuk‍kuṭārāmau taina pāyāsi mahac‍cā mahac‍ca (bahūsu) rājānubhāvaina āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ dassanāya| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānaina gantvā, yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva ārāmaṃ pāvisi| atha khau muṇḍau rājā  yaina āyasmā nāradau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau muṇḍaṃ rājānaṃ āyasmā nāradau aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, mahārāja, alabbhanīyāni ṭhānāni samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| katamāni pañ‍ca? ‘jarādhammaṃ mā jīrī’ti alabbhanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| ‘byādhidhammaṃ mā byādhīyī’ti…pai॰… ‘maraṇadhammaṃ mā mīyī’ti… ‘khayadhammaṃ mā khīyī’ti… ‘nassanadhammaṃ mā nassī’ti alabbhanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘assutavatau, mahārāja, puthuj‍janassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jarādhammaṃ jīrati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai  nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahārāja – ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau viddhau savisaina saukasal‍laina attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahārāja, assutavatau puthuj‍janassa byādhidhammaṃ byādhīyati…pai॰… maraṇadhammaṃ mīyati… khayadhammaṃ khīyati… nassanadhammaṃ nassati| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa nassanadhammaṃ nassati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ nassanadhammaṃ nassati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā  assū’ti| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahārāja – ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau viddhau savisaina saukasal‍laina attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti’’’|

‘‘sutavatau  ca khau, mahārāja, ariyasāvakassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa jarādhammaṃ jīrati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jarādhammaṃ jīrati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana jarādhammai jiṇṇai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ  paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau jarādhammai jiṇṇai na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahārāja – ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau abbuhi savisaṃ saukasal‍laṃ, yaina viddhau assutavā puthuj‍janau attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti| asaukau visal‍lau ariyasāvakau attānaṃyaiva parinibbāpaiti’’’|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, mahārāja, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa byādhidhammaṃ byādhīyati…pai॰… maraṇadhammaṃ mīyati… khayadhammaṃ khīyati… nassanadhammaṃ nassati| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa nassanadhammaṃ nassati, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ nassanadhammaṃ nassati| ahañ‍caiva khau pana nassanadhammai naṭṭhai saucaiyyaṃ kilamaiyyaṃ paridaivaiyyaṃ, urattāḷiṃ kandaiyyaṃ, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, bhattampi mai nacchādaiyya, kāyaipi dubbaṇṇiyaṃ auk‍kamaiyya, kammantāpi nappavattaiyyuṃ, amittāpi attamanā assu, mittāpi dummanā assū’ti| sau nassanadhammai naṭṭhai na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahārāja – ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau abbuhi savisaṃ saukasal‍laṃ, yaina viddhau assutavā puthuj‍janau attānaṃyaiva paritāpaiti| asaukau visal‍lau ariyasāvakau attānaṃyaiva parinibbāpaiti ’’’|

‘‘imāni  khau, mahārāja, pañ‍ca alabbhanīyāni ṭhānāni samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘na saucanāya paridaivanāya,

atthaudha labbhā api appakaupi|

saucantamainaṃ dukhitaṃ viditvā,

pac‍catthikā attamanā bhavanti||

‘‘yatau  ca khau paṇḍitau āpadāsu,

na vaidhatī atthavinicchayañ‍ñū|

pac‍catthikāssa dukhitā bhavanti,

disvā mukhaṃ avikāraṃ purāṇaṃ||

‘‘jappaina mantaina subhāsitaina,

anuppadānaina pavaiṇiyā vā|

yathā yathā yattha labhaitha atthaṃ,

tathā tathā tattha parak‍kamaiyya||

‘‘sacai  pajānaiyya alabbhanaiyyau,

mayāva añ‍ñaina vā aisa atthau|

asaucamānau adhivāsayaiyya,

kammaṃ daḷhaṃ kinti karaumi dānī’’ti jā॰ 1 jātakaipi||

aivaṃ  vuttai muṇḍau rājā āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nāmau kau nu khau (sī॰ pī॰) ayaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘saukasal‍laharaṇau nāma ayaṃ, mahārāja, dhammapariyāyau’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhantai, saukasal‍laharaṇau taggha bhantai saukasal‍laharaṇau, taggha bhantai saukasal‍laharaṇau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)! imañhi mai, bhantai, dhammapariyāyaṃ sutvā saukasal‍laṃ pahīna’’nti|

atha khau muṇḍau rājā piyakaṃ kausārakkhaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, samma piyaka, bhaddāya daiviyā sarīraṃ jhāpaitha; thūpañ‍cassā karautha| aj‍jataggai dāni mayaṃ nhāyissāma caiva vilimpissāma bhattañ‍ca bhuñ‍jissāma kammantai ca payaujaissāmā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

muṇḍarājavaggau  pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ādiyau sappurisau iṭṭhā, manāpadāyībhisandaṃ|

sampadā ca dhanaṃ ṭhānaṃ, kausalau nāradaina cāti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. nīvaraṇavaggau

1. āvaraṇasuttaṃ

51. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau caitasau ajjhāruhau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| byāpādau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau caitasau ajjhāruhau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| thinamiddhaṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau ajjhāruhaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau ajjhāruhaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| vicikicchā, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca āvaraṇai nīvaraṇai caitasau ajjhāruhai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai appahāya, abalāya pañ‍ñāya dubbalāya attatthaṃ vā ñassati paratthaṃ  vā ñassati ubhayatthaṃ vā ñassati uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nadī pabbataiyyā dūraṅgamā dūragamā (sī॰) sīghasautā hārahārinī| tassā purisau  ubhatau naṅgalamukhāni vivaraiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, majjhai nadiyā sautau vikkhittau visaṭau byādiṇṇau naiva na caiva (ka॰) dūraṅgamau assa na na ca (ka॰) sīghasautau na na ca (ka॰) hārahārī hārahāriṇī (sī॰)| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sau vata bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca āvaraṇai nīvaraṇai  caitasau ajjhāruhai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai appahāya, abalāya pañ‍ñāya dubbalāya attatthaṃ  vā ñassati paratthaṃ vā ñassati ubhayatthaṃ vā ñassati uttari vā manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca āvaraṇai nīvaraṇai caitasau ajjhāruhai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai pahāya, balavatiyā pañ‍ñāya attatthaṃ vā ñassati paratthaṃ vā ñassati ubhayatthaṃ vā ñassati uttari vā manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nadī pabbataiyyā dūraṅgamā sīghasautā hārahārinī| tassā purisau ubhatau naṅgalamukhāni pidahaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, majjhai nadiyā sautau avikkhittau avisaṭau abyādiṇṇau dūraṅgamau caiva assa sīghasautau ca hārahārī ca| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sau vata bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca āvaraṇai nīvaraṇai caitasau ajjhāruhai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai pahāya, balavatiyā pañ‍ñāya attatthaṃ vā ñassati paratthaṃ vā ñassati ubhayatthaṃ vā ñassati uttari vā manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. akusalarāsisuttaṃ

52. ‘‘akusalarāsīti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai (sī॰) sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ hayaṃ (sī॰), cāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sāyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, akusalarāsi yadidaṃ pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ| akusalarāsīti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, akusalarāsi yadidaṃ pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. padhāniyaṅgasuttaṃ

53. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, padhāniyaṅgāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| appābādhau hauti  appātaṅkau; samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya; asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī; yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu; āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samayasuttaṃ

54. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, asamayā padhānāya| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jiṇṇau hauti jarāyābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau asamayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu byādhitau hauti byādhinābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau asamayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, dubbhikkhaṃ hauti dussassaṃ dul‍labhapiṇḍaṃ, na sukaraṃ uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau asamayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhayaṃ hauti aṭavisaṅkaupau, cak‍kasamārūḷhā jānapadā pariyāyanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau asamayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti| saṅghai khau pana, bhikkhavai, bhin‍nai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ak‍kausā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paribhāsā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ parikkhaipā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paric‍cajā ca haunti| tattha appasan‍nā caiva nappasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau asamayau padhānāya| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca asamayā padhānāyāti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, samayā padhānāya| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu daharau hauti  yuvā susu kāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau, samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya  padhānakkhamāya| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau samayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, subhikkhaṃ hauti susassaṃ  sulabhapiṇḍaṃ, sukaraṃ uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau samayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussā samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau samayau padhānāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghau samaggau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharati| saṅghai khau pana, bhikkhavai, samaggai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ak‍kausā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paribhāsā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ parikkhaipā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paric‍cajā haunti| tattha appasan‍nā caiva pasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca bhiyyaubhāvau bhīyyaubhāvāya (ka॰) hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau samayau padhānāya| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca samayā padhānāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. mātāputtasuttaṃ

55. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ ubhau mātāputtā vassāvāsaṃ upagamiṃsu upasaṅkamiṃsu (ka॰) – bhikkhu ca bhikkhunī ca| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa abhiṇhaṃ dassanakāmā ahaisuṃ| mātāpi puttassa abhiṇhaṃ dassanakāmā ahausi; puttaupi mātaraṃ abhiṇhaṃ dassanakāmau ahausi| taisaṃ abhiṇhaṃ dassanā saṃsaggau ahausi| saṃsaggai sati vissāsau ahausi| vissāsai sati autārau ahausi| tai autiṇṇacittā sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya  dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaiviṃsu|

atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, sāvatthiyaṃ ubhau mātāputtā  vassāvāsaṃ upagamiṃsu – bhikkhu ca bhikkhunī ca, tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa abhiṇhaṃ  dassanakāmā ahaisuṃ, mātāpi puttassa abhiṇhaṃ dassanakāmā ahausi, puttaupi mātaraṃ abhiṇhaṃ dassanakāmau ahausi| taisaṃ abhiṇhaṃ dassanā saṃsaggau ahausi, saṃsaggai sati vissāsau ahausi, vissāsai sati autārau ahausi, tai autiṇṇacittā sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaiviṃsū’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau mañ‍ñati – ‘na mātā puttai sāraj‍jati, puttau vā pana mātarī’ti? nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikarūpampi samanupassāmi aivaṃ yaṃ aivaṃ (sī॰) rajanīyaṃ aivaṃ kamanīyaṃ aivaṃ madanīyaṃ aivaṃ bandhanīyaṃ aivaṃ mucchanīyaṃ aivaṃ antarāyakaraṃ anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, itthirūpaṃ| itthirūpai, bhikkhavai, sattā rattā giddhā gathitā gadhitā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) mucchitā ajjhausan‍nā ajjhaupan‍nā (bahūsu)| tai dīgharattaṃ saucanti itthirūpavasānugā|

‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikasaddampi…pai॰… aikagandhampi… aikarasampi… aikaphauṭṭhabbampi samanupassāmi aivaṃ rajanīyaṃ aivaṃ kamanīyaṃ aivaṃ madanīyaṃ aivaṃ bandhanīyaṃ aivaṃ mucchanīyaṃ aivaṃ antarāyakaraṃ anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, itthiphauṭṭhabbaṃ| itthiphauṭṭhabbai, bhikkhavai, sattā rattā giddhā gathitā mucchitā ajjhausan‍nā| tai dīgharattaṃ saucanti itthiphauṭṭhabbavasānugā|

‘‘itthī, bhikkhavai, gacchantīpi purisassa cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati; ṭhitāpi…pai॰… nisin‍nāpi… sayānāpi… hasantīpi… bhaṇantīpi… gāyantīpi… raudantīpi… ugghātitāpi ugghānitāpi (sī॰) … matāpi purisassa cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samantapāsau mārassā’ti mātugāmaṃyaiva sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samantapāsau mārassā’’’ti|

‘‘sal‍lapai  asihatthaina, pisācaināpi sal‍lapai|

āsīvisampi āsīdai āsaddai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yaina daṭṭhau na jīvati||

‘‘natvaiva  aikau aikāya, mātugāmaina sal‍lapai|

muṭṭhassatiṃ  tā bandhanti, paikkhitaina sitaina ca mhitaina ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

‘‘athaupi dun‍nivatthaina, mañ‍junā bhaṇitaina ca|

naisau janau svāsīsadau, api ugghātitau matau||

‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā aitai, itthirūpasmiṃ dissarai|

rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā||

‘‘taisaṃ kāmaughavūḷhānaṃ, kāmai aparijānataṃ|

kālaṃ gati gatiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavābhavaṃ, saṃsārasmiṃ purakkhatā||

‘‘yai  ca kāmai pariñ‍ñāya, caranti akutaubhayā|

tai vai pāraṅgatā laukai, yai pattā āsavakkhaya’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upajjhāyasuttaṃ

56. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina sakau upajjhāyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sakaṃ upajjhāyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitarahi mai, bhantai, madhurakajātau caiva kāyau, disā ca mai na pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca maṃ nappaṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, anabhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, atthi ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ saddhivihārikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu aivamāha – ‘aitarahi mai, bhantai, madhurakajātau caiva kāyau, disā ca maṃ na pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca mai nappaṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, anabhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, atthi ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’’ti|

‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhu, hauti indriyaisu aguttadvārassa, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttassa, avipassakassa kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttassa viharatau, yaṃ madhurakajātau caiva kāyau hauti, disā cassa na  pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca taṃ nappaṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍cassa cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, anabhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carati, hauti cassa dhammaisu vicikicchā| tasmātiha tai, bhikkhu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘indriyaisu guttadvārau bhavissāmi, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau, vipassakau kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttau viharissāmī’ti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabba’’nti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatā iminā auvādaina auvaditau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau sau bhikkhu arahattaṃ pattau yaina sakau upajjhāyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sakaṃ upajjhāyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitarahi mai, bhantai, na caiva na tvaiva (sī॰) madhurakajātau kāyau, disā ca mai pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca maṃ paṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, abhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, natthi ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ saddhivihārikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu aivamāha – ‘aitarahi mai, bhantai, na caiva madhurakajātau kāyau, disā ca mai pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca maṃ paṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, abhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, natthi ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’’ti|

‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhu, hauti indriyaisu guttadvārassa, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttassa, vipassakassa kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ  anuyuttassa viharatau, yaṃ na caiva madhurakajātau kāyau hauti, disā cassa pakkhāyanti, dhammā ca  taṃ paṭibhanti, thinamiddhañ‍cassa cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, abhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carati, na cassa hauti dhammaisu vicikicchā| tasmātiha vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘indriyaisu guttadvārā bhavissāma, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā, vipassakā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. abhiṇhapac‍cavaikkhitabbaṭhānasuttaṃ

57. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbāni itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā| katamāni pañ‍ca? ‘jarādhammaumhi, jaraṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā| ‘byādhidhammaumhi, byādhiṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā| ‘maraṇadhammaumhi, maraṇaṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā  vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā| ‘sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā| ‘kammassakaumhi, kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammapaṭisaraṇau| yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi – kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā – tassa dāyādau bhavissāmī’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘jarādhammaumhi, jaraṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ yaubbanai yaubbanamadau, yaina madaina mattā kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau yau yaubbanai yaubbanamadau sau sabbasau vā pahīyati tanu vā pana hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘jarādhammaumhi, jaraṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘byādhidhammaumhi, byādhiṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ āraugyai āraugyamadau, yaina madaina mattā kāyaina  duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau yau āraugyai āraugyamadau sau sabbasau vā pahīyati tanu vā pana hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘byādhidhammaumhi, byādhiṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘maraṇadhammaumhi, maraṇaṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ jīvitai jīvitamadau, yaina madaina mattā kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ  caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau yau jīvitai jīvitamadau sau sabbasau vā pahīyati tanu vā pana hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘maraṇadhammaumhi, maraṇaṃ anatītau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ piyaisu manāpaisu yau chandarāgau yaina rāgaina rattā kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau yau piyaisu manāpaisu chandarāgau sau sabbasau vā pahīyati tanu vā pana hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘kammassakaumhi, kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammapaṭisaraṇau, yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi – kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā – tassa dāyādau bhavissāmī’ti abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau sabbasau vā duc‍caritaṃ pahīyati tanu vā pana hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘kammassakaumhi, kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammapaṭisaraṇau, yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi – kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā – tassa dāyādau bhavissāmī’ti  abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ itthiyā vā purisaina vā gahaṭṭhaina vā pabbajitaina vā|

‘‘sa khau sacai (pī॰ ka॰) sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na  khau ahañ‍ñaivaikau jarādhammau ahañ‍caivaikau jarādhammaumhi (ka॰) jaraṃ anatītau, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbai sattā jarādhammā jaraṃ anatītā’ti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni sabbasau pahīyanti anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau ahañ‍ñaivaikau byādhidhammau byādhiṃ anatītau, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbai sattā byādhidhammā byādhiṃ anatītā’ti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni sabbasau pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau ahañ‍ñaivaikau maraṇadhammau maraṇaṃ anatītau, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbai sattā maraṇadhammā maraṇaṃ anatītā’ti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni sabbasau pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau mayhaivaikassa sabbaihi piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau, atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau’ti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni sabbasau pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na  khau ahañ‍ñaivaikau kammassakau kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammappaṭisaraṇau, yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi – kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā – tassa dāyādau bhavissāmi; atha khau yāvatā sattānaṃ āgati gati cuti upapatti sabbai sattā kammassakā kammadāyādā kammayauni kammabandhu kammappaṭisaraṇā, yaṃ kammaṃ karissanti – kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā – tassa dāyādā bhavissantī’ti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni sabbasau pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhauntī’’ti|

‘‘byādhidhammā jarādhammā, athau maraṇadhamminau|

yathā dhammā tathā sattā santā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), jigucchanti puthuj‍janā||

‘‘ahañ‍cai taṃ jigucchaiyyaṃ, aivaṃ dhammaisu pāṇisu|

na maitaṃ patirūpassa, mama aivaṃ vihārinau||

‘‘sauhaṃ aivaṃ viharantau, ñatvā dhammaṃ nirūpadhiṃ|

āraugyai yaubbanasmiñ‍ca, jīvitasmiñ‍ca yai madā||

‘‘sabbai madai abhibhausmi, naikkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khaimatau naikkhammai daṭṭhu khaimataṃ (a॰ ni॰ 3.39) ubhayatthapi aṭṭhakathāya samaiti|

tassa mai ahu ussāhau, nibbānaṃ abhipassatau||

‘‘nāhaṃ bhabbau aitarahi, kāmāni paṭisaivituṃ|

anivatti anivattī (?) bhavissāmi, brahmacariyaparāyaṇau’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. licchavikumārakasuttaṃ

58. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā licchavikumārakā saj‍jāni dhanūni ādāya kuk‍kurasaṅghaparivutā mahāvanai anucaṅkamamānā anuvicaramānā addasu  bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ; disvāna saj‍jāni dhanūni nikkhipitvā kuk‍kurasaṅghaṃ aikamantaṃ uyyaujaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  tuṇhībhūtā tuṇhībhūtā pañ‍jalikā bhagavantaṃ payirupāsanti|

taina khau pana samayaina mahānāmau licchavi mahāvanai jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau addasa tai licchavikumārakai tuṇhībhūtai tuṇhībhūtai pañ‍jalikai bhagavantaṃ payirupāsantai; disvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau licchavi udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘bhavissanti vaj‍jī, bhavissanti vaj‍jī’’’ti!

‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, mahānāma, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘bhavissanti vaj‍jī, bhavissanti vaj‍jī’’’ti? ‘‘imai, bhantai, licchavikumārakā caṇḍā pharusā apānubhā apajahāti (sī॰), apāṭubhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), apajahā (pī॰), apānutā (katthaci)| yānipi tāni kulaisu pahaiṇakāni pahīnakāni (sī॰), pahīṇakāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pahīyanti, ucchūti vā badarāti vā pūvāti vā maudakāti vā saṃkulikāti vā sakkhalikāti vā (sī॰ pī॰), tāni vilumpitvā vilumpitvā khādanti; kulitthīnampi kulakumārīnampi pacchāliyaṃ khipanti| tai dānimai tuṇhībhūtā tuṇhībhūtā pañ‍jalikā bhagavantaṃ payirupāsantī’’ti|

‘‘yassa kassaci, mahānāma, kulaputtassa pañ‍ca dhammā saṃvij‍janti – yadi vā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa, yadi vā raṭṭhikassa paittanikassa muddhābhisittassa (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.135, 136 passitabbaṃ, yadi vā saināya saināpatikassa, yadi  vā gāmagāmaṇikassa, yadi vā pūgagāmaṇikassa, yai vā pana kulaisu pac‍caikādhipac‍caṃ kārainti, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, mahānāma, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi mātāpitarau sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti| tamainaṃ mātāpitarau sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā kalyāṇaina manasā anukampanti – ‘ciraṃ jīva, dīghamāyuṃ pālaihī’ti| mātāpitānukampitassa, mahānāma, kulaputtassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, mahānāma, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisai … sāmantasaṃvauhārai (sī॰ pī॰) sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti| tamainaṃ puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisā sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā kalyāṇaina manasā anukampanti – ‘ciraṃ jīva, dīghamāyuṃ pālaihī’ti| puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisānukampitassa, mahānāma, kulaputtassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi khaittakammantasāmantasabyauhārai sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti| tamainaṃ khaittakammantasāmantasabyauhārā sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā kalyāṇaina manasā anukampanti – ‘ciraṃ jīva, dīghamāyuṃ pālaihī’ti| khaittakammantasāmantasabyauhārānukampitassa, mahānāma, kulaputtassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi yāvatā balipaṭiggāhikā daivatā sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti| tamainaṃ balipaṭiggāhikā daivatā sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā kalyāṇaina manasā anukampanti – ‘ciraṃ jīva, dīghamāyuṃ pālaihī’ti| daivatānukampitassa, mahānāma, kulaputtassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, mahānāma, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi samaṇabrāhmaṇai sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti| tamainaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā kalyāṇaina manasā anukampanti – ‘ciraṃ jīva, dīghamāyuṃ pālaihī’ti| samaṇabrāhmaṇānukampitassa, mahānāma, kulaputtassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yassa kassaci, mahānāma, kulaputtassa imai pañ‍ca dhammā saṃvij‍janti – yadi vā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa, yadi vā raṭṭhikassa paittanikassa, yadi vā saināya saināpatikassa, yadi vā gāmagāmaṇikassa, yadi vā pūgagāmaṇikassa, yai vā pana kulaisu pac‍caikādhipac‍caṃ kārainti, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti|

‘‘mātāpitukic‍cakarau, puttadārahitau sadā|

antaujanassa atthāya, yai cassa anujīvinau||

‘‘ubhin‍nañ‍caiva atthāya, vadañ‍ñū hauti sīlavā|

ñātīnaṃ pubbapaitānaṃ, diṭṭhai dhammai ca jīvataṃ jīvinaṃ (sī॰), jīvitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)||

‘‘samaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ, daivatānañ‍ca paṇḍitau|

vittisañ‍jananau hauti, dhammaina gharamāvasaṃ||

‘‘sau karitvāna kalyāṇaṃ, puj‍jau hauti pasaṃsiyau|

idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamavuḍḍhapabbajitasuttaṃ

59. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau dul‍labhau vuḍḍhapabbajitau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dul‍labhau, bhikkhavai, vuḍḍhapabbajitau nipuṇau, dul‍labhau ākappasampan‍nau, dul‍labhau bahussutau, dul‍labhau dhammakathikau, dul‍labhau vinayadharau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau dul‍labhau vuḍḍhapabbajitau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyavuḍḍhapabbajitasuttaṃ

60. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau dul‍labhau vuḍḍhapabbajitau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dul‍labhau, bhikkhavai, vuḍḍhapabbajitau suvacau, dul‍labhau suggahitaggāhī, dul‍labhau padakkhiṇaggāhī, dul‍labhau dhammakathikau, dul‍labhau vinayadharau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau dul‍labhau vuḍḍhapabbajitau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

nīvaraṇavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

āvaraṇaṃ rāsi aṅgāni, samayaṃ mātuputtikā|

upajjhā ṭhānā licchavi, kumārā aparā duvaiti||


(7) 2. sañ‍ñāvaggau

1. paṭhamasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

61. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā pañ‍ca? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā anabhiratisañ‍ñā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.121-122, 303-304 passitabbaṃ – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

62. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā pañ‍ca? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā – imā  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamavaḍḍhisuttaṃ

63. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānau ariyasāvakau ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyī ca hauti varādāyī ca kāyassa| katamāhi pañ‍cahi? saddhāya vaḍḍhati, sīlaina vaḍḍhati, sutaina vaḍḍhati, cāgaina vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati – imāhi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānau ariyasāvakau ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyī ca hauti varādāyī ca kāyassā’’ti|

‘‘saddhāya  sīlaina ca yau pavaḍḍhati yaudha vaḍḍhati (sī॰),

pañ‍ñāya cāgaina sutaina cūbhayaṃ|

sau tādisau sappurisau vicakkhaṇau,

ādīyatī sāramidhaiva attanau’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyavaḍḍhisuttaṃ

64. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānā ariyasāvikā ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyinī ca hauti varādāyinī ca kāyassa| katamāhi pañ‍cahi? saddhāya vaḍḍhati, sīlaina vaḍḍhati, sutaina vaḍḍhati, cāgaina vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati – imāhi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānā ariyasāvikā ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyinī ca hauti varādāyinī ca kāyassā’’ti|

‘‘saddhāya sīlaina ca yā pavaḍḍhati yādha vaḍḍhati (sī॰),

pañ‍ñāya cāgaina sutaina cūbhayaṃ|

sā tādisī sīlavatī upāsikā,

ādīyatī sāramidhaiva attanau’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. sākacchasuttaṃ

65. a॰ ni॰ 5.164 ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsākacchau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampadāya kathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampadāya kathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampadāya kathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampadāya kathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya kathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsākacchau sabrahmacārīna’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sājīvasuttaṃ

66. a॰ ni॰ 5.164 ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsājīvau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampadāya kathāya ca kataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampadāya kathāya ca  kataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampadāya kathāya ca kataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampadāya kathāya ca kataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya kathāya ca kataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsājīvau sabrahmacārīna’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaiddhipādasuttaṃ

67. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā pañ‍ca dhammai imai pañ‍ca dhammai (ka॰) bhāvaiti, pañ‍ca dhammai imai pañ‍ca dhammai (ka॰) bahulīkarauti, tassa dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva  dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, ussauḷhiñ‍ñaiva pañ‍camiṃ ussauḷhīyaiva pañ‍camī (sī॰)| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā imai pañ‍ca dhammai bhāvaiti, imai pañ‍ca dhammai bahulīkarauti, tassa dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaiddhipādasuttaṃ

68. ‘‘pubbaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau pañ‍ca dhammai bhāvaisiṃ, pañ‍ca dhammai bahulīkāsiṃ bahulimakāsiṃ (ka॰), bahulamakāsiṃ (ka॰)| katamai pañ‍ca? chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaisiṃ, vīriyasamādhi… cittasamādhi… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaisiṃ, ussauḷhiñ‍ñaiva pañ‍camiṃ| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ ussauḷhipañ‍camānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇiṃ sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhiṃ – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇiṃ sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhiṃ…pai॰… ‘āsavānaṃ  khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇiṃ sati sati āyatanai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nibbidāsuttaṃ

69. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñī anabhiratisañ‍ñī (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.121-122, 303-304 passitabbaṃ, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī, maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

70. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī, maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sañ‍ñāvaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai ca sañ‍ñā dvai vaḍḍhī ca, sākacchaina ca sājīvaṃ|

iddhipādā ca dvai vuttā, nibbidā cāsavakkhayāti||


(8) 3. yaudhājīvavaggau

1. paṭhamacaitauvimuttiphalasuttaṃ

71. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā caitauvimuttiphalā ca haunti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca haunti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī paṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī, maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā caitauvimuttiphalā ca haunti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca haunti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caitauvimuttau ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttau ca hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau itipi, saṃkiṇṇaparikhau saṃkiṇṇaparikkhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) itipi, abbūḷhaisikau itipi, niraggaḷau itipi, ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau itipi’’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau paunaubhavikau paunaubbhavikau (syā॰ ka॰) jātisaṃsārau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā  pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṃkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyacaitauvimuttiphalasuttaṃ

72. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā caitauvimuttiphalā ca haunti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca haunti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca| katamai pañ‍ca? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā caitauvimuttiphalā ca haunti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca haunti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caitauvimuttau ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttau ca – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau itipi, saṃkiṇṇaparikhau itipi, abbūḷhaisikau itipi, niraggaḷau itipi, ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau itipi’’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau paunaubhavikau  jātisaṃsārau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikhau  hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṃkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamadhammavihārīsuttaṃ

73. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammavihārī, dhammavihārī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu dhammavihārī hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau tāya dhammapariyattiyā divasaṃ atināmaiti, riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu pariyattibahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti| sau tāya dhammapañ‍ñattiyā divasaṃ atināmaiti, riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu pañ‍ñattibahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti| sau taina sajjhāyaina divasaṃ atināmaiti, riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu sajjhāyabahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| sau taihi dhammavitak‍kaihi divasaṃ atināmaiti, riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu vitak‍kabahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau tāya dhammapariyattiyā na divasaṃ atināmaiti, nāpi riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammavihārī hauti|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhu, daisitau mayā pariyattibahulau, daisitau pañ‍ñattibahulau, daisitau sajjhāyabahulau, daisitau vitak‍kabahulau, daisitau dhammavihārī| yaṃ khau, bhikkhu yaṃ bhikkhu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhu, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhu, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyadhammavihārīsuttaṃ

74. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammavihārī  dhammavihārī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu dhammavihārī hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ; uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) cassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu pariyattibahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, uttari cassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu pañ‍ñattibahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti, uttari cassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu sajjhāyabahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati, uttari cassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu – ‘bhikkhu vitak‍kabahulau, nau dhammavihārī’’’|

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ; uttari cassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bhikkhu dhammavihārī hauti|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhu, daisitau mayā pariyattibahulau, daisitau  pañ‍ñattibahulau, daisitau sajjhāyabahulau, daisitau vitak‍kabahulau, daisitau dhammavihārī| yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhu, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha bhikkhu, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamayaudhājīvasuttaṃ

75. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau yaudhājīvau rajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati visīdati na santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivarūpaupi aivarūpau (sī॰) pu॰ pa॰ 193, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau aikac‍cau (sī॰) yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ; api ca khau dhajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati  visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ sahati dhajaggaṃ; api ca khau ussāraṇañ‍ñaiva ussādanaṃyaiva (sī॰ pī॰) sutvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na  sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ; api ca khau sampahārai hañ‍ñati āhañ‍ñati (sī॰) byāpaj‍jati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ, sahati  sampahāraṃ| sau taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca yaudhājīvā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai yaudhājīvūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsu| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ santānaituṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| kimassa rajaggasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā kumārī vā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā’ti| sau taṃ sutvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ| sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| idamassa rajaggasmiṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau rajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sahati rajaggaṃ; api ca khau dhajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ| sikkhādubbalyaṃ  āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| kimassa dhajaggasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā kumārī vā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā’ti; api ca khau sāmaṃ passati itthiṃ vā kumāriṃ vā abhirūpaṃ dassanīyaṃ pāsādikaṃ paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgataṃ| sau taṃ disvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti  brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ| sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| idamassa dhajaggasmiṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ; api ca khau dhajaggañ‍ñaiva disvā saṃsīdati  visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ; api ca khau ussāraṇañ‍ñaiva sutvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ| sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| kimassa ussāraṇāya? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ arañ‍ñagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā suñ‍ñāgāragataṃ vā mātugāmau upasaṅkamitvā ūhasati uhasati (ka॰), auhasati (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pu॰ pa॰ 196 ul‍lapati uj‍jagghati uppaṇḍaiti| sau mātugāmaina ūhasiyamānau ul‍lapiyamānau uj‍jagghiyamānau uppaṇḍiyamānau saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ| sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| idamassa ussāraṇāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ; api ca khau ussāraṇañ‍ñaiva sutvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati, na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ; api  ca khau sampahārai hañ‍ñati byāpaj‍jati| kimassa sampahārasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ arañ‍ñagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā  suñ‍ñāgāragataṃ vā mātugāmau upasaṅkamitvā abhinisīdati abhinipaj‍jati ajjhauttharati| sau mātugāmaina abhinisīdiyamānau abhinipaj‍jiyamānau ajjhautthariyamānau sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati| idamassa sampahārasmiṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ, api ca khau sampahārai hañ‍ñati byāpaj‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ, sahati sampahāraṃ, sau taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati| kimassa saṅgāmavijayasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā suñ‍ñāgāragataṃ vā mātugāmau upasaṅkamitvā abhinisīdati abhinipaj‍jati ajjhauttharati| sau mātugāmaina abhinisīdiyamānau abhinipaj‍jiyamānau ajjhautthariyamānau vinivaiṭhaitvā vinimaucaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ|

‘‘sau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī  kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi  cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti  pajānāti| idamassa saṅgāmavijayasmiṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau sahati rajaggaṃ, sahati dhajaggaṃ, sahati ussāraṇaṃ, sahati sampahāraṃ, sau taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca yaudhājīvūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsū’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyayaudhājīvasuttaṃ

76. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ  san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati| sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati| sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati  vāyamati| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti upalikhanti (ka॰), tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā ñātakānaṃ nainti| sau ñātakaihi nīyamānau appatvāva ñātakai antarāmaggai kālaṃ karauti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ  san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati| sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti, tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā ñātakānaṃ nainti| tamainaṃ ñātakā upaṭṭhahanti paricaranti| sau ñātakaihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau paricariyamānau tainaiva ābādhaina kālaṃ karauti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati| sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti, tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā ñātakānaṃ nainti| tamainaṃ ñātakā upaṭṭhahanti paricaranti| sau ñātakaihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau paricariyamānau vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati| sau taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau  tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau yaudhājīvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau yaudhājīvau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca yaudhājīvā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai yaudhājīvūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsu| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ  vā upanissāya viharati| sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa taṃ mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati, sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau  puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa taṃ mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina pariḍayhataiva kāyaina pariḍayhati caitasā| tassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ ārāmaṃ gantvā  bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaiyyaṃ – rāgapariyuṭṭhitaumhi rāgāyitaumhi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ), āvusau, rāgaparaitau, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ; sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’ti| sau ārāmaṃ gacchantau appatvāva ārāmaṃ antarāmaggai sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati, sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti, tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā ñātakānaṃ nainti| sau ñātakaihi nīyamānau appatvāva  ñātakai antarāmaggai kālaṃ karauti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa taṃ mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina pariḍayhataiva kāyaina pariḍayhati caitasā| tassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaiyyaṃ – rāgapariyuṭṭhitaumhi, āvusau, rāgaparaitau, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ  sandhāraituṃ; sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’ti| sau ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaiti – ‘rāgapariyuṭṭhitaumhi, āvusau, rāgaparaitau, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ; sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ sabrahmacārī auvadanti anusāsanti – ‘appassādā, āvusau, kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā bahūpāyāsā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pāci॰ 417; cūḷava॰ 65; ma॰ ni॰ 1.234, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā  bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha  bhiyyau| sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| abhiramatāyasmā brahmacariyai; māyasmā sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattī’’’ti|

‘‘sau sabrahmacārīhi aivaṃ auvadiyamānau aivaṃ anusāsiyamānau aivamāha – ‘kiñ‍cāpi, āvusau, appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau; atha khau naivāhaṃ sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti| sau sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati, sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti, tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā  ñātakānaṃ nainti, tamainaṃ ñātakā upaṭṭhahanti paricaranti| sau ñātakaihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau paricariyamānau tainaiva ābādhaina kālaṃ karauti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa  taṃ mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina pariḍayhataiva kāyaina pariḍayhati caitasā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaiyyaṃ – rāgapariyuṭṭhitaumhi, āvusau, rāgaparaitau, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ  sandhāraituṃ; sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’ti| sau ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaiti – ‘rāgapariyuṭṭhitaumhi, āvusau, rāgaparaitau, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ; sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti|
