‘‘tamainaṃ sabrahmacārī auvadanti anusāsanti – ‘appassādā, āvusau, kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… asisūnūpamā kāmā  vuttā bhagavatā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| abhiramatāyasmā brahmacariyai; māyasmā sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattī’’’ti|

‘‘sau  sabrahmacārīhi aivaṃ auvadiyamānau aivaṃ anusāsiyamānau aivamāha – ‘ussahissāmi, āvusau, vāyamissāmi, āvusau, abhiramissāmi, āvusau! na dānāhaṃ, āvusau, sikkhādubbalyaṃ āvikatvā sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati, sau tasmiṃ saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai upalikkhanti, tamainaṃ apanainti; apanaitvā ñātakānaṃ nainti, tamainaṃ ñātakā upaṭṭhahanti paricaranti| sau ñātakaihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau paricariyamānau vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati rakkhitainaiva kāyaina rakkhitāya vācāya rakkhitaina cittaina upaṭṭhitāya satiyā saṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau  cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā … jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina  phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya…pai॰… sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā viyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ autarati, sau taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau yaudhājīvūpamau puggalau santau saṃvij‍jamānau bhikkhūsu| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca yaudhājīvūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsū’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaanāgatabhayasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, anāgatabhayāni sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina ārañ‍ñakaina (sabbattha a॰ ni॰ 5.181; pari॰ 443) bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina  viharituṃ appattassa  pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi aikakau arañ‍ñai viharāmi| aikakaṃ khau pana maṃ aikakaṃ khau pana (syā॰ kaṃ॰) arañ‍ñai viharantaṃ ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vicchikā (syā॰) vā  maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau; handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi aikakau arañ‍ñai viharāmi| aikakau khau panāhaṃ arañ‍ñai viharantau upakkhalitvā vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā bhuttaṃ mai byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau; handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa  sacchikiriyāyā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi aikakau arañ‍ñai viharāmi| aikakau khau panāhaṃ arañ‍ñai viharantau vāḷaihi samāgacchaiyyaṃ, sīhaina vā byagghaina vā dīpinā vā acchaina vā taracchaina vā, tai maṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyuṃ, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau; handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ  appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi aikakau arañ‍ñai viharāmi| aikakau khau panāhaṃ arañ‍ñai viharantau māṇavaihi samāgacchaiyyaṃ katakammaihi vā akatakammaihi vā, tai maṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyuṃ, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau; handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa  adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi aikakau arañ‍ñai viharāmi| santi khau panārañ‍ñai vāḷā amanussā, tai maṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyuṃ, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau; handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca anāgatabhayāni sampassamānaina alamaiva ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaanāgatabhayasuttaṃ

78. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, anāgatabhayāni sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi daharau yuvā susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā| hauti khau pana sau samayau yaṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ jarā phusati| jiṇṇaina khau pana jarāya abhibhūtaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, na sukarāni arañ‍ñavanapatthāni  pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| purā maṃ sau dhammau āgacchati aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau; handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰) vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya  asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, yaināhaṃ dhammaina saman‍nāgatau jiṇṇakaupi phāsuṃ phāsu (pī॰ ka॰) viharissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi appābādhau appātaṅkau samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya| hauti khau pana sau samayau yaṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ byādhi phusati| byādhitaina khau pana byādhinā abhibhūtaina byādhābhibhūtaina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, na sukarāni arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| purā maṃ sau dhammau āgacchati aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau; handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, yaināhaṃ dhammaina saman‍nāgatau byādhitaupi phāsuṃ viharissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aitarahi khau subhikkhaṃ susassaṃ sulabhapiṇḍaṃ, sukaraṃ uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| hauti khau pana sau samayau yaṃ dubbhikkhaṃ hauti dussassaṃ dul‍labhapiṇḍaṃ, na sukaraṃ uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| dubbhikkhai khau pana manussā yaina subhikkhaṃ taina saṅkamanti tainupasaṅkamanti (ka॰)| tattha saṅgaṇikavihārau hauti ākiṇṇavihārau| saṅgaṇikavihārai khau pana sati ākiṇṇavihārai na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi  kātuṃ, na sukarāni arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| purā maṃ sau dhammau āgacchati aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau; handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, yaināhaṃ dhammaina saman‍nāgatau dubbhikkhaipi phāsu viharissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aitarahi khau manussā samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti| hauti khau pana sau samayau yaṃ bhayaṃ hauti aṭavisaṅkaupau, cak‍kasamārūḷhā jānapadā pariyāyanti| bhayai khau pana sati manussā yaina khaimaṃ taina saṅkamanti| tattha saṅgaṇikavihārau hauti ākiṇṇavihārau| saṅgaṇikavihārai  khau pana sati ākiṇṇavihārai na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, na sukarāni arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni  paṭisaivituṃ| purā maṃ sau dhammau āgacchati aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau; handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, yaināhaṃ dhammaina saman‍nāgatau bhayaipi phāsuṃ viharissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘aitarahi khau saṅghau samaggau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharati| hauti khau pana sau samayau yaṃ saṅghau bhij‍jati| saṅghai khau pana bhin‍nai na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, na sukarāni arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| purā maṃ sau dhammau āgacchati aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau; handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, yaināhaṃ dhammaina saman‍nāgatau bhin‍naipi saṅghai phāsuṃ viharissāmī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina  ātāpinā pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca anāgatabhayāni sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā appamattaina ātāpinā  pahitattaina viharituṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyaanāgatabhayasuttaṃ

79. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, anāgatabhayāni aitarahi asamuppan‍nāni āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissanti| tāni vau khau (katthaci) paṭibujjhitabbāni; paṭibujjhitvā ca taisaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca? bhavissanti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā añ‍ñai upasampādaissanti| taipi tai (sī॰) na sakkhissanti vinaituṃ adhisīlai adhicittai adhipañ‍ñāya| taipi bhavissanti abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā añ‍ñai upasampādaissanti| taipi tai (?) na sakkhissanti vinaituṃ adhisīlai adhicittai adhipañ‍ñāya| taipi bhavissanti abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammasandausā vinayasandausau; vinayasandausā dhammasandausau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā añ‍ñaisaṃ nissayaṃ dassanti| taipi na sakkhissanti vinaituṃ adhisīlai adhicittai adhipañ‍ñāya| taipi bhavissanti abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā  abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā añ‍ñaisaṃ nissayaṃ dassanti| taipi na sakkhissanti vinaituṃ adhisīlai adhicittai adhipañ‍ñāya| taipi bhavissanti abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammasandausā vinayasandausau; vinayasandausā dhammasandausau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā abhidhammakathaṃ vaidal‍lakathaṃ kathaintā kaṇhadhammaṃ auk‍kamamānā na bujjhissanti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammasandausā vinayasandausau; vinayasandausā dhammasandausau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā yai tai suttantā tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā gambhīratthā laukuttarā suñ‍ñatāppaṭisaṃyuttā, taisu bhañ‍ñamānaisu  na sussūsissanti, na sautaṃ audahissanti, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaissanti, na ca tai dhammai uggahaitabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti| yai pana tai suttantā kavitā kavikatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰, saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.229) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā kāvaiyyā cittakkharā cittabyañ‍janā bāhirakā sāvakabhāsitā, taisu bhañ‍ñamānaisu sussūsissanti, sautaṃ audahissanti, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaissanti, tai ca dhammai uggahaitabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammasandausā vinayasandausau; vinayasandausā dhammasandausau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā| tai abhāvitakāyā samānā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā thairā bhikkhū bāhulikā bāhul‍likā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) a॰ ni॰ 2.49; 3.96 bhavissanti sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhissanti appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jissati| sāpi bhavissati bāhulikā sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhissati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| iti khau, bhikkhavai, dhammasandausā vinayasandausau; vinayasandausā dhammasandausau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ  aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| ‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca anāgatabhayāni aitarahi asamuppan‍nāni āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissanti| tāni vau paṭibujjhitabbāni; paṭibujjhitvā ca taisaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. catutthaanāgatabhayasuttaṃ

80. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, anāgatabhayāni aitarahi asamuppan‍nāni āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissanti| tāni vau paṭibujjhitabbāni; paṭibujjhitvā ca taisaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca? bhavissanti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ cīvarai kalyāṇakāmā| tai cīvarai kalyāṇakāmā samānā riñ‍cissanti paṃsukūlikattaṃ, riñ‍cissanti arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni; gāmanigamarājadhānīsu ausaritvā vāsaṃ kappaissanti, cīvarahaitu ca anaikavihitaṃ anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ piṇḍapātai kalyāṇakāmā| tai piṇḍapātai kalyāṇakāmā samānā riñ‍cissanti piṇḍapātikattaṃ, riñ‍cissanti arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni; gāmanigamarājadhānīsu ausaritvā vāsaṃ kappaissanti jivhaggaina rasaggāni pariyaisamānā, piṇḍapātahaitu ca anaikavihitaṃ anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ  vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ saināsanai kalyāṇakāmā| tai saināsanai kalyāṇakāmā samānā riñ‍cissanti rukkhamūlikattaṃ ārañ‍ñakattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), riñ‍cissanti arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni; gāmanigamarājadhānīsu ausaritvā vāsaṃ kappaissanti, saināsanahaitu  ca anaikavihitaṃ anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ bhikkhunīsikkhamānāsamaṇuddaisaihi saṃsaṭṭhā viharissanti| bhikkhunīsikkhamānāsamaṇuddaisaihi saṃsaggai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sati aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘anabhiratā vā brahmacariyaṃ carissanti, añ‍ñataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jissanti, sikkhaṃ vā pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissanti’| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ ārāmikasamaṇuddaisaihi saṃsaṭṭhā viharissanti| ārāmikasamaṇuddaisaihi saṃsaggai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sati aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘anaikavihitaṃ san‍nidhikāraparibhaugaṃ  anuyuttā viharissanti, auḷārikampi nimittaṃ karissanti, pathaviyāpi haritaggaipi’| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ anāgatabhayaṃ aitarahi  asamuppan‍naṃ āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissati| taṃ vau paṭibujjhitabbaṃ; paṭibujjhitvā ca tassa pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ|

‘‘imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca anāgatabhayāni aitarahi asamuppan‍nāni āyatiṃ samuppaj‍jissanti| tāni vau paṭibujjhitabbāni; paṭibujjhitvā ca taisaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

yaudhājīvavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai caitauvimuttiphalā, dvai ca dhammavihārinau|

yaudhājīvā ca dvai vuttā, cattārau ca anāgatāti||


(9) 4. thairavaggau

1. rajanīyasuttaṃ

81. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? rajanīyai raj‍jati, dussanīyai dusanīyai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dussati, mauhanīyai muyhati, kuppanīyai kupanīyai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), kaupanīyai (pī॰) kuppati, madanīyai maj‍jati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? rajanīyai  na raj‍jati, dussanīyai na  dussati, mauhanīyai na muyhati, kuppanīyai na kuppati, madanīyai na maj‍jati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vītarāgasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? avītarāgau hauti, avītadausau hauti, avītamauhau hauti, makkhī ca, paḷāsī ca – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vītarāgau hauti, vītadausau  hauti, vītamauhau hauti, amakkhī ca, apaḷāsī ca – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kuhakasuttaṃ

83. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kuhakau ca hauti, lapakau ca, naimittikau nimittikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), nimittakau (ka॰) ca, nippaisikau ca, lābhaina ca lābhaṃ nijigīsitā nijigiṃsitā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ca kuhakau hauti, na ca lapakau, na ca naimittikau, na ca nippaisikau, na ca lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsitā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. assaddhasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti, amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau hauti, hirīmā hauti, auttappī  hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. akkhamasuttaṃ

85. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭisambhidāpattasuttaṃ

86. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? atthapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, dhammapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, niruttipaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni tattha dakkhau hauti analasau tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau alaṃ kātuṃ  alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sīlavantasuttaṃ

87. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati| ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ sātthā sabyañ‍janā (sī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. thairasuttaṃ

88. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu bahujanaahitāya  paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanaasukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? thairau hauti rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau; ñātau hauti yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ gahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ (sī॰) bahujanaparivārau; lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā  pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā appaṭividdhā; micchādiṭṭhikau hauti viparītadassanau, sau bahujanaṃ saddhammā vuṭṭhāpaitvā asaddhammai patiṭṭhāpaiti| thairau bhikkhu rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ  āpaj‍janti, ñātau thairau bhikkhu yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ bahujanaparivārau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, lābhī thairau bhikkhu cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, bahussutau thairau bhikkhu sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu bahujanaahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanaasukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? thairau hauti rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau; ñātau hauti yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ bahujanaparivārau; lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; sammādiṭṭhikau hauti aviparītadassanau, sau bahujanaṃ asaddhammā vuṭṭhāpaitvā saddhammai patiṭṭhāpaiti| thairau bhikkhu rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, ñātau thairau bhikkhu yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ bahujanaparivārau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, lābhī thairau bhikkhu cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, bahussutau thairau bhikkhu sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau itipissa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanasukhāya  bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasaikhasuttaṃ

89. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ na pac‍cavaikkhati – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasaikhasuttaṃ

90. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu bahukic‍cau hauti bahukaraṇīyau  viyattau kiṃkaraṇīyaisu; riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu appamattakaina kammaina divasaṃ atināmaiti; riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu saṃsaṭṭhau viharati gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi ananulaumikaina gihisaṃsaggaina; riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu akālaina gāmaṃ pavisati, atidivā paṭik‍kamati; riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā ābhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā  sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya na nikāmalābhī hauti na akicchalābhī na akasiralābhī kicchalābhī kasiralābhī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī); riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, nānuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu  na bahukic‍cau hauti na bahukaraṇīyau viyattau kiṃkaraṇīyaisu; na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu na appamattakaina kammaina divasaṃ atināmaiti; na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu asaṃsaṭṭhau viharati gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi ananulaumikaina gihisaṃsaggaina; na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu na atikālaina gāmaṃ pavisati, nātidivā paṭik‍kamati; na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā ābhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya  nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, anuyuñ‍jati ajjhattaṃ caitausamathaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saikhassa bhikkhunau  aparihānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

thairavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

rajanīyau  vītarāgau, kuhakāssaddhaakkhamā|

paṭisambhidā ca sīlaina, thairau saikhā parai duvaiti||


(10) 5. kakudhavaggau

1. paṭhamasampadāsuttaṃ

91. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā pañ‍ca? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, sutasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sampadā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasampadāsuttaṃ

92. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā pañ‍ca? sīlasampadā, samādhisampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā, vimuttisampadā, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sampadā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. byākaraṇasuttaṃ

93. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñābyākaraṇāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti; pāpicchau icchāpakatau añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti; ummādā cittakkhaipā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti; adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti; sammadaiva añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca añ‍ñābyākaraṇānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. phāsuvihārasuttaṃ

94. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, phāsuvihārā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca phāsuvihārā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. akuppasuttaṃ

95. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, dhammapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, niruttipaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sutadharasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ āsaivantau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appaṭṭhau hauti appakic‍cau subharau susantausau jīvitaparikkhāraisu; appāhārau hauti anaudarikattaṃ anuyuttau; appamiddhau hauti jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ āsaivantau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kathāsuttaṃ

97. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ bhāvaintau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appaṭṭhau hauti appakic‍cau subharau susantausau jīvitaparikkhāraisu; appāhārau hauti anaudarikattaṃ anuyuttau; appamiddhau  hauti jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau; yāyaṃ kathā ābhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ bhāvaintau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ārañ‍ñakasuttaṃ

98. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ bahulīkarauntau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appaṭṭhau hauti appakic‍cau subharau susantausau jīvitaparikkhāraisu; appāhārau hauti anaudarikattaṃ anuyuttau; appamiddhau hauti jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau; ārañ‍ñakau hauti pantasaināsanau; yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu ānāpānassatiṃ bahulīkarauntau nacirassaiva akuppaṃ paṭivijjhatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sīhasuttaṃ

99. ‘‘sīhau, bhikkhavai, migarājā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamati; āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhati; vijambhitvā samantā catuddisaṃ catuddisā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.33; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.78 passitabbaṃ anuvilaukaiti; samantā catuddisaṃ catuddisā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.33; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.78 passitabbaṃ anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadati; tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamati| sau hatthissa caipi pahāraṃ daiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva pahāraṃ daiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; mahiṃsassa mahisassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) caipi pahāraṃ daiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva pahāraṃ daiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; gavassa caipi pahāraṃ daiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva pahāraṃ daiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; dīpissa caipi  pahāraṃ daiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva pahāraṃ daiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; khuddakānañ‍caipi  pāṇānaṃ pahāraṃ daiti antamasau sasabiḷārānampi sasabiḷārānaṃ (ka॰), sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva pahāraṃ daiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘mā mai yauggapathau nassā’ti|

‘‘sīhauti khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, idamassa hauti sīhanādasmiṃ| bhikkhūnañ‍caipi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; bhikkhunīnañ‍caipi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; upāsakānañ‍caipi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva  tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; upāsikānañ‍caipi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ; puthuj‍janānañ‍caipi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti antamasau an‍nabhāranaisādānampi an‍nabhāranaisādānaṃ (ka॰), sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? dhammagaru, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dhammagāravau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kakudhathairasuttaṃ

100. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| cūḷava॰ 333, 341 taina khau pana samayaina kakudhau nāma kauliyaputtau kauḷīyaputtau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa upaṭṭhākau adhunākālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau| tassa aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau hauti – saiyyathāpi nāma dvai vā tīṇi vā māgadhakāni māgadhikāni (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) gāmakkhaittāni| sau taina attabhāvapaṭilābhaina naiva attānaṃ naivattānaṃ byābādhaiti (sī॰) nau paraṃ byābādhaiti|

atha khau kakudhau daivaputtau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau  khau kakudhau daivaputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘daivadattassa, bhantai, aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti| sahacittuppādā ca, bhantai, daivadattau tassā iddhiyā parihīnau’’ti| idamavauca kakudhau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kakudhau nāma, bhantai, kauliyaputtau mamaṃ upaṭṭhākau adhunākālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| tassa aivarūpau attabhāvapaṭilābhau – saiyyathāpi nāma dvai vā tīṇi vā māgadhakāni gāmakkhaittāni| sau taina attabhāvapaṭilābhaina naiva attānaṃ nau paraṃ byābādhaiti| atha khau, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘daivadattassa, bhantai, aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmīti| sahacittuppādā ca, bhantai, daivadattau tassā iddhiyā parihīnau’ti| idamavauca, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana tai, mauggal‍lāna, kakudhau daivaputtau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci kakudhau daivaputtau bhāsati sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā’’’ti? ‘‘caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau mai, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci kakudhau daivaputtau bhāsati sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā’’’ti| ‘‘rakkhassaitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, vācaṃ! (rakkhassaitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, vācaṃ) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi cūḷava॰ 333 pana sabbatthapi dissatiyaiva! idāni sau maughapurisau attanāva attānaṃ pātukarissati|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, mauggal‍lāna, satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, mauggal‍lāna, aikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ  asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ kathaṃ nu taṃ (sī॰), kathaṃ nu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), kathaṃ taṃ (katthaci) mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma – ‘samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; yaṃ tumau karissati tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhājīvau samānau ‘parisuddhājīvaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhau mai ājīvau pariyaudātau asaṃkiliṭṭhau’ti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhājīvau samānau parisuddhājīvaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhau mai ājīvau pariyaudātau asaṃkiliṭṭhau’ti| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma – ‘samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; yaṃ tumau karissati tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā ājīvatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi ājīvatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau ‘parisuddhadhammadaisanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhā mai dhammadaisanā pariyaudātā asaṃkiliṭṭhā’ti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau parisuddhadhammadaisanaumhī’ti  paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhā mai dhammadaisanā pariyaudātā asaṃkiliṭṭhā’ti| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma – ‘samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; yaṃ  tumau karissati tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā dhammadaisanatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi dhammadaisanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau ‘parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai vaiyyākaraṇaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai vaiyyākaraṇaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma – ‘samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; yaṃ tumau karissati tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma  – ‘samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; yaṃ tumau karissati tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati| imai khau, mauggal‍lāna, pañ‍ca satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, mauggal‍lāna, parisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| na ca maṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| parisuddhājīvau samānau ‘parisuddhājīvaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhau mai ājīvau pariyaudātau asaṃkiliṭṭhau’ti| na ca maṃ sāvakā  ājīvatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi ājīvatau  rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| parisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau ‘parisuddhadhammadaisanaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhā mai dhammadaisanā pariyaudātā asaṃkiliṭṭhā’ti| na ca maṃ sāvakā dhammadaisanatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi dhammadaisanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau ‘parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhaṃ mai vaiyyākaraṇaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| na ca maṃ sāvakā vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| parisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ  pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| na ca maṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kakudhavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  sampadā byākaraṇaṃ, phāsu akuppapañ‍camaṃ|

sutaṃ kathā ārañ‍ñakau, sīhau ca kakudhau dasāti||

dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



3. tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(11) 1. phāsuvihāravaggau

1. sāraj‍jasuttaṃ

101. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇā dhammā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, assaddhassa sāraj‍jaṃ hauti, saddhassa taṃ sāraj‍jaṃ na hauti| tasmāyaṃ dhammau saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇau|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussīlassa sāraj‍jaṃ hauti, sīlavatau taṃ sāraj‍jaṃ na hauti| tasmāyaṃ dhammau saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇau|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, appassutassa sāraj‍jaṃ hauti, bahussutassa taṃ sāraj‍jaṃ na hauti| tasmāyaṃ dhammau saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇau|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusītassa sāraj‍jaṃ hauti, āraddhavīriyassa taṃ sāraj‍jaṃ na hauti| tasmāyaṃ dhammau saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇau|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, duppañ‍ñassa sāraj‍jaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñavatau taṃ sāraj‍jaṃ na hauti| tasmāyaṃ dhammau saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca saikhavaisāraj‍jakaraṇā dhammā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ussaṅkitasuttaṃ

102. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ussaṅkitaparisaṅkitau hauti pāpabhikkhūti api akuppadhammaupi api akuppadhammau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaisiyāgaucarau vā hauti, vidhavāgaucarau vā hauti, thul‍lakumārikāgaucarau vā hauti, paṇḍakagaucarau vā hauti, bhikkhunīgaucarau vā hauti|

‘‘imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ussaṅkitaparisaṅkitau hauti pāpabhikkhūti api akuppadhammaupī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mahācaurasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau visamanissitau ca hauti, gahananissitau ca, balavanissitau ca, bhaugacāgī ca, aikacārī ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau visamanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau nadīviduggaṃ vā nissitau hauti pabbatavisamaṃ vā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau visamanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau gahananissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau tiṇagahanaṃ vā nissitau hauti rukkhagahanaṃ vā raudhaṃ gaidhaṃ (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.51 vā mahāvanasaṇḍaṃ vā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau gahananissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau balavanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau rājānaṃ  vā rājamahāmattānaṃ vā nissitau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci  vakkhati, imai mai rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇissantī’ti| sacai naṃ kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tyassa rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau balavanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau bhaugacāgī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau aḍḍhau hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci vakkhati, itau bhaugaina paṭisantharissāmī’ti| sacai naṃ  kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tatau bhaugaina paṭisantharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau bhaugacāgī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau aikacārī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau aikakauva gahaṇāni niggahaṇāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kattā hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘mā mai  guyhamantā bahiddhā sambhaidaṃ agamaṃsū’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau aikacārī hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau sandhimpi chindati nil‍laupampi harati aikāgārikampi karauti paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhu khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau ca hauti, gahananissitau ca, balavanissitau ca, bhaugacāgī ca, aikacārī ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, visamaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, visamaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu gahananissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu micchādiṭṭhikau  hauti antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu gahananissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu balavanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu rājānaṃ vā rājamahāmattānaṃ vā nissitau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci vakkhati, imai mai rājānau vā  rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇissantī’ti| sacai naṃ kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tyassa rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu balavanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu bhaugacāgī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci vakkhati, itau lābhaina paṭisantharissāmī’ti| sacai naṃ kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tatau lābhaina paṭisantharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu bhaugacāgī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu aikacārī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu aikakauva pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu nivāsaṃ kappaiti| sau tattha kulāni upasaṅkamantau lābhaṃ labhati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu aikacārī hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhu khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samaṇasukhumālasuttaṃ

104. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ  saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau| yaihi khau  pana sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharati, tyassa tyāssa (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.87 manāpainaiva bahulaṃ kāyakammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ vacīkammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ manaukammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpaṃyaiva upahāraṃ upaharanti, appaṃ amanāpaṃ| yāni khau pana tāni vaidayitāni pittasamuṭṭhānāni vā saimhasamuṭṭhānāni vā vātasamuṭṭhānāni vā san‍nipātikāni vā utupariṇāmajāni vā visamaparihārajāni vā aupak‍kamikāni vā kammavipākajāni vā, tānissa na bahudaiva uppaj‍janti| appābādhau hauti, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau’ti, mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā mamaiva taṃ sammā (?) vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau’ti| ahañhi, bhikkhavai, yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau| yaihi khau pana bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ viharāmi, tai maṃ manāpainaiva bahulaṃ kāyakammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ vacīkammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ manaukammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpaṃyaiva upahāraṃ upaharanti, appaṃ amanāpaṃ| yāni khau pana tāni vaidayitāni – pittasamuṭṭhānāni vā saimhasamuṭṭhānāni vā vātasamuṭṭhānāni vā san‍nipātikāni vā utupariṇāmajāni vā visamaparihārajāni vā aupak‍kamikāni vā kammavipākajāni vā  – tāni mai na bahudaiva uppaj‍janti| appābādhauhamasmi, catun‍naṃ khau panasmi catun‍naṃ khau pana (sī॰), catun‍naṃ (syā॰ pī॰) jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī nikāmalābhī haumi (sī॰ ka॰) akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi|

‘‘yañhi  taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau’ti, mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. phāsuvihārasuttaṃ

105. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, phāsuvihārā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpaihi sīlaihi sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya, tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati  sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca phāsuvihārā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ānandasuttaṃ

106. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu saṅghai bhikkhuṃsaṃghau (syā॰ pī॰) viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu  attanā attanā ca (pī॰ ka॰) sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau nau ca (ka॰) paraṃ adhisīlai sampavattā sampavattā hauti (ka॰); aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda ānandāti bhagavā āvauca (syā॰ pī॰)! yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attanā sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ adhisīlai sampavattā; attānupaikkhī ca hauti, nau parānupaikkhī; aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā  pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda! yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attanā sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ adhisīlai sampavattā; attānupaikkhī ca hauti, nau parānupaikkhī; apañ‍ñātau ca hauti, taina ca apañ‍ñātakaina nau paritassati; aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, bhikkhu  saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda! yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attanā sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ adhisīlai sampavattā; attānupaikkhī ca hauti, nau parānupaikkhī; apañ‍ñātau ca hauti, taina ca apañ‍ñātakaina nau paritassati; catun‍nañ‍ca catun‍naṃ (pī॰ ka॰) jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘siyā  pana, bhantai, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda! yatau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attanā sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ adhisīlai sampavattā; attānupaikkhī ca hauti, nau parānupaikkhī; apañ‍ñātau  ca hauti, taina ca apañ‍ñātakaina nau paritassati; catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānañ‍ca āsavānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aittāvatāpi khau, ānanda, bhikkhu saṅghai viharantau phāsuṃ viharaiyya|

‘‘imamhā cāhaṃ, ānanda, phāsuvihārā añ‍ñau phāsuvihārau uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthīti vadāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sīlasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. asaikhasuttaṃ

108. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘katamaihi, pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaikhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina  pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. cātuddisasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cātuddisau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cātuddisau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. arañ‍ñasuttaṃ

110. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; āraddhavīriyau viharati thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivitu’’nti| dasamaṃ|

phāsuvihāravaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sāraj‍jaṃ  saṅkitau caurau, sukhumālaṃ phāsu pañ‍camaṃ|

ānanda sīlāsaikhā ca, cātuddisau arañ‍ñaina cāti||


(12) 2. andhakavindavaggau

1. kulūpakasuttaṃ

111. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kulūpakau bhikkhu kulaisu appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? asanthavavissāsī asanthutavissāsī (sī॰), asandhavavissāsī (ka॰) ca hauti, anissaravikappī ca, vissaṭṭhupasaivī viyatthupasaivī (sī॰), byatthupasaivī (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vyattūpasaivī (pī॰) ca, upakaṇṇakajappī ca, atiyācanakau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kulūpakau bhikkhu kulaisu appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kulūpakau bhikkhu kulaisu piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na asanthavavissāsī ca hauti, na anissaravikappī ca, na vissaṭṭhupasaivī ca, na upakaṇṇakajappī ca, na atiyācanakau ca| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau kulūpakau bhikkhu kulaisu piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pacchāsamaṇasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pacchāsamaṇau na ādātabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? atidūrai vā gacchati ac‍cāsan‍nai vā, na pattapariyāpan‍naṃ  gaṇhati, āpattisāmantā bhaṇamānaṃ na nivāraiti, bhaṇamānassa antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaiti, duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau aiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pacchāsamaṇau na ādātabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pacchāsamaṇau ādātabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? nātidūrai gacchati na ac‍cāsan‍nai, pattapariyāpan‍naṃ gaṇhati, āpattisāmantā bhaṇamānaṃ nivāraiti, bhaṇamānassa na antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaiti, pañ‍ñavā hauti ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pacchāsamaṇau ādātabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sammāsamādhisuttaṃ

113. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau sammāsamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau sammāsamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau sammāsamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau sammāsamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitu’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. andhakavindasuttaṃ

114. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati andhakavindai| atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yai tai, ānanda, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, tai vau, ānanda, bhikkhū pañ‍casu dhammaisu samādapaitabbā samādāpaitabbā (?) nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā| katamaisu pañ‍casu? ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, sīlavā hautha, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutā viharatha ācāragaucarasampan‍nā aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvinau, samādāya sikkhatha sikkhāpadaisū’ti – iti pātimaukkhasaṃvarai samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, indriyaisu guttadvārā viharatha ārakkhasatinau nipak‍kasatinau nipakasatinau (sī॰ syā॰), naipak‍kasatinau (?), sārakkhitamānasā  satārakkhaina caitasā saman‍nāgatā’ti – iti indriyasaṃvarai samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, appabhassā hautha, bhassai pariyantakārinau’ti – iti bhassapariyantai samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikā hautha, arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivathā’ti – iti kāyavūpakāsai samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, sammādiṭṭhikā hautha sammādassanaina saman‍nāgatā’ti – iti sammādassanai samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā| yai  tai, ānanda, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, tai vau, ānanda, bhikkhū imaisu pañ‍casu dhammaisu samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. maccharinīsuttaṃ

115. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āvāsamaccharinī hauti, kulamaccharinī hauti, lābhamaccharinī hauti, vaṇṇamaccharinī hauti, dhammamaccharinī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na āvāsamaccharinī hauti, na kulamaccharinī hauti, na lābhamaccharinī hauti, na vaṇṇamaccharinī hauti, na dhammamaccharinī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vaṇṇanāsuttaṃ

116. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca  pariyaugāhaitvā  appasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. issukinīsuttaṃ

117. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, issukinī ca hauti, maccharinī ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ saddhādaiyyañ‍ca (syā॰) vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anissukinī ca hauti, amaccharinī ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ  na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. micchādiṭṭhikasuttaṃ

118. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchādiṭṭhikā ca hauti, micchāsaṅkappā ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, sammādiṭṭhikā ca, hauti, sammāsaṅkappā ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. micchāvācāsuttaṃ

119. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchāvācā ca hauti, micchākammantā ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, sammāvācā ca hauti, sammākammantā ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ  na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. micchāvāyāmasuttaṃ

120. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchāvāyāmā ca hauti, micchāsatinī ca micchāsati ca (syā॰), saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, sammāvāyāmā ca hauti, sammāsatinī ca, saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

andhakavindavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kulūpakau pacchāsamaṇau, samādhiandhakavindaṃ|

maccharī vaṇṇanā issā, diṭṭhivācāya vāyamāti||


(13) 3. gilānavaggau

1. gilānasuttaṃ

121. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina gilānasālā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ dubbalaṃ gilānakaṃ; disvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci yaṃ kiñ‍ci (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ dubbalaṃ bhikkhavai dubbalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gilānakaṃ pañ‍ca dhammā na vijahanti, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘nacirassaiva āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’’’ti|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati, sabbasaṅkhāraisu  anic‍cānupassī, maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ dubbalaṃ gilānakaṃ imai pañ‍ca dhammā na vijahanti, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘nacirassaiva āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. satisūpaṭṭhitasuttaṃ

122. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā pañ‍ca dhammai bhāvaiti pañ‍ca dhammai bahulīkarauti, tassa dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ajjhattañ‍ñaiva sati sūpaṭṭhitā hauti dhammānaṃ udayatthagāminiyā  pañ‍ñāya, asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā imai pañ‍ca dhammai bhāvaiti imai pañ‍ca dhammai bahulīkarauti, tassa dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaupaṭṭhākasuttaṃ

123. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau dūpaṭṭhākau dupaṭṭhākau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) mahāva॰ 366 hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? asappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ na jānāti, bhaisaj‍jaṃ nappaṭisaivitā hauti, atthakāmassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa  na yathābhūtaṃ ābādhaṃ āvikattā hauti abhik‍kamantaṃ vā abhik‍kamatīti paṭik‍kamantaṃ vā paṭik‍kamatīti ṭhitaṃ vā ṭhitauti, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ  vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ tippānaṃ (sī॰) mahāva॰ 366 kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikau hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau dūpaṭṭhākau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau sūpaṭṭhākau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ jānāti, bhaisaj‍jaṃ paṭisaivitā hauti, atthakāmassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa yathābhūtaṃ ābādhaṃ āvikattā hauti abhik‍kamantaṃ vā abhik‍kamatīti paṭik‍kamantaṃ vā paṭik‍kamatīti ṭhitaṃ vā ṭhitauti, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau sūpaṭṭhākau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaupaṭṭhākasuttaṃ

124. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau nālaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? nappaṭibalau hauti bhaisaj‍jaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ; sappāyāsappāyaṃ na jānāti, asappāyaṃ upanāmaiti, sappāyaṃ apanāmaiti; āmisantarau gilānaṃ upaṭṭhāti, nau maittacittau; jaigucchī hauti uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā vantaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā nīharituṃ; nappaṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samādāpaituṃ (?) mahāva॰ 366 samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau nālaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau alaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? paṭibalau hauti bhaisaj‍jaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ; sappāyāsappāyaṃ jānāti, asappāyaṃ apanāmaiti, sappāyaṃ upanāmaiti; maittacittau gilānaṃ upaṭṭhāti, nau āmisantarau; ajaigucchī hauti uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā vantaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā nīharituṃ; paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau alaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātu’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaanāyussāsuttaṃ

125. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā anāyussā| katamai pañ‍ca? asappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ na jānāti, apariṇatabhaujī ca hauti, akālacārī ca hauti, abrahmacārī ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā anāyussā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā āyussā| katamai pañ‍ca? sappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ jānāti, pariṇatabhaujī ca hauti, kālacārī ca hauti, brahmacārī ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā āyussā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaanāyussāsuttaṃ

126. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā anāyussā| katamai pañ‍ca? asappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ na jānāti, apariṇatabhaujī ca hauti, dussīlau ca, pāpamittau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā anāyussā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā āyussā| katamai pañ‍ca? sappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ  jānāti, pariṇatabhaujī ca hauti, sīlavā ca, kalyāṇamittau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā āyussā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vapakāsasuttaṃ

127. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ saṅghamhā vapakāsituṃ vi + apa + kāsituṃ = vapakāsituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, kāmasaṅkappabahulau ca viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ saṅghamhā vapakāsituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ saṅghamhā vapakāsituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, naikkhammasaṅkappabahulau na kāmasaṅkappabahulau (ka॰) ca viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ saṅghamhā vapakāsitu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. samaṇasukhasuttaṃ

128. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, samaṇadukkhāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina, asantuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, anabhiratau  ca brahmacariyaṃ carati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca samaṇadukkhāni|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, samaṇasukhāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina  saināsanaina, santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, abhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca samaṇasukhānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. parikuppasuttaṃ

129. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āpāyikā nairayikā parikuppā ataikicchā| katamai pañ‍ca? mātā mātaraṃ (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitā hauti, pitā pitaraṃ (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitau vauraupitā (ka॰) hauti, arahaṃ arahantaṃ (ka॰), arahā (syā॰) jīvitā vauraupitau hauti, tathāgatassa duṭṭhaina cittaina lauhitaṃ uppāditaṃ hauti, saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca āpāyikā nairayikā parikuppā ataikicchā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. byasanasuttaṃ

130. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, byasanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? ñātibyasanaṃ, bhaugabyasanaṃ, raugabyasanaṃ, sīlabyasanaṃ, diṭṭhibyasanaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sattā ñātibyasanahaitu vā  bhaugabyasanahaitu vā raugabyasanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| sīlabyasanahaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhibyasanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca byasanāni|

‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā pañ‍ca? ñātisampadā, bhaugasampadā, āraugyasampadā, sīlasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| na, bhikkhavai, sattā ñātisampadāhaitu vā bhaugasampadāhaitu vā āraugyasampadāhaitu  vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sīlasampadāhaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā diṭṭhisampadāhaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sampadā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

gilānavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

gilānau satisūpaṭṭhi, dvai upaṭṭhākā duvāyusā|

vapakāsasamaṇasukhā, parikuppaṃ byasanaina cāti||


(14) 4. rājavaggau

1. paṭhamacak‍kānuvattanasuttaṃ

131. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rājā cak‍kavattī dhammainaiva cak‍kaṃ vattaiti pavattaiti (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰); taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ appativattiyaṃ (sī॰)  kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pāṇinā|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī atthañ‍ñū ca hauti, dhammañ‍ñū ca, mattañ‍ñū ca, kālañ‍ñū ca, parisañ‍ñū ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rājā cak‍kavattī dhammainaiva cak‍kaṃ pavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pāṇinā|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammainaiva anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā  brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|
