‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau atthañ‍ñū, dhammañ‍ñū, mattañ‍ñū, kālañ‍ñū, parisañ‍ñū| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammainaiva anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti; taṃ hauti dhammacak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyacak‍kānuvattanasuttaṃ

132. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa jaiṭṭhau puttau pitarā pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ dhammainaiva anuppavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pāṇinā|

‘‘katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa jaiṭṭhau puttau atthañ‍ñū ca hauti, dhammañ‍ñū ca, mattañ‍ñū ca, kālañ‍ñū ca, parisañ‍ñū ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa jaiṭṭhau puttau pitarā pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ dhammainaiva anuppavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pāṇinā|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau tathāgataina anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadaiva anuppavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau atthañ‍ñū, dhammañ‍ñū, mattañ‍ñū, kālañ‍ñū, parisañ‍ñū| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau tathāgataina anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadaiva anuppavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dhammarājāsuttaṃ

133. ‘‘yaupi  sau yaupi khau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā, saupi na arājakaṃ cak‍kaṃ vattaitī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau pana, bhantai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa dhammikassa dhammarañ‍ñau rājā’’ti? ‘‘dhammau, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati antaujanasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati khattiyaisu anuyantaisu anuyuttaisu (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.14 …pai॰… balakāyasmiṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamajānapadaisu  samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu migapakkhīsu| sa  khau sau, bhikkhu, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā antaujanasmiṃ dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā khattiyaisu anuyantaisu balakāyasmiṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamajānapadaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu migapakkhīsu dhammainaiva cak‍kaṃ pavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina  pāṇinā|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati bhikkhūsu – ‘aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpau ājīvau saivitabbau, aivarūpau ājīvau na saivitabbau; aivarūpau gāmanigamau saivitabbau, aivarūpau gāmanigamau na saivitabbau’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati bhikkhunīsu bhikkhūsu bhikkhunīsu (sī॰ pī॰) …pai॰… upāsakaisu…pai॰… upāsikāsu – ‘aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ na saivitabbaṃ; aivarūpau ājīvau saivitabbau, aivarūpau ājīvau na saivitabbau; aivarūpau gāmanigamau saivitabbau, aivarūpau gāmanigamau na saivitabbau’’’ti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhu, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau  dhammarājā dhammañ‍ñaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā bhikkhūsu, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā bhikkhunīsu, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā upāsakaisu, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā upāsikāsu dhammainaiva anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti; taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ  appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. yassaṃdisaṃsuttaṃ

134. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati, sakasmiṃyaiva vijitai viharati|

‘‘katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; aḍḍhau hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau; balavā khau pana hauti caturaṅginiyā saināya saman‍nāgatau assavāya auvādapaṭikarāya; pariṇāyakau khau panassa hauti paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭibalau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ; tassimai cattārau dhammā yasaṃ paripācainti| sau iminā yasapañ‍camaina  yasaina pañ‍camaina (ka॰), pañ‍camaina (sī॰) dhammaina saman‍nāgatau yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati, sakasmiṃyaiva vijitai viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti vijitāvīnaṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati, vimuttacittauva vimuttacittau (sī॰ pī॰), vimuttacittau ca (ka॰) viharati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu – rājāva  khattiyau muddhāvasittau jātisampan‍nau; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā – rājāva  khattiyau muddhāvasittau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau; āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu – rājāva khattiyau muddhāvasittau balasampan‍nau; pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā – rājāva khattiyau muddhāvasittau pariṇāyakasampan‍nau; tassimai cattārau dhammā vimuttiṃ paripācainti| sau iminā vimuttipañ‍camaina dhammaina saman‍nāgatau yassaṃ yassaṃ disāyaṃ viharati vimuttacittauva viharati| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti vimuttacittāna’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamapatthanāsuttaṃ

135. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau raj‍jaṃ patthaiti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; abhirūpau hauti  dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau; mātāpitūnaṃ piyau hauti manāpau; naigamajānapadassa piyau hauti manāpau; yāni tāni rañ‍ñaṃ khattiyānaṃ muddhāvasittānaṃ sippaṭṭhānāni hatthismiṃ vā assasmiṃ vā rathasmiṃ vā dhanusmiṃ vā tharusmiṃ vā tattha sikkhitau hauti anavayau|

‘‘tassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| kasmāhaṃ raj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau| kasmāhaṃ raj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi mātāpitūnaṃ piyau manāpau| kasmāhaṃ raj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi naigamajānapadassa piyau manāpau| kasmāhaṃ raj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi yāni tāni rañ‍ñaṃ khattiyānaṃ muddhāvasittānaṃ  sippaṭṭhānāni hatthismiṃ vā assasmiṃ vā rathasmiṃ vā dhanusmiṃ vā tharusmiṃ vā, tattha tatthamhi (sī॰), tatthapi (ka॰) sikkhitau anavayau| kasmāhaṃ raj‍jaṃ na patthaiyya’nti! imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau raj‍jaṃ patthaiti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ patthaiti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau  purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau, samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya; asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī, yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu; āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā|

‘‘tassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi saddhau, saddahāmi tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ‘kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ  khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi appābādhau appātaṅkau samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi asaṭhau amāyāvī yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi āraddhavīriyau viharāmi akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ  khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi pañ‍ñavā udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyya’nti! imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ patthaitī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyapatthanāsuttaṃ

136. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau auparaj‍jaṃ uparaj‍jaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) patthaiti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; abhirūpau hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau; mātāpitūnaṃ piyau hauti manāpau, balakāyassa piyau hauti manāpau; paṇḍitau hauti viyattau maidhāvī paṭibalau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ|

‘‘tassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| kasmāhaṃ auparaj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau| kasmāhaṃ auparaj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi mātāpitūnaṃ piyau  manāpau| kasmāhaṃ auparaj‍jaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ  khaumhi balakāyassa piyau manāpau| kasmāhaṃ auparaj‍jaṃ  na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭibalau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ| kasmāhaṃ auparaj‍jaṃ na patthaiyya’nti! imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau auparaj‍jaṃ patthaiti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ patthaiti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau supaṭṭhitacittau (sī॰ syā॰), sūpaṭṭhitacittau (ka॰) hauti; āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khaumhi sīlavā, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharāmi ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhāmi sikkhāpadaisu| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi bahussutau sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ satthā byañ‍janā (sī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpā mai dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi āraddhavīriyau viharāmi akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyyaṃ! ahaṃ khaumhi pañ‍ñavā udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya  sammā  dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| kasmāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayaṃ na patthaiyya’nti! imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ patthaitī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. appaṃsupatisuttaṃ

137. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, appaṃ rattiyā supanti, bahuṃ jagganti| katamai pañ‍ca? itthī, bhikkhavai, purisādhippāyā appaṃ rattiyā supati, bahuṃ jaggati| purisau, bhikkhavai, itthādhippāyau appaṃ rattiyā supati, bahuṃ jaggati| caurau, bhikkhavai, ādānādhippāyau appaṃ rattiyā supati, bahuṃ jaggati| rājā rājayuttau (pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, rājakaraṇīyaisu yuttau appaṃ rattiyā supati, bahuṃ jaggati| bhikkhu, bhikkhavai, visaṃyaugādhippāyau appaṃ rattiyā supati, bahuṃ jaggati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca appaṃ rattiyā supanti, bahuṃ jaggantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. bhattādakasuttaṃ

138. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau bhattādakau ca hauti aukāsapharaṇau ca laṇḍasāraṇau ca salākaggāhī ca rañ‍ñau nāgautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau bhattādakau ca aukāsapharaṇau ca laṇḍasāraṇau ca salākaggāhī ca, rañ‍ñau nāgautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhattādakau ca hauti, aukāsapharaṇau ca mañ‍capīṭhamaddanau pīṭhamaddanau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca salākaggāhī ca, bhikkhutvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  bhattādakau ca hauti aukāsapharaṇau ca mañ‍capīṭhamaddanau ca salākaggāhī ca, bhikkhutvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. akkhamasuttaṃ

139. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau na rājārahau hauti na rājabhauggau, na rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau hatthikāyaṃ vā disvā assakāyaṃ vā disvā rathakāyaṃ vā disvā pattikāyaṃ vā disvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti saddānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau hatthisaddaṃ vā sutvā assasaddaṃ vā sutvā rathasaddaṃ vā sutvā pattisaddaṃ vā sutvā bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddaṃ vā sutvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti saddānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti gandhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau yai tai rañ‍ñau nāgā abhijātā saṅgāmāvacarā taisaṃ muttakarīsassa gandhaṃ ghāyitvā saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti gandhānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti  rasānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau aikissā vā tiṇaudakadattiyā vimānitau vihanītau (syā॰), vihānitau (katthaci) dvīhi vā tīhi vā catūhi vā pañ‍cahi vā tiṇaudakadattīhi vimānitau saṃsīdati visīdati, na santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti rasānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau  saṅgāmagatau aikaina vā saravaigaina viddhau, dvīhi vā tīhi vā catūhi vā pañ‍cahi vā saravaigaihi viddhau saṃsīdati visīdati, na  santhambhati na sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau akkhamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau na rājārahau hauti na rājabhauggau na rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu na āhunaiyyau hauti na pāhunaiyyau na dakkhiṇaiyyau na añ‍jalikaraṇīyau na anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ, akkhamau saddānaṃ, akkhamau gandhānaṃ, akkhamau rasānaṃ, akkhamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rajanīyai rūpai sāraj‍jati, na sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rūpānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti saddānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautaina  saddaṃ sutvā rajanīyai saddai sāraj‍jati, na sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti saddānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti gandhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā rajanīyai gandhai sāraj‍jati, na sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti gandhānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rasānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā rajanīyai rasai sāraj‍jati, na sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti rasānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā rajanīyai phauṭṭhabbai sāraj‍jati, na sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu na āhunaiyyau hauti na pāhunaiyyau na dakkhiṇaiyyau na añ‍jalikaraṇīyau na anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau hatthikāyaṃ vā disvā assakāyaṃ vā disvā rathakāyaṃ vā disvā pattikāyaṃ vā  disvā na saṃsīdati na visīdati, santhambhati sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti saddānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau hatthisaddaṃ vā sutvā assasaddaṃ vā sutvā rathasaddaṃ vā sutvā pattisaddaṃ vā sutvā bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddaṃ vā sutvā na saṃsīdati na visīdati, santhambhati sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti saddānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti gandhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau yai tai rañ‍ñau nāgā abhijātā saṅgāmāvacarā taisaṃ muttakarīsassa gandhaṃ ghāyitvā na saṃsīdati na visīdati, santhambhati sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti gandhānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti rasānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau aikissā vā tiṇaudakadattiyā vimānitau dvīhi vā tīhi vā catūhi vā pañ‍cahi  vā tiṇaudakadattīhi vimānitau na saṃsīdati na visīdati, santhambhati sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti rasānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau aikaina vā saravaigaina viddhau, dvīhi vā tīhi vā catūhi vā pañ‍cahi vā saravaigaihi viddhau na saṃsīdati na visīdati, santhambhati sak‍kauti saṅgāmaṃ autarituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rajanīyai rūpai na sāraj‍jati, sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti saddānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautaina saddaṃ sutvā rajanīyai saddai na sāraj‍jati, sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti saddānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti gandhānaṃ| idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā rajanīyai gandhai na sāraj‍jati, sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti gandhānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rasānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā rajanīyai rasai na sāraj‍jati, sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rasānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā rajanīyai phauṭṭhabbai na sāraj‍jati, sak‍kauti cittaṃ samādahituṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti phauṭṭhabbānaṃ|

‘‘imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sautasuttaṃ

140. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā ca hauti, hantā ca, rakkhitā ca, khantā ca, gantā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau yamainaṃ hatthidammasārathi hatthidammasārathī (sī॰) kāraṇaṃ kāraiti – yadi vā katapubbaṃ yadi vā akatapubbaṃ  – taṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā aṭṭhikatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.114 manasi katvā sabbaṃ caitasā sabbacaitasā (?) saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau suṇāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau hantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau hatthimpi hanati hanti (sī॰ pī॰), hatthāruhampi hanati, assampi hanati, assāruhampi hanati, rathampi hanati, rathikampi rathāruhampi (pī॰) hanati, pattikampi hanati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau hantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau rakkhitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau  rakkhati purimaṃ kāyaṃ, rakkhati pacchimaṃ kāyaṃ, rakkhati purimai pādai, rakkhati pacchimai pādai, rakkhati sīsaṃ, rakkhati kaṇṇai, rakkhati dantai, rakkhati sauṇḍaṃ, rakkhati vāladhiṃ, rakkhati hatthāruhaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau rakkhitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau khamau hauti sattippahārānaṃ asippahārānaṃ usuppahārānaṃ pharasuppahārānaṃ bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau gantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau yamainaṃ hatthidammasārathi disaṃ paisaiti – yadi vā gatapubbaṃ yadi vā agatapubbaṃ – taṃ khippamaiva gantā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau gantā hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgaṃtvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā ca hauti, hantā ca, rakkhitā  ca, khantā ca, gantā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai aṭṭhiṃkatvā manasi katvā sabbaṃ caitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti (hanati) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu a॰ ni॰ 4.114 byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti; uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti (hanati) ( ) natthi sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu a॰ ni॰ 4.114 byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rakkhitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rakkhitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsa … siriṃsapa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pasamphassānaṃ; duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ  adhivāsakajātikau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yā sā disā agatapubbā iminā dīghaina addhunā, yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ, taṃ khippañ‍ñaiva gantā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  gantā hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

rājavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

cak‍kānuvattanā rājā, yassaṃdisaṃ dvai caiva patthanā|

appaṃsupati bhattādau, akkhamau ca sautaina cāti||


(15) 5. tikaṇḍakīvaggau

1. avajānātisuttaṃ

141. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? datvā avajānāti, saṃvāsaina avajānāti, ādhaiyyamukhau ādiyyamukhau (sī॰), ādaiyyamukhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), ādiyamukhau (pī॰) aṭṭhakathāya paṭhamasaṃvaṇṇanānurūpaṃ| pu॰ pa॰ 193 passitabbaṃ hauti, laulau hauti, mandau maumūhau hauti mandau hauti maumūhau (sī॰)|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau datvā avajānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalassa daiti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ daimi; ayaṃ paṭiggaṇhātī’ti| tamainaṃ datvā avajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau datvā avajānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau saṃvāsaina avajānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ saṃvasati dvai vā tīṇi vā vassāni| tamainaṃ saṃvāsaina avajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau saṃvāsaina avajānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ādhaiyyamukhau  hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau parassa vaṇṇai vā avaṇṇai vā bhāsiyamānai taṃ khippañ‍ñaiva adhimuc‍citā adhimuc‍citau (syā॰) hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ādhaiyyamukhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau laulau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ittarasaddhau hauti ittarabhattī ittarapaimau ittarappasādau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau laulau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau mandau maumūhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kusalākusalai dhammai na jānāti, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai dhammai na jānāti, hīnappaṇītai dhammai na jānāti, kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai na jānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau mandau maumūhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ārabhatisuttaṃ

142. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ārabhati ca vippaṭisārī ca hauti; tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

pu॰ pa॰ 191 ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ārabhati, na vippaṭisārī hauti; tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na ārabhati, vippaṭisārī  hauti; tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ  pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na ārabhati na vippaṭisārī hauti; tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na ārabhati na vippaṭisārī hauti; tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau ārabhati ca vippaṭisārī ca hauti, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā  nirujjhanti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmatau khau ārambhajā ārabbhajā (pī॰ ka॰), ārabhajā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āsavā saṃvij‍janti, vippaṭisārajā āsavā pavaḍḍhanti saṃvaḍḍhanti (ka॰), sādhu vatāyasmā ārambhajai āsavai pahāya vippaṭisārajai āsavai paṭivinaudaitvā cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaitu bhāvaituṃ (sī॰ pī॰); aivamāyasmā amunā pañ‍camaina puggalaina samasamau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau ārabhati na vippaṭisārī hauti, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmatau khau ārambhajā āsavā saṃvij‍janti, vippaṭisārajā āsavā na pavaḍḍhanti, sādhu vatāyasmā ārambhajai āsavai pahāya cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaitu; aivamāyasmā  amunā pañ‍camaina puggalaina samasamau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau na ārabhati vippaṭisārī hauti, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmatau  khau ārambhajā āsavā na saṃvij‍janti, vippaṭisārajā āsavā pavaḍḍhanti, sādhu vatāyasmā vippaṭisārajai āsavai paṭivinaudaitvā cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaitu; aivamāyasmā amunā pañ‍camaina puggalaina samasamau bhavissatī’’’ ti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau na ārabhati na vippaṭisārī hauti, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā  akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmatau khau ārambhajā āsavā na saṃvij‍janti, vippaṭisārajā āsavā na pavaḍḍhanti, sādhu vatāyasmā cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaitu; aivamāyasmā amunā pañ‍camaina puggalaina samasamau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau puggalā amunā pañ‍camaina puggalaina aivaṃ auvadiyamānā aivaṃ anusāsiyamānā anupubbaina āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇantī’’ti pu॰ pa॰ 191| dutiyaṃ|

3. sārandadasuttaṃ

143. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattānaṃ licchavisatānaṃ sārandadai caitiyai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā  udapādi – ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? hatthiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, assaratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, maṇiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, itthiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, gahapatiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’nti|

atha  khau tai licchavī maggai purisaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ paisaisuṃ (syā॰ ka॰) – ‘‘yadā tvaṃ yathā tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ambhau purisa, passaiyyāsi bhagavantaṃ, atha amhākaṃ āraucaiyyāsī’’ti| addasā khau sau purisau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvāna yaina tai licchavī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai licchavī aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ sau, bhantai, bhagavā gacchati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñathā’’ti|

atha khau tai licchavī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tai licchavī bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘sādhu, bhantai, yaina sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau bhagavā yaina  sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā tai licchavī aitadavauca – ‘‘kāya nuttha, licchavī, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? hatthiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, assaratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, maṇiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, itthiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, gahapatiratanassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’’nti|

‘‘kāmādhimuttānaṃ  vata, bhau, licchavīnaṃ kāmādhimuttānaṃ vata vau licchavīnaṃ (sī॰), kāmādhimuttānaṃ vata vau licchavī (syā॰), kāmādhimuttānaṃva vau licchavī (?) kāmaṃyaiva ārabbha antarākathā udapādi| pañ‍can‍naṃ, licchavī, ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa  daisaitā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisitassa viñ‍ñātā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisitassa viñ‍ñātā viñ‍ñātassa (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.195 dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, katañ‍ñū katavaidī puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, licchavī, pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. tikaṇḍakīsuttaṃ

144. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sākaitai viharati tikaṇḍakīvanai kaṇḍakīvanai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.902)| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī appaṭik‍kūlai paṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viharaiyya| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai  ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī  viharaiyya| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyya satau sampajānau|

‘‘kiñ‍ca kathañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya? ‘mā mai rajanīyaisu dhammaisu rāgau udapādī’ti – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya? ‘mā mai dausanīyaisu dhammaisu dausau udapādī’ti – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ  paṭic‍ca appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya? ‘mā mai rajanīyaisu dhammaisu rāgau udapādi, mā mai dausanīyaisu dhammaisu dausau udapādī’ti – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya? ‘mā mai dausanīyaisu dhammaisu dausau udapādi, mā mai rajanīyaisu dhammaisu rāgau udapādī’ti – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyya? ‘satau sampajānau mā mai  k‍vacani k‍vacini (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) katthaci kiñ‍canaṃ kiñ‍cana (sī॰ pī॰) rajanīyaisu dhammaisu rāgau udapādi, mā mai k‍vacani katthaci kiñ‍canaṃ dausanīyaisu dhammaisu dausau udapādi, mā mai k‍vacani katthaci kiñ‍canaṃ mauhanīyaisu dhammaisu mauhau udapādī’ti – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyya satau sampajānau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. nirayasuttaṃ

145. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī  hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mittasuttaṃ

146. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau na saivitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kammantaṃ kāraiti, adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyati, pāmaukkhaisu bhikkhūsu paṭiviruddhau hauti, dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ avatthānacārikaṃ (syā॰) anuyuttau viharati, nappaṭibalau hauti kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ  samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau na saivitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau saivitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na kammantaṃ kāraiti, na adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyati, na pāmaukkhaisu bhikkhūsu paṭiviruddhau hauti, na dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttau viharati, paṭibalau hauti kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau saivitabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. asappurisadānasuttaṃ

147. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, asappurisadānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? asak‍kac‍caṃ daiti, acittīkatvā acittikatvā (pī॰), acitiṃ katvā (syā॰), acittiṃ katvā (ka॰) daiti, asahatthā daiti, apaviddhaṃ apaviṭṭaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) daiti, anāgamanadiṭṭhikau daiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca asappurisadānāni|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, sappurisadānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sak‍kac‍caṃ daiti, cittīkatvā daiti, sahatthā daiti, anapaviddhaṃ daiti, āgamanadiṭṭhikau daiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sappurisadānānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sappurisadānasuttaṃ

148. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, sappurisadānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhāya dānaṃ daiti, sak‍kac‍caṃ dānaṃ daiti, kālaina dānaṃ daiti, anuggahitacittau anaggahitacittau (sī॰) dānaṃ daiti, attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca anupahac‍ca dānaṃ daiti|

‘‘saddhāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, dānaṃ datvā yattha yattha tassa dānassa  vipākau nibbattati, aḍḍhau ca hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau, abhirūpau ca hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau|

‘‘sak‍kac‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dānaṃ datvā yattha yattha tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, aḍḍhau ca hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau| yaipissa tai haunti puttāti vā dārāti vā dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti kammakārāti (ka॰) vā, taipi sussūsanti sautaṃ audahanti añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti|

‘‘kālaina khau pana, bhikkhavai, dānaṃ datvā yattha yattha tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, aḍḍhau ca hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau; kālāgatā cassa atthā pacurā haunti|

‘‘anuggahitacittau khau pana, bhikkhavai, dānaṃ datvā yattha yattha tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, aḍḍhau ca hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau; uḷāraisu ca pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu bhaugāya cittaṃ namati|

‘‘attānañ‍ca  parañ‍ca anupahac‍ca khau pana, bhikkhavai, dānaṃ datvā yattha yattha tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, aḍḍhau ca hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau; na cassa kutauci bhaugānaṃ upaghātau āgacchati aggitau vā udakatau vā rājatau vā cauratau vā appiyatau vā dāyādatau vā appiyatau vā dāyādatau vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), appiyadāyādatau vā (ka॰)| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sappurisadānānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasamayavimuttasuttaṃ

149. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ na pac‍cavaikkhati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasamayavimuttasuttaṃ

150. kathā॰ 267 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā samayavimuttassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

tikaṇḍakīvaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

datvā avajānāti ārabhati ca, sārandada tikaṇḍa nirayaina ca|

mittau asappurisasappurisaina, samayavimuttaṃ aparai dvaiti||

tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



4. catutthapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(16) 1. saddhammavaggau

1. paṭhamasammattaniyāmasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kathaṃ paribhauti, kathikaṃ kathitaṃ (ka॰) paribhauti, attānaṃ paribhauti, vikkhittacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti, anaikaggacittau ayaunisau ca ayaunisau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) manasi karauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ  bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na kathaṃ paribhauti, na kathikaṃ paribhauti, na attānaṃ paribhauti, avikkhittacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti, aikaggacittau yaunisau ca manasi karauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasammattaniyāmasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kathaṃ paribhauti, kathikaṃ paribhauti, attānaṃ paribhauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau aiḷamūgau, anañ‍ñātai añ‍ñātamānī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na kathaṃ paribhauti, na kathikaṃ paribhauti, na attānaṃ paribhauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti  ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau, na anañ‍ñātai añ‍ñātamānī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyasammattaniyāmasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? makkhī dhammaṃ suṇāti makkhapariyuṭṭhitau, upārambhacittau saupārambhacittau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dhammaṃ suṇāti randhagavaisī, dhammadaisakai  āhatacittau hauti khīlajātau khilajātau (syā॰ pī॰), duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau aiḷamūgau, anañ‍ñātai añ‍ñātamānī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? amakkhī dhammaṃ suṇāti na makkhapariyuṭṭhitau, anupārambhacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti na randhagavaisī, dhammadaisakai anāhatacittau hauti akhīlajātau, pañ‍ñavā hauti ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau, na  anañ‍ñātai añ‍ñātamānī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasaddhammasammausasuttaṃ

154. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ suṇanti, na sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, na sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ dhārainti, na sak‍kac‍caṃ dhātānaṃ dhatānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhanti, na  sak‍kac‍caṃ atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍janti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ suṇanti, sak‍kac‍caṃ  dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ dhārainti, sak‍kac‍caṃ dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhanti, sak‍kac‍caṃ atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍janti| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasaddhammasammausasuttaṃ

155. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dhammaṃ na pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ  na vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ na vitthāraina paraṃ paraisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), parai (?) vācainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ na vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karaunti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ na caitasā anuvitak‍kainti anuvicārainti manasānupaikkhanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya  saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau  dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraṃ vācainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karaunti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kainti anuvicārainti manasānupaikkhanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tatiyasaddhammasammausasuttaṃ

156. a॰ ni॰ 4.160 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū duggahitaṃ suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇanti dun‍nikkhittaihi padabyañ‍janaihi| dun‍nikkhittassa, bhikkhavai, padabyañ‍janassa atthaupi dun‍nayau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dubbacā haunti, dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā, akkhamā appadakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tai na sak‍kac‍caṃ suttantaṃ paraṃ vācainti; taisaṃ ac‍cayaina chin‍namūlakau suttantau hauti appaṭisaraṇau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairā bhikkhū bāhulikā haunti sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā  anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| sāpi hauti bāhulikā sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti| saṅghai khau pana, bhikkhavai, bhin‍nai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ak‍kausā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paribhāsā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ parikkhaipā ca haunti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paric‍cajanā paric‍cajā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ca haunti| tattha appasan‍nā caiva nappasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū suggahitaṃ suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇanti sunikkhittaihi  padabyañ‍janaihi| sunikkhittassa, bhikkhavai, padabyañ‍janassa atthaupi sunayau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū suvacā haunti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā, khamā padakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya  saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tai sak‍kac‍caṃ suttantaṃ paraṃ vācainti; taisaṃ ac‍cayaina na chin‍namūlakau acchin‍namūlakau (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.160 suttantau hauti sappaṭisaraṇau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairā bhikkhū na bāhulikā haunti na sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā; vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā  anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| sāpi hauti na bāhulikā na sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā, vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghau samaggau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsuṃ viharati| saṅghai khau pana, bhikkhavai, samaggai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ak‍kausā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paribhāsā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ parikkhaipā haunti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paric‍cajanā haunti| tattha appasan‍nā caiva pasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca bhiyyaubhāvau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. duk‍kathāsuttaṃ

157. ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalānaṃ kathā duk‍kathā puggalai puggalaṃ puggalaṃ puggalaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) upanidhāya| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? assaddhassa, bhikkhavai, saddhākathā duk‍kathā; dussīlassa sīlakathā duk‍kathā; appassutassa bāhusac‍cakathā duk‍kathā; maccharissa macchariyassa (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) cāgakathā duk‍kathā; duppañ‍ñassa pañ‍ñākathā duk‍kathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, assaddhassa saddhākathā duk‍kathā? assaddhau, bhikkhavai, saddhākathāya kacchamānāya abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, saddhāsampadaṃ attani na samanupassati na sampassati (sī॰), na ca labhati tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā assaddhassa saddhākathā duk‍kathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, dussīlassa sīlakathā duk‍kathā? dussīlau, bhikkhavai, sīlakathāya kacchamānāya abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadaṃ attani na samanupassati na ca labhati tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā dussīlassa sīlakathā duk‍kathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, appassutassa bāhusac‍cakathā duk‍kathā? appassutau, bhikkhavai, bāhusac‍cakathāya kacchamānāya abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, sutasampadaṃ attani na samanupassati, na ca labhati tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā appassutassa bāhusac‍cakathā  duk‍kathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, maccharissa cāgakathā duk‍kathā? maccharī, bhikkhavai, cāgakathāya kacchamānāya abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, cāgasampadaṃ attani na samanupassati na ca labhati  tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā maccharissa cāgakathā duk‍kathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, duppañ‍ñassa pañ‍ñākathā duk‍kathā? duppañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñākathāya kacchamānāya abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ attani na samanupassati, na ca labhati tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā duppañ‍ñassa pañ‍ñākathā duk‍kathā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ puggalānaṃ kathā duk‍kathā puggalai puggalaṃ upanidhāya|

‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalānaṃ kathā sukathā puggalai puggalaṃ upanidhāya| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? saddhassa, bhikkhavai, saddhākathā sukathā; sīlavatau sīlakathā sukathā; bahussutassa bāhusac‍cakathā sukathā; cāgavatau cāgakathā sukathā; pañ‍ñavatau pañ‍ñākathā sukathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, saddhassa saddhākathā sukathā? saddhau, bhikkhavai, saddhākathāya kacchamānāya nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, saddhāsampadaṃ attani samanupassati labhati ca tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā saddhassa saddhākathā sukathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau sīlakathā sukathā? sīlavā, bhikkhavai, sīlakathāya kacchamānāya nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na  patitthīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadaṃ attani samanupassati, labhati ca tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā sīlavatau sīlakathā sukathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, bahussutassa bāhusac‍cakathā sukathā? bahussutau, bhikkhavai, bāhusac‍cakathāya kacchamānāya nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati  na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, sutasampadaṃ attani samanupassati, labhati ca tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā bahussutassa bāhusac‍cakathā sukathā|

‘‘kasmā  ca, bhikkhavai, cāgavatau cāgakathā sukathā? cāgavā, bhikkhavai, cāgakathāya kacchamānāya nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, cāgasampadaṃ attani samanupassati, labhati ca tataunidānaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā cāgavatau cāgakathā sukathā|

‘‘kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñavatau pañ‍ñākathā sukathā? pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñākathāya kacchamānāya nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ attani samanupassati labhati ca tataunidānaṃ  pītipāmauj‍jaṃ| tasmā pañ‍ñavatau pañ‍ñākathā sukathā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ puggalānaṃ kathā sukathā puggalai puggalaṃ upanidhāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sāraj‍jasuttaṃ

158. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sāraj‍jaṃ auk‍kantau auk‍kamantau (ka॰) hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, dupañ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sāraj‍jaṃ auk‍kantau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu visāradau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu visāradau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. udāyīsuttaṃ

159. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī mahatiyā gihiparisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau nisin‍nau hauti| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ mahatiyā gihiparisāya parivutaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, udāyī mahatiyā gihiparisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti daisaintau nisin‍nau’’ti (syā॰)|

‘‘na khau, ānanda, sukaraṃ paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| paraisaṃ, ānanda, dhammaṃ daisaintaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? ‘anupubbiṃ kathaṃ ānupubbikathaṃ (sī॰), anupubbikathaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) kathaissāmī’ti paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau; ‘pariyāyadassāvī kathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau; ‘anuddayataṃ paṭic‍ca kathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau; ‘na āmisantarau kathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau; ‘attānañ‍ca parañ‍ca anupahac‍ca kathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau| na khau, ānanda, sukaraṃ paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| paraisaṃ, ānanda, dhammaṃ daisaintaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā paraisaṃ dhammau daisaitabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. duppaṭivinaudayasuttaṃ

160. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, uppan‍nā duppaṭivinaudayā| katamai pañ‍ca? uppan‍nau  rāgau duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍nau dausau duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍nau mauhau  duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍naṃ paṭibhānaṃ duppaṭivinaudayaṃ, uppan‍naṃ gamikacittaṃ duppaṭivinaudayaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca uppan‍nā duppaṭivinaudayā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

saddhammavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

tayau sammattaniyāmā, tayau saddhammasammausā|

duk‍kathā caiva sāraj‍jaṃ, udāyidubbinaudayāti||


(17) 2. āghātavaggau

1. paṭhamaāghātapaṭivinayasuttaṃ

161. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āghātapaṭivinayā yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, maittā tasmiṃ puggalai bhāvaitabbā; aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, karuṇā tasmiṃ puggalai bhāvaitabbā; aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, upaikkhā tasmiṃ puggalai bhāvaitabbā; aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, asatiamanasikārau tasmiṃ puggalai āpaj‍jitabbau; aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, kammassakatā tasmiṃ puggalai adhiṭṭhātabbā – ‘kammassakau ayamāyasmā kammadāyādau kammayauni kammabandhu kammappaṭisaraṇau, yaṃ kammaṃ karissati kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā tassa dāyādau bhavissatī’ti; aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca āghātapaṭivinayā, yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaāghātapaṭivinayasuttaṃ

162. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, āghātapaṭivinayā  yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti parisuddhavacīsamācārau; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau  puggalau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti parisuddhakāyasamācārau; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti aparisuddhavacīsamācārau, labhati ca kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti aparisuddhavacīsamācārau, na ca labhati kālaina  kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau parisuddhakāyasamācārau parisuddhavacīsamācārau, labhati ca kālaina vā kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau parisuddhavacīsamācārau, kathaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, bhikkhu paṃsukūlikau rathiyāya nantakaṃ disvā vāmaina pādaina niggaṇhitvā dakkhiṇaina pādaina pattharitvā vitthāraitvā (sī॰ pī॰), yau tattha sārau taṃ paripātaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khvāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau parisuddhavacīsamācārau, yāssa aparisuddhakāyasamācāratā na  sāssa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā, yā  ca khvāssa parisuddhavacīsamācāratā sāssa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā| aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau parisuddhakāyasamācārau, kathaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, paukkharaṇī saivālapaṇakapariyaunaddhā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| sau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā ubhauhi hatthaihi iticiti ca saivālapaṇakaṃ apaviyūhitvā añ‍jalinā pivitvā pak‍kamaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau parisuddhakāyasamācārau, yāssa aparisuddhavacīsamācāratā na sāssa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā, yā ca khvāssa parisuddhakāyasamācāratā sāssa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā| aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau labhati ca  kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, kathaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, parittaṃ gaupadai gaupadakai (sī॰ syā॰) udakaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| tassa aivamassa – ‘idaṃ khau parittaṃ gaupadai udakaṃ| sacāhaṃ añ‍jalinā vā pivissāmi bhājanaina vā khaubhaissāmipi taṃ  lauḷaissāmipi taṃ apaiyyampi taṃ karissāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ catuk‍kuṇḍikau catuguṇḍikau (sī॰), catukuṇḍikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), catukauṇḍikau (dī॰ ni॰ 3.7) nipatitvā gaupītakaṃ pivitvā pak‍kamaiyya’nti| sau catuk‍kuṇḍikau nipatitvā gaupītakaṃ pivitvā pak‍kamaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau labhati ca kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, yāssa aparisuddhakāyasamācāratā na sāssa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā; yāpissa aparisuddhavacīsamācāratā na sāpissa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā| yañ‍ca khau sau labhati kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ  caitasau pasādaṃ, tamaivassa tadaivassa (sī॰ syā॰) tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbaṃ| aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau na ca labhati kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, kathaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau| tassa purataupissa dūrai gāmau pacchataupissa dūrai gāmau| sau na labhaiyya sappāyāni bhaujanāni, na labhaiyya sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, na labhaiyya patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ, na labhaiyya gāmantanāyakaṃ| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau purisau passaiyya addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau| sau tasmiṃ purisai kāruñ‍ñaṃyaiva upaṭṭhāpaiyya, anuddayaṃyaiva upaṭṭhāpaiyya, anukampaṃyaiva upaṭṭhāpaiyya – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ purisau labhaiyya sappāyāni bhaujanāni, labhaiyya sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, labhaiyya patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ, labhaiyya gāmantanāyakaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? māyaṃ  ayaṃ (ka॰) purisau idhaiva anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jī’ti āpaj‍jaiyya (ka॰)! aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau aparisuddhavacīsamācārau na ca labhati kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, aivarūpaipi aivarūpai (pī॰), āvusau, puggalai kāruñ‍ñaṃyaiva upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ anuddayāyaiva upaṭṭhāpaitabbā anukampāyaiva upaṭṭhāpaitabbā – ‘ahau vata ayamāyasmā kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiyya, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiyya, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiyya! taṃ kissa haitu? māyaṃ āyasmā ayamāyasmā (ka॰) kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jī’ti upapaj‍jatīti (ka॰)! aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau parisuddhakāyasamācārau parisuddhavacīsamācārau labhati ca kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, kathaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau? saiyyathāpi, āvusau, paukkharaṇī acchaudakā sātaudakā sītaudakā acchaudikā sātaudikā sītaudikā (sī॰) saitakā saitaudakā (ka॰) supatitthā ramaṇīyā nānārukkhaihi sañchan‍nā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| sau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā nhātvā ca pivitvā ca pac‍cuttaritvā tatthaiva rukkhacchāyāya nisīdaiyya vā nipaj‍jaiyya vā|

aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, yvāyaṃ puggalau parisuddhakāyasamācārau parisuddhavacīsamācārau labhati ca kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, yāpissa  parisuddhakāyasamācāratā sāpissa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā; yāpissa parisuddhavacīsamācāratā sāpissa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbā; yampi labhati kālaina kālaṃ caitasau vivaraṃ caitasau pasādaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai manasi kātabbaṃ| aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| samantapāsādikaṃ, āvusau, puggalaṃ āgamma cittaṃ pasīdati|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍ca āghātapaṭivinayā, yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sākacchasuttaṃ

163. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

a॰ ni॰ 5.65-66 ‘‘pañ‍cahāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ sākacchau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ  byākattā hauti; attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti| imaihi khau, āvusau, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ sākacchau sabrahmacārīna’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sājīvasuttaṃ

164. a॰ ni॰ 5.65 tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘pañ‍cahi, āvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsājīvau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti, samādhisampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti, vimuttisampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti; attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti, vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadākathāya ca āgataṃ pañhaṃ byākattā hauti| imaihi khau, āvusau, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃsājīvau sabrahmacārīna’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. pañhapucchāsuttaṃ

165. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, sabbau sau pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? mandattā maumūhattā maumuhattā (sī॰) paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, pāpicchau icchāpakatau paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, paribhavaṃ paraṃ  pañhaṃ pucchati, añ‍ñātukāmau paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, atha vā panaivaṃcittau atha vā pakuppantau (sī॰ pī॰) paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘sacai mai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai nau ca (syā॰) mai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati ahamassa sammadaiva byākarissāmī’ti| yau hi kauci, āvusau, paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchati, sabbau sau imaihi pañ‍cahi  ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina| ahaṃ khau panāvusau, aivaṃcittau paraṃ pañhaṃ pucchāmi – ‘sacai mai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati  ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai mai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati, ahamassa sammadaiva byākarissāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. niraudhasuttaṃ

166. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ  samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi samāpaj‍jaiyyapi vuṭṭhahaiyyapi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – atthaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiyya, atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ kabaḷiṃkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – natthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā  sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiyya, atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – natthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yāvatatiyakampi yāvatatiyampi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) khau mai āyasmā udāyī paṭik‍kausati, na ca mai kauci bhikkhu anumaudati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ  ārādhaiyya, atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – natthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ  samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiyya, atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – natthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti|

atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavataupi khau mai sammukhā āyasmā udāyī yāvatatiyakaṃ paṭik‍kausati, na ca mai kauci bhikkhu anumaudati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ tuṇhī assa’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kaṃ pana tvaṃ, udāyi, manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ pac‍caisī’’ti? ‘‘yai tai, bhantai, daivā arūpinau sañ‍ñāmayā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau tuyhaṃ, udāyi, bālassa abyattassa bhaṇitaina! tvampi nāma bhaṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti! atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘atthi nāma, ānanda, thairaṃ bhikkhuṃ vihaisiyamānaṃ ajjhupaikkhissatha| na hi nāma, ānanda, kāruñ‍ñampi bhavissati thairamhi byattamhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bhikkhumhi vihaisiyamānamhī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhānaṃ| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiyya, atik‍kammaiva kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāpi vuṭṭhahaiyyāpi – atthaitaṃ ṭhāna’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau yaināyasmā upavāṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāvusau upavāṇa, añ‍ñai thairai bhikkhū vihaisainti| mayaṃ taina na muc‍cāma| anacchariyaṃ khau, panaitaṃ āvusau upavāṇa, yaṃ bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau aitadaiva ārabbha udāharaiyya yathā āyasmantaṃyaivaittha upavāṇaṃ paṭibhāsaiyya| idānaiva amhākaṃ sāraj‍jaṃ auk‍kanta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘katīhi nu khau, upavāṇa, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhantai, thairau bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya  anailagalāya anailagaḷāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ  khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhantai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu  sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, upavāṇa! imaihi khau, upavāṇa, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau thairau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| imai cai, upavāṇa, pañ‍ca dhammā thairassa bhikkhunau na saṃvij‍jaiyyuṃ, taṃ sabrahmacārī na sak‍karaiyyuṃ na garuṃ karaiyyuṃ na mānaiyyuṃ na pūjaiyyuṃ khaṇḍic‍caina pālic‍caina valittacatāya| yasmā ca khau, upavāṇa, imai pañ‍ca dhammā thairassa bhikkhunau saṃvij‍janti, tasmā taṃ sabrahmacārī sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjaintī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. caudanāsuttaṃ

167. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘caudakaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? kālaina vakkhāmi, nau akālaina; bhūtaina vakkhāmi, nau abhūtaina; saṇhaina vakkhāmi, nau pharusaina; atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi, nau anatthasaṃhitaina; maittacittau maittacittaina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) cūḷava॰ 400 passitabbaṃ vakkhāmi, nau dausantarau dausantaraina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| caudakaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ passāmi akālaina caudiyamānaṃ nau kālaina kupitaṃ, abhūtaina caudiyamānaṃ nau bhūtaina kupitaṃ, pharusaina caudiyamānaṃ nau saṇhaina kupitaṃ, anatthasaṃhitaina caudiyamānaṃ nau atthasaṃhitaina kupitaṃ, dausantaraina caudiyamānaṃ nau maittacittaina kupitaṃ|

‘‘adhammacuditassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau upadahitabbau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) – ‘akālaināyasmā cuditau nau kālaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; abhūtaināyasmā cuditau nau bhūtaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; pharusaināyasmā cuditau nau saṇhaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; anatthasaṃhitaināyasmā cuditau nau atthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; dausantaraināyasmā cuditau nau maittacittaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāyā’ti| adhammacuditassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau|
