‘‘adhammacaudakassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – ‘akālaina tai, āvusau, cauditau cuditau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nau kālaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; abhūtaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau bhūtaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; pharusaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau saṇhaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; anatthasaṃhitaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau atthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; dausantaraina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau maittacittaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāyā’ti| adhammacaudakassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā na añ‍ñaupi bhikkhu  abhūtaina caudaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāti|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, āvusau, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ passāmi kālaina caudiyamānaṃ nau akālaina kupitaṃ, bhūtaina caudiyamānaṃ nau abhūtaina kupitaṃ, saṇhaina caudiyamānaṃ nau pharusaina kupitaṃ, atthasaṃhitaina caudiyamānaṃ nau anatthasaṃhitaina kupitaṃ, maittacittaina caudiyamānaṃ nau dausantaraina kupitaṃ|

‘‘dhammacuditassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – ‘kālaināyasmā cuditau nau akālaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; bhūtaināyasmā cuditau nau abhūtaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; saṇhaināyasmā cuditau nau pharusaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; atthasaṃhitaināyasmā cuditau nau anatthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; maittacittaināyasmā cuditau nau dausantaraina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāyā’ti| dhammacuditassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau|

‘‘dhammacaudakassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – ‘kālaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau akālaina, alaṃ  tai avippaṭisārāya; bhūtaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau abhūtaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; saṇhaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau  pharusaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; atthasaṃhitaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau anatthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; maittacittaina tai, āvusau, cauditau nau dausantaraina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāyā’ti| dhammacaudakassa, āvusau, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā añ‍ñaupi  bhikkhu bhūtaina cauditabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāti|

‘‘cuditaina, āvusau, puggalaina dvīsu dhammaisu patiṭṭhātabbaṃ – sac‍cai ca, akuppai ca| maṃ caipi pañ‍cahi (syā॰ ka॰), āvusau, parai caudaiyyuṃ kālaina vā akālaina vā bhūtaina vā abhūtaina vā saṇhaina vā pharusaina vā atthasaṃhitaina vā anatthasaṃhitaina vā maittacittā maittacittaina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.227 passitabbaṃ vā dausantarā dausantaraina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) vā, ahampi dvīsuyaiva dhammaisu patiṭṭhahaiyyaṃ – sac‍cai ca, akuppai ca| sacai jānaiyyaṃ – ‘atthaisau mayi dhammau’ti, ‘atthī’ti naṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘saṃvij‍jataisau mayi dhammau’ti| sacai jānaiyyaṃ – ‘natthaisau mayi dhammau’ti, ‘natthī’ti naṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘naisau dhammau mayi saṃvij‍jatī’ti|

‘‘aivampi khau tai aivampi khau (ka॰), sāriputta, vuc‍camānā atha ca panidhaikac‍cai maughapurisā na padakkhiṇaṃ gaṇhantī’’ti|

‘‘yai tai, bhantai, puggalā assaddhā jīvikatthā na saddhā  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā saṭhā māyāvinau kaitabinau kaiṭubhinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu aguttadvārā bhaujanai amattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai anapaikkhavantau sikkhāya na tibbagāravā bāhulikā sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā kusītā hīnavīriyā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā duppañ‍ñā aiḷamūgā, tai mayā aivaṃ vuc‍camānā na padakkhiṇaṃ gaṇhanti|

‘‘yai pana tai, bhantai, kulaputtā  saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā asaṭhā amāyāvinau akaitabinau anuddhatā anun‍naḷā acapalā amukharā avikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu guttadvārā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ  anuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai apaikkhavantau sikkhāya tibbagāravā na bāhulikā na sāthalikā auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā āraddhavīriyā  pahitattā upaṭṭhitassatinau sampajānā samāhitā aikaggacittā pañ‍ñavantau anaiḷamūgā, tai mayā aivaṃ vuc‍camānā padakkhiṇaṃ gaṇhantīti|

‘‘yai tai, sāriputta, puggalā assaddhā jīvikatthā na saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā saṭhā māyāvinau kaitabinau uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu aguttadvārā bhaujanai amattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai anapaikkhavantau sikkhāya na tibbagāravā bāhulikā sāthalikā auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā kusītā hīnavīriyā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā duppañ‍ñā aiḷamūgā, tiṭṭhantu tai|

‘‘yai pana, tai sāriputta, kulaputtā saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā asaṭhā amāyāvinau akaitabinau anuddhatā anun‍naḷā acapalā amukharā avikiṇṇavācā indriyaisu guttadvārā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā sāmañ‍ñai apaikkhavantau sikkhāya tibbagāravā na bāhulikā na sāthalikā auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā āraddhavīriyā pahitattā upaṭṭhitassatinau sampajānā samāhitā aikaggacittā pañ‍ñavantau anaiḷamūgā, tai tvaṃ, sāriputta, vadaiyyāsi| auvada, sāriputta, sabrahmacārī; anusāsa, sāriputta, sabrahmacārī – ‘asaddhammā vuṭṭhāpaitvā saddhammai patiṭṭhāpaissāmi sabrahmacārī’ti| aivañhi tai, sāriputta, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. sīlasuttaṃ

168. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dussīlassa, āvusau, sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi  …pai॰… phaiggupi… sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi  asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, āvusau, sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ  hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rukkhau, sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi…pai॰… phaiggupi… sāraupi  pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti a॰ ni॰ 5.168; 6.50; 7.65| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. khippanisantisuttaṃ

169. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti, kusalaisu dhammaisu suggahitaggāhī ca, bahuñ‍ca gaṇhāti, gahitañ‍cassa nappamussatī’’ti? ‘‘āyasmā khau ānandau bahussutau| paṭibhātu āyasmantaṃyaiva ānanda’’nti| ‘‘tainahāvusau sāriputta, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi  karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu atthakusalau ca hauti, dhammakusalau ca, byañ‍janakusalau ca, niruttikusalau ca, pubbāparakusalau ca| aittāvatā khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, suggahitaggāhī ca, bahuñ‍ca gaṇhāti, gahitañ‍cassa nappamussatī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau! abbhutaṃ, āvusau!! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina| imaihi ca mayaṃ pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dhāraima – ‘āyasmā ānandau atthakusalau dhammakusalau byañ‍janakusalau niruttikusalau pubbāparakusalau’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bhaddajisuttaṃ

170. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā bhaddaji yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ bhaddajiṃ āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau bhaddaji, dassanānaṃ aggaṃ, kiṃ savanānaṃ aggaṃ, kiṃ sukhānaṃ aggaṃ, kiṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ aggaṃ, kiṃ bhavānaṃ agga’’nti?

‘‘atthāvusau, brahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī, yau taṃ brahmānaṃ passati, idaṃ dassanānaṃ aggaṃ| atthāvusau, ābhassarā nāma daivā sukhaina abhisan‍nā parisan‍nā| tai kadāci karahaci udānaṃ udānainti – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’nti! yau taṃ saddaṃ suṇāti, idaṃ savanānaṃ aggaṃ| atthāvusau, subhakiṇhā nāma daivā| tai santaṃyaiva tusitā sukhaṃ paṭivaidainti, idaṃ sukhānaṃ aggaṃ| atthāvusau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā daivā, idaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ  aggaṃ| atthāvusau, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā daivā, idaṃ bhavānaṃ agga’’nti| ‘‘samaiti khau idaṃ āyasmatau bhaddajissa, yadidaṃ bahunā janainā’’ti?

‘‘āyasmā  khau, ānandau, bahussutau| paṭibhātu āyasmantaṃyaiva ānanda’’nti| ‘‘tainahāvusau bhaddaji, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā bhaddaji āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yathā  passatau khau, āvusau, anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, idaṃ dassanānaṃ aggaṃ| yathā suṇatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, idaṃ savanānaṃ aggaṃ| yathā sukhitassa anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, idaṃ sukhānaṃ aggaṃ| yathā sañ‍ñissa anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, idaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ aggaṃ| yathā bhūtassa anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, idaṃ bhavānaṃ agga’’nti| dasamaṃ|

āghātavaggau  dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai āghātavinayā, sākacchā sājīvatau pañhaṃ|

pucchā niraudhau caudanā, sīlaṃ nisanti bhaddajīti||


(18) 3. upāsakavaggau

1. sāraj‍jasuttaṃ

171. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau sāraj‍jaṃ auk‍kantau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau sāraj‍jaṃ auk‍kantau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau visāradau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau visāradau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. visāradasuttaṃ

172. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau avisāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī  hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau avisāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau visāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau visāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nirayasuttaṃ

173. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau  aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vairasuttaṃ

174. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍ca, gahapati, bhayāni vairāni appahāya ‘dussīlau’ iti vuc‍cati, nirayañ‍ca upapaj‍jati| katamāni pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātaṃ, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācāraṃ, musāvādaṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imāni  khau, gahapati, pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni appahāya ‘dussīlau’ iti vuc‍cati, nirayañ‍ca upapaj‍jati|

‘‘pañ‍ca, gahapati, bhayāni vairāni pahāya ‘sīlavā’ iti vuc‍cati, sugatiñ‍ca upapaj‍jati| katamāni pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātaṃ, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācāraṃ, musāvādaṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imāni khau, gahapati, pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni pahāya ‘sīlavā’ iti vuc‍cati, sugatiñ‍ca upapaj‍jati|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na samparāyikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰…|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, kāmaisumicchācārī…pai॰…|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, musāvādī…pai॰…|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na samparāyikaṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hautī’’ti|

‘‘yau  pāṇamatipātaiti, musāvādañ‍ca bhāsati|

laukai adin‍naṃ ādiyati, paradārañ‍ca gacchati|

surāmairayapānañ‍ca, yau narau anuyuñ‍jati||

‘‘appahāya pañ‍ca vairāni, dussīlau iti vuc‍cati|

kāyassa bhaidā duppañ‍ñau, nirayaṃ saupapaj‍jati||

‘‘yau  pāṇaṃ nātipātaiti, musāvādaṃ na bhāsati|

laukai adin‍naṃ nādiyati, paradāraṃ na gacchati|

surāmairayapānañ‍ca, yau narau nānuyuñ‍jati||

‘‘pahāya pañ‍ca vairāni, sīlavā iti vuc‍cati|

kāyassa  bhaidā sappañ‍ñau, sugatiṃ saupapaj‍jatī’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. caṇḍālasuttaṃ

175. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau upāsakacaṇḍālau ca hauti upāsakamalañ‍ca upāsakapatikuṭṭhau ca upāsakapatikiṭṭhau ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau hauti; dussīlau hauti; kautūhalamaṅgalikau hauti, maṅgalaṃ pac‍caiti nau kammaṃ; itau ca bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati; tattha ca pubbakāraṃ karauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau upāsakacaṇḍālau ca hauti upāsakamalañ‍ca upāsakapatikuṭṭhau ca|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau upāsakaratanañ‍ca hauti upāsakapadumañ‍ca upāsakapuṇḍarīkañ‍ca upāsakapuṇḍarīkau ca (pī॰ ka॰)| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau hauti; sīlavā hauti; akautūhalamaṅgalikau hauti, kammaṃ pac‍caiti nau maṅgalaṃ; na itau bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati; idha ca pubbakāraṃ karauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakau upāsakaratanañ‍ca hauti upāsakapadumañ‍ca upāsakapuṇḍarīkañ‍cā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pītisuttaṃ

176. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati pañ‍camattaihi upāsakasataihi parivutau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tumhai khau, gahapati, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| na khau, gahapati, tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhi karaṇīyā – ‘mayaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārainā’ti| tasmātiha, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kinti  mayaṃ kālaina kālaṃ pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāmā’ti! aivañhi vau, gahapati, sikkhitabba’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘tumhai khau, gahapati, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| na khau, gahapati, tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhi karaṇīyā – mayaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraināti| tasmātiha, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – kinti mayaṃ kālaina kālaṃ pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāmāti! aivañhi vau, gahapati, sikkhitabba’nti| yasmiṃ, bhantai, samayai ariyasāvakau pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍cassa ṭhānāni tasmiṃ samayai na haunti| yampissa kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa akusalūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa akusalūpasaṃhitaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yasmiṃ, bhantai, samayai ariyasāvakau pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, imānissa pañ‍ca imāni pañ‍cassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ṭhānāni tasmiṃ samayai na hauntī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta! yasmiṃ, sāriputta, samayai ariyasāvakau  pavivaikaṃ  pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍cassa ṭhānāni tasmiṃ samayai na haunti| yampissa kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa kāmūpasaṃhitaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa akusalūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa akusalūpasaṃhitaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yampissa kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ, tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| yasmiṃ, sāriputta, samayai ariyasāvakau pavivaikaṃ pītiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, imānissa imānaittha (sī॰) pañ‍ca ṭhānāni tasmiṃ samayai na hauntī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vaṇij‍jāsuttaṃ

177. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, vaṇij‍jā upāsakaina akaraṇīyā| katamā pañ‍ca? satthavaṇij‍jā, sattavaṇij‍jā, maṃsavaṇij‍jā, maj‍javaṇij‍jā, visavaṇij‍jā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca vaṇij‍jā upāsakaina akaraṇīyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. rājāsuttaṃ

178. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘ayaṃ purisau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratauti paṭiviratau hautīti (sī॰), paṭiviratau hauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā pāṇātipātā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘ayaṃ purisau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā pāṇātipātā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’ti| api ca, khvassa tathaiva pāpakammaṃ  pavaidainti tathaiva pāpakaṃ kammaṃ pavaidayanti (sī॰), tadaiva pāpakammaṃ pavaidaiti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) – ‘ayaṃ purisau itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā jīvitā vauraupaisīti vauraupaitīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā pāṇātipātahaitu hananti vā bandhanti  vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaunti| api nu tumhaihi aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati sūyissati (sī॰ pī॰) cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘ayaṃ purisau adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā adin‍nādānā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘ayaṃ purisau adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā adin‍nādānā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’ti| api ca khvassa tathaiva pāpakammaṃ pavaidainti – ‘ayaṃ purisau gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyīti ādiyati (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā adin‍nādānahaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ  vā karaunti| api nu tumhaihi aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’ti| api ca khvassa tathaiva pāpakammaṃ pavaidainti – ‘ayaṃ purisau paritthīsu parakumārīsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jīti āpaj‍jati (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā kāmaisumicchācārahaitu hananti  vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaunti| api nu tumhaihi aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘ayaṃ purisau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā  musāvādā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘ayaṃ purisau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā musāvādā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’ti| api ca khvassa tathaiva pāpakammaṃ pavaidainti – ‘ayaṃ purisau gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā musāvādaina atthaṃ pabhañ‍jīti bhañ‍jatīti (sī॰), bhañ‍jati (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhañ‍jīti (pī॰)| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā musāvādahaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaunti| api nu tumhaihi aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ  vā karauntī’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā  paṭiviratauti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇihaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karauntī’ti| api ca khvassa tathaiva pāpakammaṃ pavaidainti – ‘ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuttau itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā jīvitā vauraupaisi vauraupaiti (syā॰); ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuttau gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi ādiyati (sī॰ syā॰); ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuttau paritthīsu parakumārīsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍ji āpaj‍jati (sī॰ syā॰); ayaṃ purisau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuttau gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā musāvādaina atthaṃ pabhañ‍jīti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānahaitu hananti vā bandhanti vā pabbājainti vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaunti| api nu tumhaihi aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. gihisuttaṃ

179. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati pañ‍camattaihi upāsakasataihi parivutau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yaṃ kañ‍ci yaṃ kiñ‍ci (sī॰ pī॰), sāriputta, jānaiyyātha gihiṃ audātavasanaṃ pañ‍casu sikkhāpadaisu saṃvutakammantaṃ catun‍naṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhiṃ akicchalābhiṃ akasiralābhiṃ, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti|

‘‘katamaisu pañ‍casu sikkhāpadaisu saṃvutakammantau hauti? idha, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaisu pañ‍casu sikkhāpadaisu saṃvutakammantau hauti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī? idha, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi, satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayamassa paṭhamau ābhicaitasikau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārau adhigatau hauti avisuddhassa cittassa visuddhiyā apariyaudātassa cittassa pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti  – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| ayamassa dutiyau ābhicaitasikau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārau adhigatau hauti avisuddhassa cittassa visuddhiyā apariyaudātassa cittassa pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| ayamassa tatiyau ābhicaitasikau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārau adhigatau hauti  avisuddhassa cittassa visuddhiyā apariyaudātassa cittassa pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayamassa catutthau ābhicaitasikau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārau adhigatau hauti avisuddhassa cittassa visuddhiyā apariyaudātassa cittassa pariyaudapanāya| imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘yaṃ kañ‍ci, sāriputta, jānaiyyātha gihiṃ audātavasanaṃ – imaisu pañ‍casu sikkhāpadaisu saṃvutakammantaṃ, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhiṃ  akicchalābhiṃ akasiralābhiṃ, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi  khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti|

‘‘nirayaisu bhayaṃ disvā, pāpāni parivaj‍jayai|

ariyadhammaṃ samādāya, paṇḍitau parivaj‍jayai||

‘‘na hiṃsai pāṇabhūtāni, vij‍jamānai parak‍kamai|

musā ca na bhaṇai jānaṃ, adin‍naṃ na parāmasai||

‘‘saihi dāraihi santuṭṭhau, paradārañ‍ca āramai nāramai (sī॰ syā॰)|

mairayaṃ vāruṇiṃ jantu, na pivai cittamauhaniṃ||

‘‘anussaraiyya  sambuddhaṃ, dhammañ‍cānuvitak‍kayai|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (?) abyāpajjhaṃ (ka॰) hitaṃ cittaṃ, daivalaukāya bhāvayai||

‘‘upaṭṭhitai daiyyadhammai, puñ‍ñatthassa jigīsatau jigiṃsatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

santaisu paṭhamaṃ din‍nā, vipulā hauti dakkhiṇā||

‘‘santau havai pavakkhāmi, sāriputta suṇauhi mai|

iti  kaṇhāsu saitāsu, rauhiṇīsu harīsu vā||

‘‘kammāsāsu sarūpāsu, gausu pāraivatāsu vā|

yāsu kāsuci aitāsu, dantau jāyati puṅgavau||

‘‘dhaurayhau balasampan‍nau, kalyāṇajavanik‍kamau|

tamaiva  bhārai yuñ‍janti, nāssa vaṇṇaṃ parikkharai||

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  manussaisu, yasmiṃ kismiñ‍ci jātiyai|

khattiyai brāhmaṇai vaissai, suddai caṇḍālapuk‍kusai||

‘‘yāsu kāsuci aitāsu, dantau jāyati subbatau|

dhammaṭṭhau sīlasampan‍nau, sac‍cavādī hirīmanau||

‘‘pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī|

pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

‘‘pāragū sabbadhammānaṃ, anupādāya nibbutau|

tasmiñ‍ca virajai khaittai, vipulā hauti dakkhiṇā||

‘‘bālā ca avijānantā, dummaidhā assutāvinau|

bahiddhā dadanti dānāni, na hi santai upāsarai||

‘‘yai ca santai upāsanti, sappañ‍ñai dhīrasammatai|

saddhā ca naisaṃ sugatai, mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā||

‘‘daivalaukañ‍ca tai yanti, kulai vā idha jāyarai|

anupubbaina nibbānaṃ, adhigacchanti paṇḍitā’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. gavaisīsuttaṃ

180. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| addasā khau bhagavā addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai mahantaṃ sālavanaṃ; disvāna disvā (sī॰ pī॰) maggā auk‍kamma uk‍kamma (katthaci) yaina taṃ sālavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ sālavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai sitaṃ pātvākāsi|

atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kau  nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau bhagavatau sitassa pātukammāya? na akāraṇaina tathāgatā sitaṃ pātukarauntī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā  ānandau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau bhagavatau sitassa pātukammāya? na akāraṇaina tathāgatā sitaṃ pātukarauntī’’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, ānanda, imasmiṃ padaisai nagaraṃ ahausi iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bahujanaṃ ākiṇṇamanussaṃ| taṃ khau panānanda, nagaraṃ kassapau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau upanissāya vihāsi| kassapassa khau panānanda, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa gavaisī nāma upāsakau ahausi sīlaisu aparipūrakārī| gavaisinā khau, ānanda, upāsakaina pañ‍camattāni upāsakasatāni paṭidaisitāni samādapitāni samādāpitāni (?) ahaisuṃ sīlaisu aparipūrakārinau| atha khau, ānanda, gavaisissa upāsakassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ bahūpakārau bahukārau (katthaci) pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā samādāpaitā (?), ahañ‍camhi sīlaisu aparipūrakārī, imāni ca pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni sīlaisu aparipūrakārinau| ic‍caitaṃ samasamaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci atiraikaṃ; handāhaṃ atiraikāyā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, ānanda, gavaisī upāsakau yaina tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni aitadavauca – ‘aj‍jataggai maṃ āyasmantau sīlaisu paripūrakāriṃ dhāraithā’ti! atha khau, ānanda, taisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayyau khau gavaisī amhākaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ayyau hi nāma gavaisī  sīlaisu paripūrakārī bhavissati| kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰ pī॰) pana maya’nti pana na mayanti (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.159; cūḷava॰ 330; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1020 pāḷiyā saṃsandaitabbaṃ! atha khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni yaina gavaisī upāsakau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā gavaisiṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘aj‍jataggai ayyau gavaisī imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni sīlaisu paripūrakārinau dhāraitū’ti| atha khau, ānanda, gavaisissa upāsakassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā, ahañ‍camhi sīlaisu paripūrakārī, imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni sīlaisu paripūrakārinau| ic‍caitaṃ samasamaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci atiraikaṃ; handāhaṃ atiraikāyā’’’ti!

‘‘atha khau, ānanda, gavaisī upāsakau yaina tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni aitadavauca – ‘aj‍jataggai maṃ āyasmantau brahmacāriṃ dhāraitha ārācāri anācāriṃ (pī॰) virataṃ maithunā gāmadhammā’ti| atha khau, ānanda, taisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ  aitadahausi – ‘ayyau khau gavaisī amhākaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ayyau hi nāma gavaisī brahmacārī bhavissati ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| kimaṅgaṃ pana maya’nti! atha khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni yaina gavaisī upāsakau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā gavaisiṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘aj‍jataggai ayyau gavaisī imānipi pañ‍ca  upāsakasatāni brahmacārinau dhāraitu ārācārinau viratā maithunā gāmadhammā’ti| atha khau, ānanda, gavaisissa upāsakassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ahañ‍camhi sīlaisu paripūrakārī| imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni sīlaisu paripūrakārinau| ahañ‍camhi brahmacārī ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni brahmacārinau ārācārinau viratā maithunā gāmadhammā| ic‍caitaṃ samasamaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci atiraikaṃ; handāhaṃ atiraikāyā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, ānanda, gavaisī upāsakau yaina tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni aitadavauca – ‘aj‍jataggai maṃ āyasmantau aikabhattikaṃ dhāraitha rattūparataṃ virataṃ  vikālabhaujanā’ti| atha khau, ānanda, taisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayyau khau gavaisī bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ayyau hi nāma gavaisī aikabhattikau bhavissati rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| kimaṅgaṃ pana maya’nti! atha khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni yaina gavaisī upāsakau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā gavaisiṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ  – ‘aj‍jataggai ayyau gavaisī imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni aikabhattikai dhāraitu rattūparatai viratai vikālabhaujanā’ti| atha khau, ānanda, gavaisissa upāsakassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ  upāsakasatānaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ahañ‍camhi sīlaisu paripūrakārī| imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni sīlaisu paripūrakārinau| ahañ‍camhi brahmacārī ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni brahmacārinau ārācārinau viratā maithunā gāmadhammā| ahañ‍camhi aikabhattikau rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| imānipi pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni aikabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhaujanā| ic‍caitaṃ samasamaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci atiraikaṃ; handāhaṃ atiraikāyā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, ānanda, gavaisī upāsakau yaina kassapau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kassapaṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’nti| alattha khau, ānanda, gavaisī upāsakau kassapassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panānanda, gavaisī bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panānanda, gavaisī bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausi|

‘‘atha  khau, ānanda, taisa pañ‍can‍naṃ upāsakasatānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayyau khau gavaisī amhākaṃ bahūpakārau pubbaṅgamau samādapaitā| ayyau hi nāma gavaisī kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissati| kimaṅgaṃ pana maya’nti! atha khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni yaina kassapau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā kassapaṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’nti| alabhiṃsu khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca upāsakasatāni kassapassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alabhiṃsu upasampadaṃ|

‘‘atha khau, ānanda, gavaisissa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau imassa anuttarassa vimuttisukhassa nikāmalābhī haumi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| ahau vatimānipi pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni imassa anuttarassa vimuttisukhassa nikāmalābhinau assu akicchalābhinau akasiralābhinau’ti| atha khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni vūpakaṭṭhā bhikkhusatāni aikaikā vūpakaṭṭhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharantā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihariṃsu| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñiṃsu’’|

‘‘iti khau, ānanda, tāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni gavaisīpamukhāni uttaruttari uttaruttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṇītapaṇītaṃ  vāyamamānā anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ sacchākaṃsu| tasmātiha, ānanda, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uttaruttari paṇītapaṇītaṃ vāyamamānā anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ sacchikarissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, ānanda, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

upāsakavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sāraj‍jaṃ  visāradau nirayaṃ, vairaṃ caṇḍālapañ‍camaṃ|

pīti vaṇij‍jā rājānau, gihī caiva gavaisināti||


(19) 4. arañ‍ñavaggau

1. ārañ‍ñikasuttaṃ

181. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikā ārañ‍ñatā (sabbattha) pari॰ 443 passitabbaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau ārañ‍ñikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihīti ārañ‍ñikau hauti, appicchataṃyaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva idamaṭṭhitaṃyaiva (sī॰ pī॰) nissāya ārañ‍ñikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ārañ‍ñikā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ ārañ‍ñikānaṃ yvāyaṃ ārañ‍ñikau appicchataṃyaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya ārañ‍ñikau hauti, ayaṃ  imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ ārañ‍ñikānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau pāmaukkhau (a॰ ni॰ 4.95; 10.91) ca uttamau ca pavarau ca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau, sappimaṇḍau sappimhā sappimaṇḍau (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.662 tattha aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ ārañ‍ñikānaṃ yvāyaṃ ārañ‍ñikau appicchataṃyaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya ārañ‍ñikau hauti, ayaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ ārañ‍ñikānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. cīvarasuttaṃ

182. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlikā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā paṃsukūlikau hauti…pai॰… idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya paṃsukūlikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca paṃsukūlikā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. rukkhamūlikasuttaṃ

183. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlikā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā rukkhamūlikau hauti…pai॰… idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya rukkhamūlikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca rukkhamūlikā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sausānikasuttaṃ

184. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, sausānikā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā sausānikau hauti…pai॰… idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya sausānikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sausānikā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. abbhaukāsikasuttaṃ

185. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, abbhaukāsikā…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. naisaj‍jikasuttaṃ

186. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, naisaj‍jikā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yathāsanthatikasuttaṃ

187. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, yathāsanthatikā…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. aikāsanikasuttaṃ

188. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, aikāsanikā…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. khalupacchābhattikasuttaṃ

189. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, khalupacchābhattikā…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. pattapiṇḍikasuttaṃ

190. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, pattapiṇḍikā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ‘vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihī’ti pattapiṇḍikau hauti, appicchataṃyaiva nissāya  santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya pattapiṇḍikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pattapiṇḍikā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ pattapiṇḍikānaṃ yvāyaṃ pattapiṇḍikau appicchataṃyaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva  nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ayaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ pattapiṇḍikānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau, sappimaṇḍau tattha aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ pattapiṇḍikānaṃ yvāyaṃ pattapiṇḍikau appicchataṃyaiva  nissāya santuṭṭhiṃyaiva nissāya sal‍laikhaṃyaiva nissāya pavivaikaṃyaiva nissāya idamatthitaṃyaiva nissāya pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ayaṃ imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ pattapiṇḍikānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

arañ‍ñavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

arañ‍ñaṃ cīvaraṃ rukkha, susānaṃ abbhaukāsikaṃ|

naisaj‍jaṃ santhataṃ aikāsanikaṃ, khalupacchāpiṇḍikaina cāti||


(20) 5. brāhmaṇavaggau

1. sauṇasuttaṃ

191. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, paurāṇā brāhmaṇadhammā aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissanti, nau brāhmaṇaisu| katamai pañ‍ca? pubbai sudaṃ pubbassudaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇiṃyaiva gacchanti, nau abrāhmaṇiṃ| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇimpi gacchanti, abrāhmaṇimpi gacchanti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, sunakhā sunakhiṃyaiva gacchanti, nau asunakhiṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau paurāṇau brāhmaṇadhammau aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissati, nau brāhmaṇaisu|

‘‘pubbai sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇiṃ utuniṃyaiva gacchanti, nau anutuniṃ| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā  brāhmaṇiṃ utunimpi gacchanti, anutunimpi gacchanti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, sunakhā sunakhiṃ utuniṃyaiva gacchanti, nau anutuniṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau paurāṇau brāhmaṇadhammau aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissati, nau brāhmaṇaisu|

‘‘pubbai sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇiṃ naiva kiṇanti nau vik‍kiṇanti, sampiyainaiva saṃvāsaṃ saṃbandhāya saṃsaggatthāya (sī॰ pī॰) saṃpavattainti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇiṃ kiṇantipi vik‍kiṇantipi, sampiyainapi saṃvāsaṃ saṃbandhāya saṃpavattainti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, sunakhā sunakhiṃ naiva kiṇanti nau vik‍kiṇanti, sampiyainaiva saṃvāsaṃ saṃbandhāya saṃpavattainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau paurāṇau brāhmaṇadhammau aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissati, nau brāhmaṇaisu|

‘‘pubbai sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā na san‍nidhiṃ karaunti dhanassapi  dhañ‍ñassapi rajatassapi jātarūpassapi| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā san‍nidhiṃ karaunti dhanassapi dhañ‍ñassapi rajatassapi jātarūpassapi| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, sunakhā na san‍nidhiṃ karaunti dhanassapi dhañ‍ñassapi rajatassapi jātarūpassapi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau paurāṇau brāhmaṇadhammau aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissati, nau brāhmaṇaisu|

‘‘pubbai  sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya pātau pātarāsāya bhikkhaṃ pariyaisanti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā avasaisaṃ ādāya pak‍kamanti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, sunakhā sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya pātau pātarāsāya bhikkhaṃ pariyaisanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau paurāṇau brāhmaṇadhammau aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissati, nau brāhmaṇaisu| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca paurāṇā brāhmaṇadhammā aitarahi sunakhaisu sandissanti, nau brāhmaṇaisū’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dauṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

192. atha  khau dauṇau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dauṇau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ  maitaṃ, bhau gautama – ‘na samaṇau gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaitī’ti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, tathaiva| na hi bhavaṃ gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā  āsanaina vā nimantaiti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, na sampan‍namaivā’’ti| ‘‘tvampi nau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau paṭijānāsī’’ti? ‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhau gautama, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhitau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau’ti, mamaiva taṃ, bhau gautama, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| ahañhi, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau’’ti|

‘‘yai khau, tai dauṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā  isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau, yaisamidaṃ  aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti sajjhāyitamanusajjhāyanti vācitamanuvācainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau, vāmakau, vāmadaivau, vaissāmittau, yamadaggi yamataggi (sī॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.284, 526, 536; ma॰ ni॰ 2.427; mahāva॰ 300; a॰ ni॰ 5.192 passitabbaṃ, aṅgīrasau, bhāradvājau, vāsaiṭṭhau, kassapau, bhagu; tyāssu’mai pañ‍ca brāhmaṇai pañ‍ñāpainti – brahmasamaṃ, daivasamaṃ, mariyādaṃ, sambhin‍namariyādaṃ, brāhmaṇacaṇḍālaṃyaiva pañ‍camaṃ| taisaṃ tvaṃ dauṇa, katamau’’ti?

‘‘na khau mayaṃ, bhau gautama, pañ‍ca brāhmaṇai jānāma, atha khau mayaṃ brāhmaṇātvaiva  jānāma| sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ahaṃ imai pañ‍ca brāhmaṇai jānaiyya’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇauhi, sādhukaṃ  manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhau’’ti khau dauṇau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau brahmasamau hauti? idha, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| sau aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ kaudhāraṃ brahmacariyaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) carati mantai adhīyamānau| aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā mantai adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina|

‘‘tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva  kasiyā na vaṇij‍jāya na gaurakkhaina na issatthaina na issattaina (ka॰) na rājapaurisaina na sippañ‍ñataraina, kaivalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimañ‍ñamānau| sau ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ niyyādaitvā nīyyādaitvā (sī॰), nīyādaitvā (pī॰), niyyātaitvā (katthaci) kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (sī॰), iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (ka॰) abyābajjhaina (?) pharitvā viharati| karuṇā…pai॰… muditā… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau imai cattārau  brahmavihārai bhāvaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ brahmalaukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau brahmasamau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau daivasamau hauti? idha, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| sau aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ carati mantai adhīyamānau| aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā mantai adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva kasiyā na vaṇij‍jāya na gaurakkhaina na issatthaina na rājapaurisaina na sippañ‍ñataraina, kaivalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ  anatimañ‍ñamānau| sau ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ niyyādaitvā dāraṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina|

‘‘tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva kayaina na vik‍kayaina, brāhmaṇiṃyaiva udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ| sau brāhmaṇiṃyaiva gacchati, na khattiyiṃ na vaissiṃ na suddiṃ na caṇḍāliṃ na naisādiṃ na vainiṃ na vaiṇiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) na rathakāriṃ na puk‍kusiṃ gacchati, na gabbhiniṃ gacchati, na pāyamānaṃ gacchati, na anutuniṃ gacchati| kasmā ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na gabbhiniṃ gacchati? sacai, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau gabbhiniṃ gacchati, atimīḷhajau nāma sau hauti māṇavakau vā māṇavikā māṇavakī (ka॰) vā| tasmā, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na gabbhiniṃ gacchati| kasmā ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na pāyamānaṃ gacchati? sacai, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau pāyamānaṃ gacchati, asucipaṭipīḷitau nāma sau hauti māṇavakau vā māṇavikā vā| tasmā, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na pāyamānaṃ gacchati| tassa sā hauti brāhmaṇī naiva kāmatthā na davatthā na ratatthā, pajatthāva brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hauti| sau maithunaṃ uppādaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imai cattārau jhānai  bhāvaitvā kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau daivasamau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau mariyādau hauti? idha, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| sau aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ carati mantai adhīyamānau| aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā mantai adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva kasiyā na vaṇij‍jāya na gaurakkhaina na issatthaina na rājapaurisaina na sippañ‍ñataraina, kaivalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimañ‍ñamānau| sau ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ niyyādaitvā dāraṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina|

‘‘tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva kayaina na vik‍kayaina, brāhmaṇiṃyaiva udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ| sau brāhmaṇiṃyaiva gacchati, na khattiyiṃ na vaissiṃ na suddiṃ na caṇḍāliṃ na naisādiṃ na vainiṃ na rathakāriṃ na puk‍kusiṃ gacchati, na gabbhiniṃ gacchati, na pāyamānaṃ gacchati, na anutuniṃ gacchati| kasmā ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na gabbhiniṃ gacchati? sacai, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau gabbhiniṃ gacchati, atimīḷhajau nāma sau hauti māṇavakau vā māṇavikā vā| tasmā, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na gabbhiniṃ gacchati| kasmā ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na pāyamānaṃ gacchati? sacai, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau pāyamānaṃ gacchati, asucipaṭipīḷitau nāma sau hauti māṇavakau vā māṇavikā vā| tasmā, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau na pāyamānaṃ gacchati| tassa sā hauti brāhmaṇī naiva kāmatthā na davatthā na ratatthā, pajatthāva brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hauti| sau maithunaṃ uppādaitvā tamaiva puttassādaṃ nikāmayamānau kuṭumbaṃ ajjhāvasati, na agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| yāva paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādau tattha tiṭṭhati, taṃ na vītik‍kamati| ‘yāva paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādau tattha brāhmaṇau ṭhitau taṃ na vītik‍kamatī’ti, khau, dauṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇau mariyādauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau mariyādau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau sambhin‍namariyādau hauti? idha, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau  anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| sau aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ carati mantai adhīyamānau| aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā mantai adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina|

‘‘tattha ca, dauṇa, kau dhammau? naiva kasiyā na vaṇij‍jāya na gaurakkhaina na issatthaina na rājapaurisaina na sippañ‍ñataraina, kaivalaṃ bhikkhācariyāya kapālaṃ anatimañ‍ñamānau| sau ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ niyyādaitvā dāraṃ  pariyaisati dhammainapi adhammainapi kayainapi vik‍kayainapi brāhmaṇimpi udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ| sau brāhmaṇimpi gacchati khattiyimpi gacchati vaissimpi gacchati suddimpi gacchati caṇḍālimpi gacchati naisādimpi gacchati vainimpi gacchati rathakārimpi gacchati puk‍kusimpi gacchati gabbhinimpi gacchati pāyamānampi gacchati utunimpi gacchati anutunimpi gacchati| tassa sā hauti brāhmaṇī kāmatthāpi davatthāpi ratatthāpi pajatthāpi brāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī hauti| yāva paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādau tattha na tiṭṭhati, taṃ vītik‍kamati| ‘yāva paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ mariyādau tattha brāhmaṇau na ṭhitau taṃ vītik‍kamatī’ti khau, dauṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇau sambhin‍namariyādauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau sambhin‍namariyādau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇacaṇḍālau hauti? idha, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau, yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau  anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| sau aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni  kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ carati mantai adhīyamānau| aṭṭhacattālīsavassāni kaumārabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā mantai adhīyitvā ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisati dhammainapi adhammainapi kasiyāpi vaṇij‍jāyapi gaurakkhainapi issatthainapi rājapaurisainapi sippañ‍ñatarainapi, kaivalampi bhikkhācariyāya, kapālaṃ anatimañ‍ñamānau|

‘‘sau ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ niyyādaitvā dāraṃ pariyaisati dhammainapi adhammainapi kayainapi vik‍kayainapi brāhmaṇimpi udakūpassaṭṭhaṃ| sau brāhmaṇimpi gacchati khattiyimpi gacchati vaissimpi gacchati suddimpi gacchati caṇḍālimpi gacchati naisādimpi gacchati vainimpi gacchati rathakārimpi gacchati puk‍kusimpi gacchati gabbhinimpi gacchati pāyamānampi gacchati utunimpi gacchati anutunimpi gacchati| tassa sā hauti brāhmaṇī kāmatthāpi davatthāpi ratatthāpi pajatthāpi brāhmaṇassa  brāhmaṇī hauti| sau sabbakammaihi jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti| tamainaṃ brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kasmā bhavaṃ brāhmaṇau paṭijānamānau sabbakammaihi jīvikaṃ kappaitī’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, aggi sucimpi ḍahati asucimpi ḍahati, na ca taina aggi upalippati upalimpati (ka॰); aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, sabbakammaihi caipi brāhmaṇau jīvikaṃ kappaiti, na ca taina brāhmaṇau upalippati’| ‘sabbakammaihi jīvikaṃ kappaitī’ti khau, dauṇa, tasmā brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇacaṇḍālauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, dauṇa, brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇacaṇḍālau hauti|

‘‘yai khau tai, dauṇa, brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā  isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau yaisamidaṃ aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samīhitaṃ tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti sajjhāyitamanusajjhāyanti vācimanuvācainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau, vāmakau, vāmadaivau, vaissāmittau, yamadaggi, aṅgīrasau, bhāradvājau, vāsaiṭṭhau, kassapau, bhagu; tyāssumai pañ‍ca brāhmaṇai pañ‍ñāpainti – brahmasamaṃ, daivasamaṃ, mariyādaṃ, sambhin‍namariyādaṃ, brāhmaṇacaṇḍālaṃyaiva pañ‍camaṃ| taisaṃ tvaṃ, dauṇa, katamau’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ santai mayaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇacaṇḍālampi na pūraima| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

193. atha khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina kadāci dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina kadāci dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā’’ti?

‘‘yasmiṃ, brāhmaṇa, samayai kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ  samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau saṃsaṭṭhau lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya vā| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ  mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyya  na passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau agginā santattau uk‍kudhitau uk‍kaṭṭhitau (sī॰ pī॰), uk‍kuṭṭhitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ussadakajātau usumakajātau (katthaci), ussurakajātau (ka॰), usmudakajātau (ma॰ ni॰ 3 majjhimanikāyai)| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ  nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau saivālapaṇakapariyaunaddhau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa  nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ  samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau vātairitau calitau bhantau ūmijātau ummijātau (pī॰)| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ  sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau āvilau luḷitau kalalībhūtau andhakārai nikkhittau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti na passati, dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  khau, brāhmaṇa, samayai na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau asaṃsaṭṭhau lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya  vā| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau agginā asantattau anuk‍kudhitau anussadakajātau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰…  saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau na saivālapaṇakapariyaunaddhau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau na vātairitau na calitau na bhantau na ūmijātau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati  …pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi  mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau ālaukai nikkhittau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti passati, dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina kadāci dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| ayaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina kadāci dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kāraṇapālīsuttaṃ

194. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina kāraṇapālī karaṇapālī (ka॰) brāhmaṇau licchavīnaṃ kammantaṃ kāraiti| addasā khau kāraṇapālī brāhmaṇau piṅgiyāniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dūratauva  āgacchantaṃ; disvā piṅgiyāniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘handa, kutau nu bhavaṃ piṅgiyānī āgacchati divā divassā’’ti? ‘‘itauhaṃ idhāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), itau hi khau ahaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 1.288), bhau, āgacchāmi samaṇassa gautamassa santikā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñati bhavaṃ, piṅgiyānī, samaṇassa  gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ? paṇḍitau mañ‍ñai’’ti? ‘‘kau cāhaṃ, bhau, kau ca samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ jānissāmi! saupi nūnassa tādisauva yau samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ jānaiyyā’’ti! ‘‘uḷārāya khalu bhavaṃ, piṅgiyānī, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pasaṃsāya pasaṃsatī’’ti| ‘‘kau cāhaṃ, bhau, kau ca samaṇaṃ  gautamaṃ pasaṃsissāmi! pasatthappasatthauva pasaṭṭhapasaṭṭhau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sau bhavaṃ gautamau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana bhavaṃ, piṅgiyānī, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau samaṇai gautamai aivaṃ abhippasan‍nau’’ti?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, purisau aggarasaparitittau na añ‍ñaisaṃ hīnānaṃ rasānaṃ pihaiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, yatau yatau tassa bhautau gautamassa  dhammaṃ suṇāti – yadi suttasau, yadi gaiyyasau, yadi vaiyyākaraṇasau, yadi abbhutadhammasau – tatau tatau na añ‍ñaisaṃ puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇappavādānaṃ pihaiti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, purisau jighacchādubbalyaparaitau madhupiṇḍikaṃ adhigacchaiyya| sau yatau yatau sāyaitha, labhataiva sāyaiyya, labhaithaiva (ma॰ ni॰ 1.205) sādurasaṃ asaicanakaṃ; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, yatau yatau tassa bhautau gautamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti – yadi suttasau, yadi gaiyyasau, yadi vaiyyākaraṇasau, yadi abbhutadhammasau – tatau tatau labhataiva attamanataṃ, labhati caitasau pasādaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, purisau candanaghaṭikaṃ adhigacchaiyya – haricandanassa vā lauhitacandanassa vā| sau yatau yatau ghāyaitha – yadi mūlatau, yadi majjhatau, yadi aggatau – adhigacchataiva  adhigacchaithaiva (?) surabhigandhaṃ asaicanakaṃ; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, yatau yatau tassa bhautau gautamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti – yadi suttasau, yadi gaiyyasau, yadi vaiyyākaraṇasau, yadi abbhutadhammasau – tatau tatau adhigacchati pāmauj‍jaṃ adhigacchati saumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, purisau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| tassa kusalau bhisak‍kau ṭhānasau ābādhaṃ nīharaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, yatau yatau tassa bhautau gautamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti – yadi suttasau, yadi gaiyyasau, yadi vaiyyākaraṇasau, yadi abbhutadhammasau – tatau tatau saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, paukkharaṇī acchaudakā sātaudakā sītaudakā saitakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| sau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā nhātvā ca pivitvā ca sabbadarathakilamathapariḷāhaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhau, yatau yatau tassa bhautau gautamassa dhammaṃ suṇāti – yadi suttasau, yadi gaiyyasau, yadi vaiyyākaraṇasau, yadi abbhutadhammasau – tatau tatau sabbadarathakilamathapariḷāhā paṭippassambhantī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai kāraṇapālī brāhmaṇau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇaṃ jāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ nihantvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti||

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau piṅgiyāni, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau piṅgiyāni! saiyyathāpi, bhau piṅgiyāni, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ nikuj‍jitaṃ (ka॰) vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya andhakārai  vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā piṅgiyāninā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau piṅgiyāni, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ piṅgiyānī dhāraitu, aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. piṅgiyānīsuttaṃ

195. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattāni licchavisatāni bhagavantaṃ payirupāsanti| appaikac‍cai licchavī nīlā haunti nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī pītā haunti pītavaṇṇā pītavatthā pītālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī lauhitakā haunti lauhitakavaṇṇā lauhitakavatthā lauhitakālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī audātā  haunti audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā| tyassudaṃ bhagavā atiraucati vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā ca|

atha  khau piṅgiyānī brāhmaṇau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ, sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ piṅgiyānī’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau piṅgiyānī brāhmaṇau bhagavatau sammukhā sāruppāya gāthāya abhitthavi –

‘‘padmaṃ padumaṃ (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.132 yathā kaukanadaṃ kaukanudaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sugandhaṃ,

pātau siyā phul‍lamavītagandhaṃ|

aṅgīrasaṃ passa viraucamānaṃ,

tapantamādic‍camivantalikkhai’’ti||

atha khau tai licchavī pañ‍cahi uttarāsaṅgasataihi piṅgiyāniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ acchādaisuṃ| atha khau piṅgiyānī brāhmaṇau taihi pañ‍cahi uttarāsaṅgasataihi bhagavantaṃ acchādaisi|

atha khau bhagavā tai licchavī aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ, licchavī, ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisaitā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisitassa viñ‍ñātā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisitassa viñ‍ñātā  dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katañ‍ñū katavaidī puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, licchavī, pañ‍can‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’nti a॰ ni॰ 5.143| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahāsupinasuttaṃ

196. ‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau pañ‍ca mahāsupinā pāturahaisuṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau  ayaṃ mahāpathavī mahāsayanaṃ ahausi, himavā pabbatarājā bibbauhanaṃ bibbauhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bimba + auhanaṃ = iti padavibhāgau ahausi, puratthimai samuddai vāmau hatthau auhitau ahausi, pacchimai samuddai dakkhiṇau hatthau auhitau  ahausi, dakkhiṇai samuddai ubhau pādā auhitā ahaisuṃ| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ paṭhamau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau tiriyā nāma tiṇajāti nābhiyā uggantvā nabhaṃ āhac‍ca ṭhitā ahausi| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ dutiyau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau saitā kimī kaṇhasīsā pādaihi ussak‍kitvā ( ) (agganakhatau) katthaci dissati yāva jāṇumaṇḍalā paṭicchādaisuṃ| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau  sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ tatiyau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau cattārau sakuṇā nānāvaṇṇā catūhi disāhi āgantvā pādamūlai nipatitvā sabbasaitā sampaj‍jiṃsu| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ catutthau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau mahatau mīḷhapabbatassa uparūpari caṅkamati alippamānau mīḷhaina| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ pañ‍camau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau ayaṃ mahāpathavī mahāsayanaṃ ahausi, himavā pabbatarājā bibbauhanaṃ ahausi, puratthimai samuddai vāmau hatthau auhitau ahausi, pacchimai samuddai dakkhiṇau hatthau auhitau ahausi, dakkhiṇai samuddai ubhau pādā auhitā ahaisuṃ; tathāgataina, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina anuttarā sammāsambaudhi abhisambuddhā| tassā abhisambaudhāya ayaṃ paṭhamau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau tiriyā nāma  tiṇajāti nābhiyā uggantvā nabhaṃ āhac‍ca ṭhitā ahausi; tathāgataina, bhikkhavai, arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau abhisambujjhitvā yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitau| tassa abhisambaudhāya ayaṃ dutiyau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau saitā kimī kaṇhasīsā pādaihi ussak‍kitvā yāva jāṇumaṇḍalā paṭicchādaisuṃ; bahū, bhikkhavai, gihī audātavasanā tathāgataṃ pāṇupaitā pāṇupaitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saraṇaṃ gatā| tassa abhisambaudhāya ayaṃ tatiyau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau cattārau sakuṇā nānāvaṇṇā catūhi disāhi āgantvā pādamūlai nipatitvā sabbasaitā sampaj‍jiṃsu; cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, vaṇṇā khattiyā brāhmaṇā vaissā suddā tai tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ sacchikaraunti| tassa abhisambaudhāya ayaṃ catutthau mahāsupinau pāturahausi|

‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau mahatau mīḷhapabbatassa uparūpari caṅkamati  alippamānau mīḷhaina; lābhī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ, taṃ tattha ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tathāgatau agathitau agadhitau (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau anajjhāpan‍nau (ka॰) anajjhaupan‍nau (sī॰ syā॰) ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| tassa abhisambaudhāya ayaṃ pañ‍camau mahāsupinau  pāturahausi|

‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau imai pañ‍ca mahāsupinā pāturahaisu’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vassasuttaṃ

197. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, vassassa antarāyā, yaṃ naimittā naimittakā (katthaci) na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati| katamai pañ‍ca? upari, bhikkhavai, ākāsai taijaudhātu pakuppati| taina uppan‍nā maighā paṭivigacchanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau vassassa antarāyau, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, upari ākāsai vāyaudhātu pakuppati| taina uppan‍nā maighā paṭivigacchanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau vassassa antarāyau, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rāhu asurindau pāṇinā udakaṃ sampaṭicchitvā mahāsamuddai chaḍḍaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau vassassa antarāyau, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, vassavalāhakā daivā pamattā haunti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau vassassa antarāyau, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussā adhammikā haunti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau vassassa antarāyau, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca vassassa antarāyā, yaṃ naimittā na jānanti, yattha naimittānaṃ cakkhu na kamatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. vācāsuttaṃ

198. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā vācā subhāsitā hauti, nau dubbhāsitā, anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ ananuvaj‍jā viñ‍ñūnaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kālaina  ca bhāsitā hauti, sac‍cā ca bhāsitā hauti, saṇhā ca bhāsitā hauti, atthasaṃhitā ca bhāsitā hauti, maittacittaina ca bhāsitā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā  vācā subhāsitā hauti, nau dubbhāsitā, anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūna’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kulasuttaṃ

199. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavantau pabbajitā kulaṃ upasaṅkamanti, tattha manussā pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai sīlavantai pabbajitai kulaṃ upasaṅkamantai manussā disvā cittāni pasādainti pasīdanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), saggasaṃvattanikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai sīlavantai pabbajitai kulaṃ upasaṅkamantai manussā pac‍cuṭṭhainti abhivādainti āsanaṃ dainti, uc‍cākulīnasaṃvattanikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai sīlavantai pabbajitai kulaṃ upasaṅkamantai manussā macchairamalaṃ paṭivinainti paṭivinaudainti (sī॰ pī॰), mahaisakkhasaṃvattanikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai sīlavantai pabbajitai kulaṃ upasaṅkamantai manussā yathāsatti yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajanti, mahābhaugasaṃvattanikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai sīlavantai pabbajitai kulaṃ upasaṅkamantai manussā paripucchanti paripañhanti dhammaṃ suṇanti, mahāpañ‍ñāsaṃvattanikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ kulaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ hauti| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavantau pabbajitā kulaṃ upasaṅkamanti, tattha manussā imaihi pañ‍cahi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nissāraṇīyasuttaṃ

200. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, nissāraṇīyā nissaraṇīyā (pī॰), nissaraṇiyā (dī॰ ni॰ 3.321) dhātuyau| katamā pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau  kāmaṃ kāmai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.321 manasikarautau kāmaisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| naikkhammaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ sukataṃ (pī॰ ka॰) subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ (katthaci, dī॰ ni॰ 3.321) kāmaihi; yai ca kāmapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati| idamakkhātaṃ kāmānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau byāpādaṃ manasikarautau byāpādai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| abyāpādaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau abyāpādai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ byāpādaina; yai ca byāpādapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati| idamakkhātaṃ byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vihaisaṃ manasikarautau  vihaisāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| avihaisaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau avihaisāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ vihaisāya; yai ca vihaisāpac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati| idamakkhātaṃ vihaisāya nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rūpaṃ manasikarautau rūpai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| arūpaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau arūpai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ rūpaihi; yai ca rūpapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati| idamakkhātaṃ rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sak‍kāyaṃ manasikarautau sak‍kāyai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau  sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ suvisaṃyuttaṃ sak‍kāyaina; yai ca sak‍kāyapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati| idamakkhātaṃ sak‍kāyassa nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tassa kāmanandīpi nānusaiti, byāpādanandīpi nānusaiti, vihaisānandīpi nānusaiti, rūpanandīpi nānusaiti, sak‍kāyanandīpi nānusaiti (sau) ( ) katthaci natthi kāmanandiyāpi ananusayā, byāpādanandiyāpi ananusayā, vihaisānandiyāpi ananusayā, rūpanandiyāpi ananusayā, sak‍kāyanandiyāpi ananusayā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niranusayau, acchaicchi acchaij‍ji (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) taṇhaṃ, vivattayi vāvattayi (sī॰) saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassa| imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

brāhmaṇavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sauṇau  dauṇau saṅgāravau, kāraṇapālī ca piṅgiyānī|

supinā ca vassā vācā, kulaṃ nissāraṇīyaina cāti||

catutthaṃpaṇṇāsakaṃ samattau|



5. pañ‍camapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(21) 1. kimilavaggau

1. kimilasuttaṃ
