201. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kimilāyaṃ kimbilāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.40; 7.59 viharati vaiḷuvanai| atha khau āyasmā kimilau kimbilau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhammai agāravā viharanti appatissā, saṅghai agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ agāravā viharanti appatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhammai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sagāravā viharanti sappatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau  ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dhammassavanasuttaṃ

202. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā dhammassavanai| katamai pañ‍ca? assutaṃ suṇāti, sutaṃ pariyaudāpaiti, kaṅkhaṃ vitarati vihanati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), diṭṭhiṃ ujuṃ karauti, cittamassa pasīdati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā dhammassavanai’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. assājānīyasuttaṃ

203. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau bhaddau (pī॰) assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? aj‍javaina, javaina, maddavaina, khantiyā, saurac‍caina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? aj‍javaina, javaina, maddavaina, khantiyā, saurac‍caina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. balasuttaṃ

204. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. caitaukhilasuttaṃ

205. a॰ ni॰ 9.71; ma॰ ni॰ 1.185; dī॰ ni॰ 3.319 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitaukhilā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati  na sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya  sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati…pai॰… saṅghai kaṅkhati…pai॰… sikkhāya kaṅkhati…pai॰… sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti  anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caitaukhilā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vinibandhasuttaṃ

206. a॰ ni॰ 9.72; dī॰ ni॰ 3.320 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitasauvinibandhā caitauvinibaddhā (sāratthadīpanīṭīkāyaṃ)| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau avigatarāgau (ka॰) hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai avītarāgau  hauti…pai॰… yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya  brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yāgusuttaṃ

207. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā yāguyā| katamai pañ‍ca? khuddaṃ khudaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) khudhāti sak‍katānulaumaṃ| mahāva॰ 282 paṭihanati, pipāsaṃ paṭivinaiti, vātaṃ anulaumaiti, vatthiṃ saudhaiti, āmāvasaisaṃ  pācaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā yāguyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dantakaṭṭhasuttaṃ

208. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādanai| katamai pañ‍ca? acakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ duggandhaṃ hauti, rasaharaṇiyau na visujjhanti, pittaṃ saimhaṃ bhattaṃ pariyaunandhati pariyaunaddhanti (sī॰ pī॰), pariyaunaddhati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) cūḷava॰ 282 passitabbaṃ, bhattamassa nacchādaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādanai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādanai| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ  na duggandhaṃ hauti, rasaharaṇiyau visujjhanti, pittaṃ saimhaṃ bhattaṃ na pariyaunandhati, bhattamassa chādaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādanai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. gītassarasuttaṃ

209. cūḷava॰ 249 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ bhaṇantassa| katamai pañ‍ca? attanāpi tasmiṃ sarai sāraj‍jati, paraipi tasmiṃ sarai sāraj‍janti, gahapatikāpi ujjhāyanti – ‘yathaiva mayaṃ gāyāma, aivamaivaṃ khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā gāyantī’ti, sarakuttimpi nikāmayamānassa samādhissa bhaṅgau hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ bhaṇantassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. muṭṭhassatisuttaṃ

210. mahāva॰ 353 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamayatau| katamai pañ‍ca? dukkhaṃ supati, dukkhaṃ paṭibujjhati, pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā na rakkhanti, asuci muc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamayatau|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamayatau| katamai pañ‍ca? sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā rakkhanti, asuci na muc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamayatau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kimilavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kimilau  dhammassavanaṃ, ājānīyau balaṃ khilaṃ|

vinibandhaṃ yāgu kaṭṭhaṃ, gītaṃ muṭṭhassatinā cāti||


(22) 2. ak‍kausakavaggau

1. ak‍kausakasuttaṃ

211. ‘‘yau  sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ariyūpavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, tassa pañ‍ca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai pañ‍ca? pārājikau vā hauti chin‍naparipanthau chin‍naparibandhau (ka॰), añ‍ñataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, bāḷhaṃ vā raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ariyūpavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, tassa imai pañ‍ca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bhaṇḍanakārakasuttaṃ

212. ‘‘yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, tassa pañ‍ca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai pañ‍ca? anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati, adhigatā adhigataṃ (sabbattha) parihāyati, pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, tassa imai pañ‍ca ādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sīlasuttaṃ

213. mahāva॰ 209; dī॰ ni॰ 3.316 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau pamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahatiṃ bhaugajāniṃ nigacchati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ, yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ, yadi gahapatiparisaṃ, yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – avisāradau upasaṅkamati maṅkubhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau appamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati – yadi khattiyaparisaṃ, yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ, yadi gahapatiparisaṃ, yadi samaṇaparisaṃ – visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| ayaṃ bhikkhavai, tatiyau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā sīlavatau sīlasampadāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bahubhāṇisuttaṃ

214. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā bahubhāṇismiṃ puggalai| katamai pañ‍ca? musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphappalāpaṃ bhaṇati, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā bahubhāṇismiṃ puggalai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā mantabhāṇismiṃ puggalai| katamai pañ‍ca? na musā bhaṇati, na pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, na pharusaṃ bhaṇati, na samphappalāpaṃ bhaṇati, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā mantabhāṇismiṃ puggalai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaakkhantisuttaṃ

215. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā akkhantiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? bahunau janassa appiyau hauti amanāpau, vairabahulau ca hauti, vaj‍jabahulau ca, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ  duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā akkhantiyā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā khantiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? bahunau janassa piyau hauti manāpau, na vairabahulau hauti, na vaj‍jabahulau, asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā khantiyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaakkhantisuttaṃ

216. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā akkhantiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? bahunau janassa appiyau hauti amanāpau, luddau ca hauti, vippaṭisārī ca, sammūḷhau  kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā akkhantiyā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā khantiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? bahunau janassa piyau hauti manāpau, aluddau ca hauti, avippaṭisārī ca, asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā khantiyā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaapāsādikasuttaṃ

217. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā apāsādikai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ upavadati, anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū garahanti, pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā apāsādikai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā pāsādikai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā pāsādikai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaapāsādikasuttaṃ

218. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā apāsādikai| katamai pañ‍ca? appasan‍nā  nappasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ hauti, satthusāsanaṃ akataṃ na kataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) hauti, pacchimā janatā  diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, cittamassa nappasīdati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā apāsādikai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā pāsādikai| katamai pañ‍ca? appasan‍nā pasīdanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca bhiyyaubhāvau hauti, satthusāsanaṃ kataṃ hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, cittamassa pasīdati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā pāsādikai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aggisuttaṃ

219. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā aggismiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? acakkhussau, dubbaṇṇakaraṇau, dubbalakaraṇau, saṅgaṇikāpavaḍḍhanau saṅgaṇikāpavaddhanau (sī॰), saṅgaṇikārāmabaddhanau (ka॰), tiracchānakathāpavattanikau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā aggismi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. madhurāsuttaṃ

220. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā madhurāyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? visamā, bahurajā, caṇḍasunakhā, vāḷayakkhā, dul‍labhapiṇḍā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā madhurāya’’nti| dasamaṃ|

ak‍kausakavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ak‍kausabhaṇḍanasīlaṃ, bahubhāṇī dvai akhantiyau|

apāsādikā dvai vuttā, aggismiṃ madhuraina cāti||


(23) 3. dīghacārikavaggau

1. paṭhamadīghacārikasuttaṃ

221. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharatau| katamai pañ‍ca? assutaṃ na suṇāti, sutaṃ na pariyaudāpaiti, sutainaikac‍caina avisāradau hauti, gāḷhaṃ bāḷhaṃ (syā॰) raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, na ca mittavā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharatau|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā samavatthacārai| katamai pañ‍ca? assutaṃ suṇāti, sutaṃ pariyaudāpaiti, sutainaikac‍caina visāradau hauti, na gāḷhaṃ raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, mittavā ca hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā samavatthacārai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyadīghacārikasuttaṃ

222. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharatau| katamai pañ‍ca? anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati, adhigatā parihāyati, adhigatainaikac‍caina avisāradau hauti, gāḷhaṃ raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, na ca mittavā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dīghacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ anuyuttassa viharatau|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā  samavatthacārai| katamai pañ‍ca? anadhigataṃ adhigacchati, adhigatā na parihāyati, adhigatainaikac‍caina visāradau hauti, na gāḷhaṃ raugātaṅkaṃ phusati, mittavā ca hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā samavatthacārai’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. atinivāsasuttaṃ

223. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā atinivāsai| katamai pañ‍ca? bahubhaṇḍau hauti bahubhaṇḍasan‍nicayau, bahubhaisaj‍jau hauti bahubhaisaj‍jasan‍nicayau, bahukic‍cau hauti bahukaraṇīyau byattau kiṃkaraṇīyaisu, saṃsaṭṭhau viharati gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi ananulaumikaina gihisaṃsaggaina, tamhā ca āvāsā pak‍kamantau sāpaikkhau pak‍kamati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā atinivāsai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā samavatthavāsai| katamai pañ‍ca? na bahubhaṇḍau hauti na bahubhaṇḍasan‍nicayau, na bahubhaisaj‍jau hauti na bahubhaisaj‍jasan‍nicayau, na bahukic‍cau hauti na bahukaraṇīyau na byattau kiṃkaraṇīyaisu, asaṃsaṭṭhau viharati gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi ananulaumikaina gihisaṃsaggaina, tamhā ca āvāsā pak‍kamantau anapaikkhau pak‍kamati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā samavatthavāsai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. maccharīsuttaṃ

224. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā atinivāsai| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamaccharī hauti, kulamaccharī hauti, lābhamaccharī hauti, vaṇṇamaccharī hauti, dhammamaccharī hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā atinivāsai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā samavatthavāsai| katamai pañ‍ca? na  āvāsamaccharī hauti, na kulamaccharī hauti, na lābhamaccharī hauti, na vaṇṇamaccharī hauti, na dhammamaccharī hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā samavatthavāsai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamakulūpakasuttaṃ

225. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kulūpakai kulupakai (syā॰ pī॰), kulūpagai (sī॰)| katamai pañ‍ca? anāmantacārai  āpaj‍jati, rahau nisaj‍jāya āpaj‍jati, paṭicchan‍nai āsanai āpaj‍jati, mātugāmassa uttari chappañ‍cavācāhi  dhammaṃ daisaintau āpaj‍jati, kāmasaṅkappabahulau viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā kulūpakai’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyakulūpakasuttaṃ

226. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kulūpakassa bhikkhunau ativailaṃ kulaisu saṃsaṭṭhassa viharatau| katamai pañ‍ca? mātugāmassa abhiṇhadassanaṃ, dassanai sati saṃsaggau, saṃsaggai sati vissāsau, vissāsai sati autārau, autiṇṇacittassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘anabhiratau vā brahmacariyaṃ carissati añ‍ñataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jissati sikkhaṃ vā pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissati’| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā kulūpakassa bhikkhunau ativailaṃ kulaisu saṃsaṭṭhassa viharatau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bhaugasuttaṃ

227. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā bhaugaisu| katamai pañ‍ca? aggisādhāraṇā bhaugā, udakasādhāraṇā bhaugā, rājasādhāraṇā bhaugā, caurasādhāraṇā bhaugā, appiyaihi dāyādaihi sādhāraṇā bhaugā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā bhaugaisu|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā bhaugaisu| katamai pañ‍ca? bhaugai nissāya attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā  sukhaṃ pariharati, mātāpitarau sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisai sukhaiti  pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, mittāmac‍cai sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā bhaugaisū’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ussūrabhattasuttaṃ

228. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā ussūrabhattai kulai| katamai pañ‍ca? yai tai atithī pāhunā, tai na kālaina  paṭipūjainti; yā tā balipaṭiggāhikā daivatā, tā na kālaina paṭipūjainti; yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhaujanā, tai na kālaina paṭipūjainti; dāsakammakarapaurisā vimukhā kammaṃ karaunti; tāvatakaṃyaiva asamayaina bhuttaṃ anaujavantaṃ hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā ussūrabhattai kulai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā samayabhattai kulai| katamai pañ‍ca? yai tai atithī pāhunā, tai kālaina paṭipūjainti; yā tā balipaṭiggāhikā daivatā, tā kālaina paṭipūjainti; yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhaujanā, tai kālaina paṭipūjainti; dāsakammakarapaurisā avimukhā kammaṃ karaunti; tāvatakaṃyaiva samayaina bhuttaṃ aujavantaṃ hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā samayabhattai kulai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamakaṇhasappasuttaṃ

229. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kaṇhasappai| katamai pañ‍ca? asuci, duggandhau, sabhīru, sappaṭibhayau, mittadubbhī – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā kaṇhasappai| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai ādīnavā mātugāmai| katamai pañ‍ca? asuci, duggandhau, sabhīru, sappaṭibhayau, mittadubbhī – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā mātugāmai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyakaṇhasappasuttaṃ

230. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kaṇhasappai| katamai pañ‍ca? kaudhanau, upanāhī, ghauravisau, duj‍jivhau, mittadubbhī – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā kaṇhasappai|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai ādīnavā mātugāmai| katamai pañ‍ca? kaudhanau, upanāhī, ghauravisau, duj‍jivhau, mittadubbhī| tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa ghauravisatā – yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau tibbarāgau| tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa duj‍jivhatā – yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pisuṇavācau| tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa mittadubbhitā – yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau aticārinī| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā mātugāmai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dīghacārikavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai dīghacārikā vuttā, atinivāsamaccharī|

dvai ca kulūpakā bhaugā, bhattaṃ sappāparai duvaiti||


(24) 4. āvāsikavaggau

1. āvāsikasuttaṃ

231. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu abhāvanīyau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ākappasampan‍nau hauti na vattasampan‍nau; na bahussutau hauti na sutadharau; na paṭisal‍laikhitā sal‍laikhitā (ka॰ sī॰) hauti na paṭisal‍lānārāmau; na kalyāṇavācau hauti na kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau; duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau aiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu abhāvanīyau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu bhāvanīyau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ākappasampan‍nau  hauti vattasampan‍nau; bahussutau hauti sutadharau; paṭisal‍laikhitā hauti paṭisal‍lānārāmau; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau; pañ‍ñavā hauti ajaḷau  anaiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu bhāvanīyau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. piyasuttaṃ

232. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ  parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saubhanasuttaṃ

233. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu āvāsaṃ saubhaiti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sīlavā  hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; paṭibalau hauti upasaṅkamantai dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu āvāsaṃ saubhaitī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bahūpakārasuttaṃ

234. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu āvāsassa  bahūpakārau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; khaṇḍaphul‍laṃ paṭisaṅkharauti; mahā khau pana bhikkhusaṅghau abhik‍kantau nānāvairaj‍jakā bhikkhū gihīnaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āraucaiti – ‘mahā khau, āvusau, bhikkhusaṅghau abhik‍kantau nānāvairaj‍jakā bhikkhū, karautha puñ‍ñāni, samayau puñ‍ñāni kātu’nti; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu āvāsassa bahūpakārau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. anukampasuttaṃ

235. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu gihīnaṃ gihiṃ (syā॰), gihī (katthaci) anukampati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhisīlai adhisīlaisu (syā॰) samādapaiti; dhammadassanai nivaisaiti; gilānakai upasaṅkamitvā satiṃ uppādaiti – ‘arahaggataṃ āyasmantau  satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaithā’ti; mahā khau pana bhikkhusaṅghau abhik‍kantau nānāvairaj‍jakā bhikkhū gihīnaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āraucaiti – ‘mahā khau, āvusau, bhikkhusaṅghau abhik‍kantau nānāvairaj‍jakā bhikkhū, karautha puñ‍ñāni, samayau puñ‍ñāni kātu’nti; yaṃ khau panassa bhaujanaṃ dainti lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā taṃ attanā paribhuñ‍jati, saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu gihīnaṃ anukampatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaavaṇṇārahasuttaṃ

236. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti; ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā  pasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti; saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti; anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti; saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaavaṇṇārahasuttaṃ

237. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; āvāsamaccharī hauti āvāsapaligaidhī; kulamaccharī hauti kulapaligaidhī; saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; na āvāsamaccharī hauti na āvāsapaligaidhī; na kulamaccharī hauti na kulapaligaidhī; saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. tatiyaavaṇṇārahasuttaṃ

238. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā  avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; āvāsamaccharī hauti; kulamaccharī hauti; lābhamaccharī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; na āvāsamaccharī hauti; na kulamaccharī hauti; na lābhamaccharī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamamacchariyasuttaṃ

239. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āvāsamaccharī hauti; kulamaccharī hauti; lābhamaccharī hauti; vaṇṇamaccharī dhammamaccharī (ka॰) hauti; saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na āvāsamaccharī hauti; na kulamaccharī hauti; na lābhamaccharī hauti; na vaṇṇamaccharī hauti; saddhādaiyyaṃ na vinipātaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyamacchariyasuttaṃ

240. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āvāsamaccharī hauti; kulamaccharī hauti; lābhamaccharī hauti; vaṇṇamaccharī hauti; dhammamaccharī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na āvāsamaccharī hauti; na kulamaccharī hauti; na lābhamaccharī  hauti; na vaṇṇamaccharī hauti; na dhammamaccharī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

āvāsikavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

āvāsikau  piyau ca saubhanau,

bahūpakārau anukampakau ca|

tayau avaṇṇārahā caiva,

macchariyā duvaipi cāti yathābhataṃ cāpi avaṇṇagaidhā, catuk‍kamacchaira pañ‍camaina cāti (sī॰ syā॰) yathābhataṃ avaṇṇañ‍ca, catukau macchariyaina cāti (ka॰)||


(25) 5. duc‍caritavaggau

1. paṭhamaduc‍caritasuttaṃ

241. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā duc‍caritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū garahanti; pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti; kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā duc‍caritai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā sucaritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ na upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti; kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti; kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā sucaritai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamakāyaduc‍caritasuttaṃ

242. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kāyaduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā kāyasucaritai…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamavacīduc‍caritasuttaṃ

243. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā vacīduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā vacīsucaritai…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamamanauduc‍caritasuttaṃ

244. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā manauduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā manausucaritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi  attānaṃ na upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū  pasaṃsanti; kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti; kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā manausucaritai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaduc‍caritasuttaṃ

245. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā duc‍caritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū garahanti; pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; saddhammā vuṭṭhāti; asaddhammai patiṭṭhāti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā duc‍caritai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā sucaritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ na upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti; kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; asaddhammā vuṭṭhāti; saddhammai patiṭṭhāti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā sucaritai’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyakāyaduc‍caritasuttaṃ

246. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā kāyaduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā kāyasucaritai…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyavacīduc‍caritasuttaṃ

247. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā vacīduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā vacīsucaritai…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyamanauduc‍caritasuttaṃ

248. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā manauduc‍caritai…pai॰… ānisaṃsā manausucaritai| katamai pañ‍ca? attāpi attānaṃ  na upavadati; anuvic‍ca viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti; kalyāṇau  kittisaddau abbhuggacchati; asaddhammā vuṭṭhāti; saddhammai patiṭṭhāti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā manausucaritai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sivathikasuttaṃ

249. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā sivathikāya sīvathikāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| katamai pañ‍ca? asuci, duggandhā, sappaṭibhayā, vāḷānaṃ amanussānaṃ āvāsau, bahunau janassa āraudanā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā sivathikāya|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai ādīnavā sivathikūpamai puggalai| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| idamassa asucitāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, sivathikā asuci; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘tassa asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatassa, asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatassa, asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgatassa pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| idamassa duggandhatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, sivathikā duggandhā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘tamainaṃ asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgataṃ, asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgataṃ, asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgataṃ paisalā sabrahmacārī ārakā parivaj‍janti| idamassa sappaṭibhayasmiṃ vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, sivathikā sappaṭibhayā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘sau asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau, asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau, asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau sabhāgaihi puggalaihi saddhiṃ saṃvasati| idamassa vāḷāvāsasmiṃ  vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, sivathikā vāḷānaṃ amanussānaṃ āvāsau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘tamainaṃ asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgataṃ, asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgataṃ, asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgataṃ paisalā sabrahmacārī disvā khīyadhammaṃ khīyanadhammaṃ (sī॰) āpaj‍janti – ‘ahau vata nau dukkhaṃ yai mayaṃ aivarūpaihi puggalaihi saddhiṃ saṃvasāmā’ti! idamassa āraudanāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, sivathikā bahunau janassa āraudanā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā sivathikūpamai puggalai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. puggalappasādasuttaṃ

250. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā puggalappasādai| katamai pañ‍ca? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai puggalau abhippasan‍nau hauti, sau tathārūpaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti yathārūpāya āpattiyā saṅghau ukkhipati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau piyau manāpau sau saṅghaina ukkhittau’ti| bhikkhūsu appasādabahulau hauti| bhikkhūsu appasādabahulau samānau añ‍ñai bhikkhū na bhajati| añ‍ñai bhikkhū abhajantau saddhammaṃ na suṇāti| saddhammaṃ asuṇantau saddhammā parihāyati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau ādīnavau puggalappasādai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ puggalai puggalau abhippasan‍nau hauti, sau tathārūpaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti yathārūpāya āpattiyā saṅghau antai nisīdāpaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau piyau manāpau sau saṅghaina antai nisīdāpitau’ti| bhikkhūsu appasādabahulau hauti| bhikkhūsu appasādabahulau samānau añ‍ñai bhikkhū na bhajati| añ‍ñai bhikkhū abhajantau saddhammaṃ na suṇāti| saddhammaṃ asuṇantau saddhammā parihāyati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau ādīnavau puggalappasādai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ puggalai puggalau abhippasan‍nau hauti, sau disāpak‍kantau hauti…pai॰… sau vibbhantau hauti…pai॰… sau  kālaṅkatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau piyau manāpau sau kālaṅkatau’ti| añ‍ñai bhikkhū na bhajati| añ‍ñai bhikkhū  abhajantau saddhammaṃ na suṇāti| saddhammaṃ asuṇantau saddhammā parihāyati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau ādīnavau puggalappasādai| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā puggalappasādai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

duc‍caritavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

duc‍caritaṃ  kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ|

catūhi parai dvai sivathikā, puggalappasādaina cāti||

pañ‍camapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



(26) 6. upasampadāvaggau

1. upasampādaitabbasuttaṃ

251. mahāva॰ 84 ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaikhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asaikhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asaikhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asaikhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asaikhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nissayasuttaṃ

252. mahāva॰ 84 ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nissayau dātabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaikhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰… asaikhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi…pai॰… nissayau dātabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sāmaṇairasuttaṃ

253. mahāva॰ 84 ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asaikhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; asaikhaina samādhikkhandhaina… asaikhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina… asaikhaina vimuttikkhandhaina… asaikhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pañ‍camacchariyasuttaṃ

254. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, macchariyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca macchariyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ macchariyānaṃ aitaṃ paṭikuṭṭhaṃ patikiṭṭhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), paṭik‍kiṭṭhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), paṭikiṭṭhaṃ (ka॰), yadidaṃ dhammamacchariya’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. macchariyappahānasuttaṃ

255. ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, macchariyānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? āvāsamacchariyassa pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati; kulamacchariyassa…pai॰… lābhamacchariyassa… vaṇṇamacchariyassa… dhammamacchariyassa pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ macchariyānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamajhānasuttaṃ

256. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitu’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7-13. dutiyajhānasuttādisattakaṃ

257-263. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… abhabbau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… abhabbau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… abhabbau sautāpattiphalaṃ… abhabbau sakadāgāmiphalaṃ… abhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ… abhabbau arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (sī॰) sacchikātuṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… bhabbau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… bhabbau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… bhabbau sautāpattiphalaṃ… bhabbau sakadāgāmiphalaṃ… bhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ… bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| tairasamaṃ|

14. aparapaṭhamajhānasuttaṃ

264. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, akatañ‍ñutaṃ akatavaiditaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, akatañ‍ñutaṃ akatavaiditaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitu’’nti| cuddasamaṃ|

15-21. aparadutiyajhānasuttādisattakaṃ

265-271. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… sautāpattiphalaṃ… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ… anāgāmiphalaṃ… arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, akatañ‍ñutaṃ akatavaiditaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… sautāpattiphalaṃ… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ… anāgāmiphalaṃ… arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai  pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, akatañ‍ñutaṃ akatavaiditaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| aikavīsatimaṃ|

upasampadāvaggau chaṭṭhau|



1. sammutipaiyyālaṃ

1. bhattuddaisakasuttaṃ

272. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau na samman‍nitabbau na sammanitabbau (ka॰) cūḷava॰ 326 passitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ  gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau na samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau samman‍nitabbauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau sammatau sammataupi (sī॰) na paisaitabbau…pai॰… sammatau paisaitabbau… bālau vaiditabbau… paṇḍitau vaiditabbau… khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati… akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati… yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai… yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhaṃ  jānāti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-14. saināsanapañ‍ñāpakasuttāditairasakaṃ

273-285. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattaṃ na jānāti…pai॰… saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattaṃ jānāti…pai॰…|

saināsanagāhāpakau  na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) na jānāti…pai॰… saināsanagāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) jānāti…pai॰…|

bhaṇḍāgārikau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… guttāguttaṃ na jānāti… bhaṇḍāgārikau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… guttāguttaṃ jānāti…pai॰…|

cīvarapaṭiggāhakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ na jānāti… cīvarapaṭiggāhakau samman‍nitabbau  …pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ jānāti…pai॰…|

cīvarabhājakau  na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… bhājitābhājitaṃ na jānāti… cīvarabhājakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… bhājitābhājitaṃ jānāti…pai॰…|

yāgubhājakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… yāgubhājakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰…|

phalabhājakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… phalabhājakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰…|

khaj‍jakabhājakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… bhājitābhājitaṃ na jānāti… khaj‍jakabhājakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… bhājitābhājitaṃ jānāti…pai॰…|

appamattakavissaj‍jakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… vissaj‍jitāvissaj‍jitaṃ na jānāti… appamattakavissaj‍jakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… vissaj‍jitāvissaj‍jitaṃ jānāti…|

sāṭiyaggāhāpakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ na jānāti … sāṭiyaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ jānāti…|

pattaggāhāpakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ na jānāti… pattaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… gahitāgahitaṃ jānāti…|

ārāmikapaisakau  na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… ārāmikapaisakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰…|

sāmaṇairapaisakau na samman‍nitabbau…pai॰… sāmaṇairapaisakau samman‍nitabbau…pai॰…|

sammatau na paisaitabbau…pai॰… sammatau paisaitabbau…pai॰…|

sāmaṇairapaisakau bālau vaiditabbau…pai॰… paṇḍitau vaiditabbau… khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati… akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati… yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai… yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, paisitāpaisitaṃ jānāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

sammutipaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



2. sikkhāpadapaiyyālaṃ

1. bhikkhusuttaṃ

286. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, abrahmacārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, abrahmacariyā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-7. bhikkhunīsuttādichak‍kaṃ

287-292. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā bhikkhunī…pai॰… sikkhamānā… sāmaṇairau… sāmaṇairī… upāsakau… upāsikā yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātinī hauti, adin‍nādāyinī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārinī hauti, musāvādinī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyinī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā upāsikā yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā upāsikā yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratā  hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā upāsikā yathābhataṃ nikkhittā aivaṃ saggai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ājīvakasuttaṃ

293. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ājīvakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, abrahmacārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  ājīvakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9-17. nigaṇṭhasuttādinavakaṃ

294-302. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau nigaṇṭhau…pai॰… muṇḍasāvakau… jaṭilakau… paribbājakau… māgaṇḍikau… taidaṇḍikau… āruddhakau… gautamakau… daivadhammikau  yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau daivadhammikau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti| sattarasamaṃ|

sikkhāpadapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



3. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

303. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

304. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

305. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

306. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

307. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

308-1151. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā| dausassa… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa  … madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – pamādassa, bhikkhavai, paṭinissaggāya imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya,

pahānāya khayāya vayaina ca|

virāganiraudhā cāgañ‍ca,

paṭinissaggau imai dasāti||

pañ‍cakanipātau niṭṭhitau|

tatridaṃ vagguddānaṃ –

saikhabalaṃ  balañ‍caiva, pañ‍caṅgikañ‍ca sumanaṃ|

muṇḍanīvaraṇañ‍ca sañ‍ñañ‍ca, yaudhājīvañ‍ca aṭṭhamaṃ|

thairaṃ kakudhaphāsuñ‍ca, andhakavindadvādasaṃ|

gilānarājatikaṇḍaṃ, saddhammāghātupāsakaṃ|

arañ‍ñabrāhmaṇañ‍caiva, kimilak‍kausakaṃ tathā|

dīghācārāvāsikañ‍ca, duc‍caritūpasampadanti||

pañ‍cakanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
